Page 9

 Girgizashi yai yace "Umar ka dawo da hankalinka nan, kai baka tambayi yarinyar da aka ce an baka bane? Koni magana ta gaskiya kunnena abinda yajimin kenan auran Umar da Bilkisu."
 Fizge jikinshi yai yana huci ya nunashi da yatsa yace "Yaya Naziru karka batamin rai wallahi in raina ya baci ba abinda bazan iya aikatawa ba, uban waye zai sani auran wata a duniya inba Ibtisam ba Yarinyarnan mata tace tun farko kuma wlh duk wanda ya nemi shiga tsakanina da ita zan iya mai ila."
 Shi kanshi Naziru ya tsorata da yanda Umar ke magana, a tsorace ya sakeshi yana kallan yanda yake wani huci, Umar ya kalleshi yace "Bari naje gun Abba ko shi limamin sai ya sanar dani meke faruwa."
 Naziru kam ya fahimci lalai akwai matsala da sauri ya juya ya nufi inda Kawunsa yake cikin mutane, shikanshi Kawun jikinsa duk yai sanyi kawai dai yana yake ne cikin mutane amma yasan tabbas akwai matsala. Yana hango Naziru ya nufoshi da sauri yace "Ina Umar?"
Naziru jiki a sanyaye yace "Ya tafi neman Abba ko Liman."
 Shafar fuskarsa Kawun yai yace "kafin yaran nan ya sa mutane sumana kallan mahaukatan kauye kajashi ya bat gun nan."
 Cikin damuwa Naziru yace "Matsalar yaki tsayawa ya saurareni, bansan ya zanyi ba Kawu."
 Da sauri Kawun ya shiga neman Umar a cikin mutanen, masallacin ya nufa dan yafi kokontan can zaije, yana shiga kuwa ya hangoshi a tsaye da alama mutanen dake durkushe kusa da limamin, da sauri Kawu yasa hannu ya rikoshi suka fito.
 Umar gaba daya ya firgice yace "Kawu tambayar liman nake san yi me yasa yace ya bada auran Bilkisu gareni? In ma kuskure yai ya dawo ya kira mutane a sake daura aure."
 Kawun ya jashi can gefe yace "Umar bansan meya faru ba tsakaninka da uban rikonka, sai dai tabbas shi Bilkisu ya baka, sai dai in kaine baka tsaya ka fahimceshi ba, bayan an daura aure dakanshi a masallaci yace "Yaya nikam Umar ya gamamin komai tunda ya amince da auran Bilkisu."
Rai a bace ya nuna kansa yace "Ni? Wlh cutata akai ni ban taba kawo auran nakashashiya ba, Kawu Allah cutata yai, bakuma zan yarda ba, duk bala'in da za'ai sai dai ayi amma ba wanda ya isa ya rabani da Ibtisam."
 Baiko tsaya jin me Kawu zaice ba ya wuce ya fara tafiya ransa na wani irin kuna, motar da su Aminu ya kalla wacce yaganshi dazu ya iso a ciki, a kufule ya dau dutse ya kalli gefe da gefe ganin gun ba mutane kawai ya buga gaban motar iya karfinsa sannan ya wuce. Karar glass din motar da mutane sukaji ne yasa suka juya, shikam adaidaita ya tara kawai ya shiga.
 Ganibyai mai adaidaitar baya sauri cikin bacin raj yace "Kai Dalla malam kajani da sauri wannan tafiyar kamar wani dan koyo? In kasan baka iya ba uban wa yace ka fara sana'ar?"
 Wannan magana ta batawa mai adaidaita rai gashi shima dama ba hakuri gareshi ba, parking yai a gefen titi yace "Kai bansan hauka da iskancin, tashi sama kake so nai ko me? In shaye shaye kai kaje can ka sau........" Ai Umar ya gama kaiwa wuya cakumo kwalarsa yai ya daga hannu yana neman kaimai duka, shima mutumin ya cakumo wuyan Umar, ganin baisan bata lokaci yasa ya sakeshi rai a bace ya fito ya tari wata, gaba daya huci kawai yakeyi.

********
 Gida kam ya cika sosai da mutane anata hidimar yin abinci an sauke wasu ana daura wasu, a daki kam Ibty tana zaune ita da kawayenta 'yan uwa sukaji ana guda ana cewa an daura, sanye take da wani hadaden lace wanda Abba ya dinka musu ita da Yaya Bilkisu iri daya, sunyi kyau iya kyau ga dinkin ya karbesu, Iman dama ta iya makeup ita ta zane musu fuskarsu, sunyi kyau sosai abinsu.
Bilkisu tana kasa cikin mutane su kuma suna sama anata hira, itakam sam batajin dadi haka kawai takejin jikinta kamar mai zazzabi, Ainah ce ta kirata dagawa tai tare da dan matsawa gefe tace "Ainah!"
Ai Ainah bata amsa ba tace "Ibty yau naga abinda ya hanani sukuni, wai yau ake daura auran yayana amma ba wanda ya fadamin?"
 Dan idanta Ibty ta runtse tace "Ainah maganar gaskiya......."
 "Ibty kinsan abin dariyar? Sunanku daya da amaryar."
 Yawun bakinta ta hadiya tace "Ainah wai nice amaryar."
 Ainah bata fahimci me take nufi ba tace "Kece me?"
 Kiran da aka kwalla ma Ibtisam ne yasa tace "Zan kiraki anjima" bata jira me zatace ba ta kashe wayar, fitowa tai daga dakin, kanwar Umma ce ta kalleta tace "ki sauko mai hoto yazo."
 Kai ta daga sannan ta koma ta dauko mayafin kayan sannan ta sauko, sun tsaya kenan ana shi shiryawa za'a dau hoto tundaga wajen harabar gidan sukaji ana kwallawa Ibtisam kira cikin kakkausar murya, gabanta ne ya yanke yai wani irin faduwa balle dataji muryar Umar, me yake yi hakan? Ta dauka dai yanzu komai ya wuce ko?
 Kafafunta ne suka dan fara rawa jin yanda yake kiranta kai tsaye kuma kana ji kasan muryar bacin rai ce.
 Gaba daya mutanen falan mamaki ne ya kamasu, Umma ta mike cikin mamaki tace "Umar lafiya kuwa?"
 Suna kokarin fitowa ya shigo cikin falan, ya cire babbar rigar ya rike a hannunsa, bai damu da taron mutanen dake falan ba ciki kuwa harda mahaifiyarsa, idanunsa kawai ya sauke kan Ibtisam yace "Ibty meke faruwa?"
 Kallansa tai gaba daya ta kasa magana sai rawa da jikinta kawai yakeyi, takowa yai yazo inda take yanayinsa ya canza sosai cikin tsawa yace "Meke faruwa nace?"
 Baya tadanyi gaba daya jikinta ya kasa daina rawa, cikin fada mahaifiyarsa tace "Umar me kakeyi hakan? Bakaganin manya ne? Ko sallama da gaisuwa bakai ba kazo ka tsareta kamar 'yar cikinka."
 Kallan Goggon yai ji yake kamar yai ihu, wani mugun kallo ya zabgawa Bilkisu sannan ya kalli Ibtisam yace "Wuce muje ki fadawa Abba dani za'a daura miki aure kafin ranki ya baci a cikin gidan nan."
 Jin wannan kalmar yasa gaba daya matan dake gun suka sauke salati, Umma cikin tsananin mamaki tace "Mene? Umar me kake cewa?"
 Ibty kam zubewa tai a kasa kawai jikinta na rawa, ta kasa ko magana balle hawaye.
 Umar ya kalli Umma yace "Ibtisam nake so kuma ita tafi kowa sanin tsawon lokacin dana dauka ina santa akan me za'a dauramin aure da Bilkisu?"
 Nan fa falo ya kara rincabewa gaba daya kowa yahau salati ana kallan ikon Allah, yau meke faruwa a gidan nan? Umma kam ta kasa ko magana sai kallan Umar da takeyi.
 Shikam ko a jikinsa ya kalli Ibtisam yace "Bakiji nace ki tashi ba?" Yai maganar yana kokarin fizgota, cikin tsawa mahaifiyarsa tace "Kaci gidanku Umar, mu zakayiwa diban albarka? Ka fita daga nan ko sai na sharara maka mari? Hankaline bakada shi ko tunani ne bakadashi?"
 Kallan Goggo yai kawai ya dunkule hannu, ya fara huci yana kallan Ibtisam.
 Goggo a fusace tace "Bakaji nace ka fita ba?"
 Juyawa yai kamar zan sa ihu ya fita daga falan, a kofar falan yaja ya tsaya fuskarsa kamar wanda aka aikowa da sakon mutuwa.
 Kanwar Abba cikin hanzari ta dau waya ta kirashi, ta sanar dashi yazo gida yanzun nan dan ba lafiya.
 Bilkisu kam kawai hawaye ta shiga yi, da sauri taja kekenta ta koma daki, sauka tai ta dan dafa kago ta zauna sannan taja bargo ta lulube kanta, a hankali wani wahalalen kuka ya kwace mata. Me ke faruwa? Taya ango zaizo yace ba amarya yakeso ba kanwarta yakeso? Bayan an daura musu aure? Wannan wani irin bala'i ne?

*******
Umma a fusace ta dago Ibtisam ta girgizata iya karfinta tace "kin dawo hayyacinki ko sai na miki tsinanen duka?"
 Idanunta ne kawai suka fara cicikowa, haryanzu jikinta bai dawo da karkarwa ba. Umma ta kalleta tace "Meke faruwa? Umar ke yake so ko me?"
 Bakinta ne ya hau rawa ta rasa ta ina zata fara magana, wani wawan mari taji an wanketa dashi, ta rike kuncinsa anan fa hawaye suka gangaro mata, bakinta na rawa tace "Umma nasan yana sona amma na dauka ya hakura dani ne zai auri Yaya tunda Abba ne yamai magana."
 Saketa Umma tai iya karfinta hakan yasa Ibty ta dan fadi kasa, jiki a sanyaye Umma ta zauna akan kujera dabas, nan fa aka fara kananan maganganu, dayawa suka hau Ibty da masifa ai laifinta ne, hawaye kawai Ibtisam takeyibta dukunkune.
 Abba ne ya shigo shi dasu Kawu, tundaga hanyar shiga sukaga Umar, yana tsaye fuskarsa haryanzu ba alamar saukowa.
 Kawu kam fuskarsa kawai ya shafa dan yasan kwanan zancen.
 Abba ya kalli Umar yace "Umar kai kuma nan ka taho anata nemanka a dau hotuna?"
 Mamaki ne ya kama Abba ganin ko amsamai baiyi ba, daga ciki kanwarsa ta fito da sauri cikin ahin gulma tace " Yaya shigo shigo."
 Shiga yai ganin yanda kowa yai cirko cirko yasa yaji gabanshi yadan fadi, dama gashi ya baro can da takaicin fasa motar Aminu da akai.
 Zama yai yace "Meke faruwa?"
Nan ta zayyanemai abinda ya faru, tashin hankalin da ba'a misaltawa, Inalilahi wa ina ilaihi raji'un abinda Abba kawai yake cewa kenan.
 Kallan Umar yai yamai alama ya shigo.
 Umar ya shigo ya zauna daga gefe, Abba yace "Umar meyasa baka fadamin Ibtisam kake so ba?"
Umar ya kalleshi yace "Ni dakace 'yarka ita kadai na kawo shiyasa banyi tunanin wani abu ba na amince."
 Kawu ya kalleshi yace "Wannan laifinka ne ko ma menene ya kamata ka tsaya kaji sunan yarinyar kafin ka amince."
 Umar ya kalli Kawu yace "Ni dai yanzu koma menene bai dameni ba Kawu, Ibtisam matatace dole wanda ya aureta ya bani takardarta na aureta, ni ma zan saki Bilkisu dama ban dauketa a matsa............"
 Bugun dayaji a kafadarsa baisa ya juya ba, Goggo cikin kunar rai tace "Umar bakada hankali ne? Mutumin daya rikeka ya maka gata kake fadawa zaka saki 'yarsa sannan yasa a saki daya 'yar tashi ya baka?"
 Umar ya kalli Ibtisam yace "Ko ma menene sai dai ku fada amma Allah ba wanda ya isa yai zaman aure da ita inba ni ba."
 Yanzu kam Abba ya tabbatar Umar baya tunani, ya kuma tabbatar inya bar Ibtisam yaran nan zai iya ja musu bala'i.
 Dakewa Abba yai yace "Koma menene ya riga ya faru, in har bakasan Bilkisu wannan daban sai dai auran Ibtisam da Aminu baka isa ka kashe shi ba, da ba yanzu zata tare ba amma yanzu bazan barta ko kwana daya ta kara a gidan nan ba, gidan mijinta zata tafi, aure shiri ne na Allah, da ace Aminu ba mijinta bane da auran nan bazai taba yiwu ba."
 Umar ya kalli Abba yace "Allah Abba bamai fitar da ita ya kaita gidan wani in ba ni ba."
 Baki Abba ya bude yace "Umar? Ni kake fadawa haka?"
Ran Kawu yai matukar baci yace "Tashi ka fita daga nan ka koma bangarenka."
 Umar ya kalli Kawu yanaji kamar zaiyi hauka yace "Inhar tabar gidan nan duk abinda ya biyo baya kar wanda yaga laifina."
 Yana kaiwa nan ya mike ya fita, kanwar Abba tace "Anya Umar baya shaye shaye? Na dauka baya magana sosai yau me idona yake gani?"
 Abba kam salati kawai yakeyi tashin hankalin da ya tsinci kansa a ciki ko sanda akace Bilkisu tanada sikila bai ji ba.
 Ibtisam kam kuma take iya ranta, Umma bata ko bi ta kanta ba ta wuce daki gun Bilkisu, Abba ne ya kalleta yace "jeki hada kayanki da kaina zan kaiki gidansu Aminu, ki zauna a gidan mijinki gobe duk yanda za'ai za'azo a miki jere."
 Kasa tashi tai, Iman tazo ta dagata tanata kuka haka suka hau sama.
 Umar kam a daki kayansa shima ya hau hadawa zuwa zaiyi ya dau Ibtisam su wuce kauye bazai iya wannan rainin hankalin ba.

Nace ikon Allah sai kallo🤔 wayaga karfin hali barawo da sallama?

Page 10😘


  Iman ta kama hannun Ibtisam wacce ta mike tana kuka, sama suka wuce nan Iman ta shiga hada mata kayanta, meyasa kowa ke neman daurawa Ibtisam laifi? Bayan kana gani kasan sanda Umar ke mata bana hankali bane? Da alama itama neman kwatar kanta takeyi. Tana hada mata kaya itama hawaye na zubo mata dan yanda Ibtisam ke kuka duk mai tausayi sai ya tausaya mata.
 Abba ne ya turo akan Ibtisam tai sauri ta fito, haka suka fito da ita tana tafe tana kuka, dakin Bilkisu ta shiga, Umma ta gani rungume da Bilkisu tana kuka, karasawa tai ta zube a kasa jikin gadan da suke tana kuka tana kiran sunan Umma.
 Fuskar Umma a daure sosai ta kalleta tace "Ke kuma kukan me kike? Naji ita tanada hujjar kuka ke fa?"
 Kai Ibtisam ta girgiza tace "Umma wlh ba'a san raina bane naki fada muku, har daki na biyoki ina san na sanar dake, sannan lokacin da kuka fara maganar hadin nan kai tsaye na tambayeku dan ni nasan akwai wani abin, amma alokacin kukace inata tambaya ai shi ya fara amincewa, shiyasa na dauka ya hakura dani ne, ita kanta Yaya har daki na biyota na tambayeta ta nunamin ita ke san Ya Umar? Umma ya kukeso nayi? Menene laifina????" Abinda ta fada kenan ta sa wani irin kuka, duk da jikin Umma yayi sanyi ta kuma tuno tabbas duk abinnan data fada ya faru amma haryanzu bata sauko daga bacin kan laifin Ibtisam bane.
 Kanta ta kauda wanda hakan ya kara daga hankalin Ibtisam, kanwar Abba ce ta shigo ta fito da ita tana kuka tana ba Umma hakuri tasata a motar Abba.

 ******
 Umar kam a daki ya gama hada kayansa tsaf, cikin bacin rai ya kalli hoton frame din Abba dake makale a dakin wanda suka dauka tare, a kufule ya dau hoton ya makashi da kasa iya karfinsa sannan ya dauko jakarsa yana neman fitowa.
 Da sauri Naziru ya maida kofar ya danne iya karfinsa daga waje.
 A fusace Umar yace "Naziru kasan Allah in har baka bude kofar nan a duk abinda ya biyo baya karkaga laifina? Wlh zan iya tada gidannan da abinda ke cikinsa."
 Naziru ya kara danne kofar iya karfinsa yace "Da alama ka fara haukacewa, ka kuma nunawa duniya kamar yanda aka daukemu 'yan kauye duk zaman birnin da zamuyi bazamu kile ba, banda rashin d'a da tunani mutumin daya rikeka kake neman ruguza rayuwar 'ya'yansa?"
 Cikin tsawa yace "In ya rikeni shine ya halliceni? Ko kuwa ni daya nunamin rashin d'a'a anga laifinsa? Ko kuwa ni ba mutum bane?"
 Nan fa kokawa ta shiga dan Umar iya karfinsa yake sakawa, daga waje shima Naziru iya nasa karfin yake sakawa, cikin tashin sa'a Naziru yai baya hakan yasa Umar fitowa cikin tsananin karfi.
 Wani mugun kallao ya zabgamai sannan ya nunashi da yatsa yace "Inhar kai dan uwanane na jini to ka tabbatar ka kyaleni dan wlh duk mai kokarin rabani da Ibtisam to yana daga cikin makiyana." Yai magana yana rataya jakarsa, mamaki ne ya kamashi dan yana fitowa harabar yaga motar Abba na fita, ai kuwa idanunsa suka leko mai Ibtisam a zaune a bayan mota, sakar jakar yai ya biyo motar da gudu iya karfinsa, Kawunsa da ke kokarin maida gate ne ya bugamai wani kallo yace "Wallahi in har ka kuskura ka fita daga kofar gidan nan to ka shirya fuskantar bacin raina, akan wannan haukar taka zan iya sallamaka."
 Turus yaja ya tsaya idanunsa suka canza launi, ya kalli Kawu yace "Kawu inhar su sun manta da farinciki na kai a matsayinka na mahaifi bai kamata ka bi bayana ba?"
 Banzan kallo Kawu yamai yace "Inbi bayanka akan hauka? So hauka ne akace maka? Ko kuwa akanka aka fara so? Ko kuma akanka aka fara rasa abinda mutum ke so? Banda hauka da rashin hankali ka kalli mahaifin daya rikeka kace wai zaka saki 'yarsa yasa shima a saki daya 'yar kuma wai ya baka? Tunda nake a rayuwata ban taba ji bayani wanda yake tattare da rashin tunani da hauka ba irin wannan, ko mu dake kauye bamu shigo birni ba munsan wannan hauka da rashin tunani ne."
 Huci kawai Umar yakeyi ya dunkule hannunsa yanaji kamar ya naushi gate din gidan, tabbas wlh sai dai suyi abinda zasuyi amma Ibtisam bazai taba barinta ya kwana da wani kato ba inba shiba.
 Kokarin fita ya farayi, Kawu ya matso ya wanka mai mari iya karfinsa sannan yace "Wallahi in baka wuce daga nan ba sai jikinka ya fadama."
 Babu alamar wucewa a tattare dashi sai dai ya daina kokarin fita, ko gizau baiyi ba da marin dan yana ganin halin da zuciyarsa ke ciki ya ninka wannan marin, a ransa yace "Ai ko tsakar dare ne zan je na dauko matata dan wlh in har wannan sokon namijin yasa hannu ya taba Ibty to lalai za'ai kisan kai a daren yau." A fusace ya juya bangarensa.


*******
Abba kam yana fita cikin karfi yaja motar dan ya hango sanda Umar ya fara tahowa, tafiya sosai yai sannan yaja gefen titi ya tsaya, hannunsa biyu yasa ya shafi fuskarsa wannan wani irin bala'i ne?
 Kukan Ibtisam ne yasa ya dan sauke ajiyar zuciya yace "Ibtisam meyasa baki sanar dani halin da ake ciki ba? Da alama wannan halin na Umar ba yau ya fara shi ba."
 Dagowa tai tana kuka tace "Abba bansan ya zanyi nasanar dakai ba, yaran nan dan yayanka ne wanda duk duniya ku biyu kuka rage, ka yadda da Ya Umar sama damu, Abba ban isa ko shiga cikin kawaye nai ba, sallama wannan ban isa na amsawa namiji ba, Abba tunda na fara makaranta shi ke kaini shike zuwa daukana wai duk dan karna kula kowa." Ta share hawayenta tace "ko adaidaita na shiga ban isa nai ko murmushi ba sai yace mai adaidaitan nake wa murmushi, haka rayuwata take cikin ukuba saboda shi, jin ya amince zai auri Yaya wlh na dauka hakura yai dani yana ganin ai Yaya batada lafiya tana zaman gida, duk da haka naje na sanar da Umma ta katseni, Abba bansan ya zanyi da rayuwata ba." Kuka ne yaci karfinta, gaba daya tausayinta ya kamashi. Fita yai daga cikin motar, ya kira Baffa.
 Suna zaune suna cin abinci shida Aminu a gidan Ummy, dan yau ranar farinciki ce a garesu matuka, Kanin Aminu ma daga Singapo yace gobe zai taho.
 Wayar Baffa ce tai kara, ya ajiye cokalin hannunsa sannan yazarota daga cikin aljihunsa. Ganin Abba ne yasa ya kara yana cewa "Sirikina baka gaji da ganina bane?"
 Abba yai dan yake yace "Sulaiman ina cikin wani hali ne, wanda nake neman alfarma a gunka."
 Jin haka yasa Baffa yadan mike ya maida hankali sosai kan wayar yace "Meke faruwa?"
 Abba yai dan murmushin takaici yace "Ya Aminu? Ya akaji da asarar da aka mai dazu?" 
Baffa yace "Haba bakomai, tsautsayi ne ai da alama yara ne basu kula ba suka buga mai." A ran Abba yace "Yanzu kam inada yakinin Umar ne."
 A fili ya daure yace "So nake Ibtisam ta tare gidan mijinta, yau ta kwana a haka gobe insha Allahu za'a zo ai jere duk inda jibi take an kammala, wani uzuri ne babba ya taso wanda dole ta kwana gidan mijinta ayau din nan."
 Sosai wannan kalamai na Abba sunbashi mamaki sai dai tunda yaji bai fadamai dalili kai tsaye ba ya tabbatar akwai wani babban dalilin daya hanashi.
 Kallan Amini yai wanda yake cin abincinsa hankali a kwance, gashi basu gama katange Hajiyarcan ba, menene mafita?
 Daurewa yai yace "Shikenan yanzu ka kawota gida na, in ma tafiyar ne sai su wuce ita da mijinta, ai dama a musulunce ba ace sai anyi gayya gun kai amarya ba."
 Ajiyar zuciya Abba yai yace "Nagode sosai! Insha Allahu zan iso yanzun nan."
 Wayar ya kashe Baffa ya fito daga falo ya nufi dakinsa sannan ya kira Ummy a waya. Bata jima ba ta shigo cikin fara'a tace "Alhaji! Ina cikin bakin ma baza'a barni ba?"
 Zama yai a bakin gado sannan ya mata nuni data zauna kusa dashi. Kallansa take harta zauna kafin ya kwashe abinda ke faruwa ya sanar da ita.
 Tayi mamaki kwarai kafin tace "in har hakane ina ganin Aminu ya dau matarsa su kwana a hotel, suje can suyi kwana uku har a kammala yi mata jeren, kafin nan an gama raba gidan."
 "Hotel kuma? Da haka a kawota nan gidan mana."
 Ummy ta girgiza kai tace "Dama inata tunanin yanda za'ai a basu dama su fahimci junansu, komai anyishi cikin sauri, Aminu nada yara, duk da agun Hajiya suke kwana amma agunshi suke, daga auranta za'a sata kula da yara? Ya za'ai su samu damar zama su biyu? In kawosu guna suyi sati ne? Duk wadannan tunanin sun cabemin wanda jin hakan yamin daidai, nan gidan ma in an kawota taya zasu zauna su biyu?" Tadan sauke ajiyar zuciya tace "Ga mutane a gidan nan me kake tunanin za'ace in aka ganta?"
 Baffa yai shiru alamar tunani, tabbas yanzu ya gane me take nufi.
 Sansanyan murmushi yai yace "Asma'u shiyasa a koda yaushe nake alfahari dake, na kuma tabbatar barin Hajiya ba laifinki bane, kinyi iya yinki fin karfinmu tai, wanda na tabbatar Allah yana tare damu."
Falo Baffa ya dawo ya sanar da Aminu wannan hukuncin, mamaki ne matuka ya bayyana a fuskarshi yace "Dama can abinda Ummy ta tsara kenan ko?"
 Ummy ta hade fuska tace "Dama can haka nai niyya na tabbatar in na sanar dakai ne bazaka yarda ba, shiyasa nace a bari sai bayan an daura muku aure."
 Shiru yai shifa bawai auran ba wannan takurar da za'a dinga bulomai dasu ya tsana, yanzu fisabillilahi nan akacemai ba yanzu zata tare ba, yanzu kuma anzomai da maganar wani hotel? Kamar wanda bashida gida?
 Ummy ce ta katseshi tace "Ka tashi ka dau motar Baffanka yanzu za'a kawota ka amsheta ku wuce abinku, in kaje gida dauko kayanka basai ta shiga ciki ba kaje kai kadai ka kwaso ku wuce."
 Mamakin yanda Ummy ta daure fuska sosai tana jawabi yake, lalai wannan shine a dakeka a hanaka kuka.
 Haka ya fito duk ransa a bace, motar Baffa ya dauka,  daga ciki Baffa yamai waya yace "yaje waje sun iso."
 Haka ya wuce ya gaida Abba sannan ta fito ta shiga mota, kayanta tasa a baya ta zauna a gaba. Itakam ko a novel bata taba karanta amarya mara gata irinta ba, ace an kawota babu wani farinciki, uwarta ko kallan arziki bata mata ba.
 Kanta kawai tasa akan cinyoyinta tana kuka, tana a haka Aminu ya shigo.
 Idanunsa yadan runtse dan ya tsani kuka sam, dakewa yai yaja mota suka nufi gidansa, a waje yai parking ya shiga ya dauko jaka ya zuba kaya duk ransa a bace, wannan wani irin lamari ne? Ko auren soyayya sukai ai gida ya kamata a kawota amma wai wani hotel kamar a film sun tafi honeymoon?
 Cikin bacin rai ya gama hada kaya ya fito yaja mota.
A Bristol Palace yai parking sannan ya kalleta, haryanzu kuka take, ya daure yace "Zanshiga nai check in."
Bata amsa ba shima bai jira amsarta ba, sai daya gama komai sannan ya dawo ya kalleta yace "Muje."
 Dagowa tai duk fuskarta hawaye, gabanta ne ya fadi ganinsu a hotel, me zasuyi anan? Kallansa tai tana neman magana sai dai jitai batada bakin magana.
 Aminu ya dau tissue ya mika mata yace "Goge fuskarki kafin mutane su dauka satoki nai."
Ansa tai ta goge fuskarta sannan ta fito sam ta manta da jakarta.
 Aminu ya kalli jakar lalai wannan yarinyar, badai da saninta tabarmai jakar ya daukar mata ba? 
 Kallanta yai kamar zai mata magana sai kuma ya fasa, dauka yai sannan yai gaba, wuceta yai ta bi bayansa.
 Babban daki ya kama musu, ta zauna akan kujera, ko sallah batai ba, mikewa tai ta kalli falan ba kowa sai ita, nufar daki tai da niyar tai alwala shi kuma daidainan ya cire manyan kayansa yana sanye da vest da wandan shaddar, kokarin yin wanka da alwala yakeyi.
 Idanu ta zaro da sauri ta koma falo, bakijta nadan rawa tace "Yahkuri."
 Aminu kam da sauri ya maida kayansa sannan ya zauna akan gado cikin dakewa yace "Shigo ko ba toilet zaki ba?"
 Shiga tai kanta a kasa wai dan karta kalleshi, tana kokarin kuma kanta da bango taji yace "Me kikeyi hakan?"
Tsayawa tai sannan ta kalleshi da sauri, ganin saura kiris ta make kanta yasa ta sake kallansa tace "Yahkuri na dauka baka gama shiryawa bane."
 Tai maganar tare da wucewa toilet da sauri, alwala tai sannan ta fito ta koma falo tai sallah, ya dade akan sallaya tana kuka tana kaiwa Allah kukanta, shikam Aminu duk kunnensa ya gaji dajin wannan kukan, wai haka amaren yanzu suke? Shi ko dan suna kaunar junansu da Na'ima ne? Dan kuka kadan tai, wannan anya ko abinci ma taci? 
 Yadan kauda kai ai wannan ra’ayinta ne. Harya gama abinda yake ya kwanta bata shigo ba, yanzu ya daina jin sheshekar kukanta.
 Ibty kam tun tana kuka har bacci ya dauketa, dan ta rasa yanda zatai gashi Abba ya kwace wayarta yace “Ko an sai mata waya karta kira kowa saishi, itakam tanasan tasan halinda su Umma kw ciki. A haka bacci ya dauketa a durkushe.
 Tun Aminu na lissafi hardai ya kasa kwantar da hankali ya mike, falon ya fito a zaune take inda tai salla ta hada kai da gwiwa, kallanta yai sannan a fili yace “Ba dole bacci ya daukeki ba, kinata kuka kamar ba gobe, kinfi awa hudu kina abu daya.
 Kamar zai juya sai kuma tausayin kwanciyar datai yadan kamashi, ko ya sata a daki shi ya kwanta anan?
 Hannu yasa yadan tabata yace “Tashi ki koma daki.�?
 Sam bataji ba saboda nannauyan baccin daya tafi da ita, dan tabata ya sake yi da dan karfi, ita kuma daidainan tayi mafarkin Umar na janta a daji tana turjewa.
 Cikin firgici ta rike hannun Aminu iya karfinta tana wani irin kuka, tana cewa “Kayi hakuri dan Allah! Na tuba!�?
 Yanda ta matsemai hannu da kalamanta ne yasa yadan sunkuyo hannu yasa yadan daga kanta yace “Tashi!�?
 Bude ido tai gabanta na faduwa, ta zubasu akanshi, wasu zafafan hawaye ne suka zubo mata, mamaki ya kamashi yace “Sakemin hannu ko?�?
 Kallan hannun tai sannan ta sakeshi da sauri, ya dan gyara zamanta, mikewa yai cikin dakewa yace “Ki wuce ciki ki kwanta, in kuma kinajin yunwa a kawo miki abinci.�?
 Kai kawai ta girgiza sannan ta mike a hankali tace “Nagode Ya Aminu, banajin yunwa.�?
 Abinda tace kenan ta wuce ciki, kafada yadan daga kadan sannan ya zauna ya jawo wayars ayana dube dube, kallan dakin yai yace “Tunda batajin yunwa ai tasan cikinta.�?

Umar kam ya gama saitawa ta yanda zai je gida ya sato Ibtisam, ya kuma samu abokansa biyu wadanda zasu taimaka mai, sai dai abin tambayar “Inane gidan?�? Taya zaisan gidan? Wannan abu yasa sun rasa yanda zasuyi shi kuma yasha alwashin bazai kwana ba sai da Ibty…�?.

      (.........In Between)

   *H̝a̝s̝k̝e̝ W̝r̝i̝t̝e̝r̝s̝ A̝s̝s̝o̝c̝i̝a̝t̝i̝o̝n̝*

*Top Ten! Takun Haske Batch A*
*Dan Allah in baki siya ba karki karanta, kudi kalilan zaki biya ki samu wannan garabasar*

*11*

  Duk yanda Aminu yaso yai bacci ya kasa, sai juye yake akan kujera, ajiyar zuciyar da take tayi irin na wacce taci kuka ta gaji shine duk ya hanashi bacci. Kamar ya jawo dakin ya rufe sai dai yana ganin hakan bai kamata ba in wani abin ya sameta cikin dare fa? Ganin dare natayi gashi yanasan zuwa aiki gobe yasa ya mike ya nufi dakin. Tana kwance ta dukunkune ko bargo bata rufa ba, ta dukunkune jikin hijab dinta, shiru yai yana kallanta yanda take bacci tanata sauke ajiyar zuciya. 
A fili yace "In kinsan bakyasan auren meyasa baki bi shawarata ba? Bayan na sanar dake kice bakyaso?" Ganin dai shikadai ke maganarsa ba amsa yasa yai kamar zai juya, haryayi taku biyo sai kuma naga yadawo yaja bargon dake kan gadan ya luluba mata, cikin baccinta taji alamun hannu ana sa mata abu. A hankali ta bude idanunta ta saukesu akanshi.
 Da sauri ya saki bargon tare da mikewa sosai yai gyaran murya, kafin yace "Meye amfanin bargon in kinajin sanyi?"
 Bakinta tadan motsa a hankali kafin tace "Ya Aminu karfe nawa?"
 Agoggon hannunsa ya kalla kafin yace "karfe biyu na dare."
 Wani abu tadan hadiya a bakinta kafin tace "Nasan sun kwanta yanzo, amma Yaya dan Allah bani aran wayarka ko text nayiwa Abba."
 "Yanzun?" Abinda ya tambayeta kenan tadan mike zaune fuskarta da idanunta duk sun kumbura, shi dama haka amaren suke? 
 Wayarsa ya dauko ya bude sannan ya mika mata, kafin ya juya ya fita. Zama yai a falo yana mamakin yanda yanayinta duk ya canza, ji yai tausayinta ya kamashi, shifa abinda bayaso kenan azo ana auren dana sani bayan shi ba shine ya tilastata ba.
 Ibtisam kam daga daki sako ta turawa Abba tana tambayar ko gida kowa lafiya, ganin anfi miti biyar ba reply yasa tasan bacci yake.
 Saukowa tai daga kan gadan a hankali, jitai jiri jiri na yunwa na neman yarda ita, rabanta fa da abinci ashe tun safe ko? Tazo zataci akace tai wanka ta shirya tukunna, bayan ta shirya kuma tayi sallah akace ta fito daukan hoto, daga nan kuma shikenan komai ya rikice, gashi da safen kosai taci kawai tasha dan tea.
 Bango ta dan dafa tare da takawa a hankali zuwa falan, kusa dashi taje yana zaune akan kujera ta mikamai wayar da hannu biyu, kallanta yai bai amsa ba yace "Kingama?" Kai ta daga a hankalin sannan tace "Nagode!" 
 "Wayarki a gida kika barta? Ko kwacewa akai?" Kai tadan girgiza a hankali kafin tace "Mantawa nai da akace na fito bansamu damar daukowa ba." 
 Kai ya daga sannan yasa hannu ya amshi wayar, juyawa tai, tai dan gefe kadan tana neman faduwa, da sauri ta rike kujerar dake gefe, mamaki ne ya kamashi ya mike ya tako zuwa inda take, hannu yasa akan saman goshinta, wanda hakan yasa ta dawo ta kalleshi, samun kanta tai da saukar da idanta da sauri saboda wani kwarjini daya mata.
 Hannunsa ya dauke daga saman goshinta sannan yasa a kan wuyanta, jitai numfashinta na neman daukewa na wucin gadi, ta kasa ko kwakwaran motsi, muryarsa taji yace "Baki da lafiya?"
 Bakinta nadan rawa tace "A'a kawai kaina ke ciwo sai dan jiri da nakeji."
 "Abinci fa? Yaushe rabanki dashi? Tunda naji ba zazzabi bane.�?
 Yanzun ma kanta na kasa a hankali tace "Da safe nasha tea da kosai."
 Wani kallo ya mata na bacin rai da alama yarinta na damun yarinyar nan, tun abincin safe bataci komai ba ta zauna kuma tanata uban kuka? Kamar wacce akayiwa rasuwa?
 Cikin dan zafi yace "Shiga daki ki zauna."
 Bata ko musa ba ta wuce tana tafiya a hankali tana dan bin bango, da kallo ya bita sannan yadan girgiza kai, saman fridge ya kalla kayan tea ne kawai a gun, ya kuna kettle ya dafa ruwan zafi sannan ya hada mata tea, bacci yakeji dan har wata doguwar hamma yai, daukan cup din yai ya nufi daki, harta dan fara gyangyadi, gyaran murya yai yana daga tsaye. Bude idanunta tai tare da gyara zama, mika mata cup din yai yace "Sha kafin ki kwanta"
 Hannu tasa ta amsa, idanunta suka dan ciciko a hankali tace "Nagode." Bai amsa ta ba ya koma falo, kwanciya yai kankace me yanzu kam bacci yayi gaba dashi.
 Ibty kam tana gama sha tai alwala tai saloli wanda haryanzu babu karfi sam a jikinta, da kyar ta koma kan gado ta kwanta kafin bacci yai gaba da ita, yanzu kam bacci ne mai nauyi ya dauketa.

********
A can bangaren Umar kuwa yayi sintiri harya gaji, ganin bashida wata hanyar dazai sa yasan inda aka kai Ibtisam yasa ya dawo gida.
 Yan Uwa yanzu kam ana zaune a falo duk kowa yayi jugum 'yar hirar ma duk kowa ya daina, wasu ma sun tafi sun kwanta bacci.
Shiga yai da sallama kansa a kasa kai bakace shine na dazu ba.
 Ganin haka yasa kowa hankalinsa yadan kwanta, tsugunawa yai sannan ya bada hakuri kafin ya nemi shiga dakin Bilkisu wai dan ya bata hakuri.
 Wannan abu ya kwantar da hankalin kowa nan fa ya wuce dakinta su kuma kowa ya saki hamdalla.
 Bilkisu tana zaune akan gado tana cin abincin da aka kawo mata, da taki ci sai dai Umma da kanta ta zauna ta tilastata sannan ta mata fada, jin sallar Umar yasa Umma ta kalli kofa cikin mamaki sannan ta amsa.
 Umar ya shiga ya tsuguna a kasa sannan yaba Umma hakuri, wani dadi ne ya ziyarci zuciyarta, ta fito daga dakin cikin jin dadi tana godiya ga Allah.
 Zama yai a kan gadan kafin ya watsa mata wani kallo, Bilkisu gabanta ne ya fadi ganin yanda ya canza, cokalin hannunta ta ajiye cikin tsoro tace "Ya Umar?"
 Wayarsa ya mika mata yace "Saka sabuwar number Ibtisam."
 Da sauri ta kalleshi kafin ta girgiza kai tace "Ibtisam ai bata canza number ba." Cikin zafi ya nunata da yatsa yace "Kinsan Allah? In har bakyasan na baki saki uku a daren nan ki fadamin gaskiya."
 Hawaye ne suka zubo mata tace "Wallahi bansan wata number ba bayan wacce take da ita."
 Fuskarsa a daure ya maida wayarsa yace "Wace unguwa aka kaita?"
 Nan ma kai ta girgiza tana haways tace "Wallahi shima bansani ba, kaga ko jere ba'a mata ba balle asan wace unguwar ce."
 Fuskarsa takai matuka gun baci, hannu ya sa ya fizgo wuyan rigarta, samun kanta tai da numfashi dakyar ya saketa yana hucin bacin rai, yace "ki tabbatar kin binciko inda aka kai ta kafin gobe da safe, kuma karki kuskura ki fadawa kowa ni na sakaki, in kuma takardarki kike nema ni a yanzu ma sai na mika miki."
 Yana kaiwa nan ya mike ya fito, da kallo ta bishi tana wasu zafafan hawaye masu zafi.
 Umar kam sai shimai albarka su Goggo suke dan tabbas yanzu kam hankalinsu ya kwanta, shikam waje ya fita zuciyarsa na wani irin tafasa, ranar haka ya kwana ko gyangyadi baiyi ba, ko idanunsa ya rufe hango Ibty yake a kusa da wannan sokon mutumin suna mai dariya.

*********
  Har akai kiran assalatu dukansu ba wanda ya farka, sai karfe bakwai na safe Aminu ya bude idanunshi, yana ganin agoggo a zabure ya mike tsaye gashi 8 ya kamata ya shiga office, wayarsa ya dauka yana kokarin sanar dasu zaiyi late kadan, sakon Baffa ne ya shigo "Na sanar yau bazaka samu zuwa aiki ba, ina kuma sa ran ka kula da matarka"
 "Baffa!" Abinda ya fada kenan tare da ajiyar zuciya, sakon Abba ya gani hakan yasa ya ajiye wayar tunda sakon ba shi akayiwa ba.
 A hankali ya shigo dakin ya bude jakanya kwashi kayan dazai saka, shikam ai a toilet ma zai shirya dan bayasan raini, ina girma ace kanasa kaya yarinya sa'ar kanwar kanwarka ta ganka?
  A hankali ya kwashi duk abinda zai saka sannan ya nufi toilet yana tafiya a hankali, kamar daga sama yaji ance "Ya Aminu ka yadda abu."
 Runtse ido yai sannan ya mike sosai ya kalleta fuskarsa a daure sannan ya kalli kasa, jiyai kamar zai nutse ganin boxers dinsa, wannan wani irin abin kunya ne? Cikin takaici ya dauka bai ko kalleta ba ya wuce toilet.
 Ibtisam kam batama kula da menene ba, ta danji motsi ne ta bude idanunta ganin abu ya fado yasa tamai magana.
 Aminu kam haka yai wanka ya shirya a bandaki duk ji yake raini ya gama shiga tunda har taga boxers dinshi. Fitowa yai fuskarsa a hade yana kokarin komawa falo.
 Ita kuma ta yashi ta gama gyara gado kenan tana jiran ya fito ta shiga tai alwala.
 Ganin ya fito yasa tace “Ina kwana?�?
 Dan kallanta yai na dan second sannan ya maida kansa gefe yace “Ya jikin naki?�?
 A hankali tace “Na warki.�?
 Harya fara tafiya tace “Ya Aminu? Abba yayi reply?�?
 Tsayawa yai sannan ya dauko wayarsa ya bude, mika mata yai bai bari ta kalli cikin idansa ba. Itakam a hankali ta karaso ta amsa ta duba sakon daya bude mata “Ibtisam ki kwantar da hankalinki nan komai ya daidaita, ki kula da mijinki.�?
 Idanu ta runtse sannan ta sauke wata ajiyar zuciya wacce yasashi kallanta,  daidainan idanunsu suka hadu da juna.
 Dauke idanu yai itama ta sauke nata kasa, mika mai wayar tai ya amsa yasa a aljihu, kafin yace “Me zakici? Zan je nai order a restaurant dinsu.�?
 Tace “Koma menene ba matsala.�?
Okay kawai yace sannan ya fita daga dakin, falo ya nufa ya tada sallar asuba.
Ibtisam wani ajiyar zuciyar ta sake saukewa yanzu kam najin dadi ne dan da alama komai ya daidaita a gida.
 Toilet ta shiga tai wanka tai alwala sannan ta fito tai sallah, jakartata ta bude ta dauko wata doguwar eigar lace wanda bayan an gama dinkin, akazo akai kwalliya da stones wanda ya fito da kyan dinkin sosai, ganin yanda kayan yadan kamata yasa ta dauko mayafin lace din ta ajiye akan gado sannan ta dau hijab ta tada sallah, ta dade tana kai kukanta gun Allah tana addu’ar Allah yasa kar sanadiyarta rayuwar Yayarta ta kara shiga cikin bala’i.
 Kallan dakin hotel din tai dan sai yanzu ta kurewa dakin kallo, hijab din jikinta ta ninke ta maida akwatin sannan ta fito falo tana dan tasan bayanan saboda taji alamun fitarsa tana sallah, a ranta tace “Da alama ansha kudi dan tasan babban daki irin wannan mai kyau dole a kashe kudi, jikinta ne yai sanyi cikin takaici tace “Duk laifinane da badan anyi haka ba ai ba yanda za’ai Abba ya sa a kaita cikin gaggawa haka.�?
 Zama tai a kan kujera tana mamakin wai ashe Umar shi ya dauka ita zai aura? Itakam ta kasa gane meke faruwa ta kuma kasa fahimtar kwantar da hankalinta ya kamata tai ko kuma kuka? Batasan auran Umar sai dai bazataso dalilinta yayarta ta shiga cikin bala’i da masifa ba.
 Ta tafi tunani sam bataji alamun bude kofarsa ba, doguwar rigarnan ce kawai a jikinta ko dankwali bata daura ba saboda ta leko taga falan ne ta koma ta daura dankwali ta sa babban mayafin data fito dashi.
Aminu kam kallanta yai cikin mamaki, a ransa yace “Har ta gama kukan amarcin?�?
 Kamar yanda ya saba gyaran murya yai hakan yasa ta kalli gun, idanu ta zaro a tsorace sannan ta mike cikin sauri, samun kanta tai da sa hannu a saman kirjinta, duk kunya ta rufeta tai daki cikin sauri har tana neman faduwa.
 Baki yadan bude cikin mamaki, me take rufewa? Lalai yaran zamani dauka tai kallan kirjinta yai ko me?
 Itakam daki ta shiga da sauri ta dauko mayafi ta yafa sannan ta zauna akan bakin gado tace “Wannan ai sai ya dauka banida kunya, jiya na kwan ina kuka yau nazo nunamai kaina.�? Fuskarta ta rufe da hannayenta tace “Dama yana min kallan sai a hankali yanzu na nunamai banda kunya.�? Tadan bubuga kafa a kasa tare da sake rufe fuskarta cikin jin haushin kanta.
 Aminu dake tsaye inda ta barshi duk yana kallan abinda take a daki, saboda a bude tabar kofar, kuma yana tsaye daga falan ta inda zai iya hango cikin dakin.
 “Me kuma takeyi hakan?�? Tambayar datazo mai kenan, Ibty ta bude ido cikin kunyar kanta kawai suka sake hada ido, sam ta manta bata kule dakin ba, shikenan itakam yau ta mutu, wannan abin kunyar har ina?
 Sandarewa tai a zaune ta kasa ko motsi shikam komawa yai ya zauna akan kujera tare da girgiza kai, wato yarinyarnan dasu Islam da alama banbancinsu kadan ne, waya ya dauko yana danawa yana jiran su kawo musu abinci.

*********

 An fito da kadan daga cikin kayan da aka riga aka siya na kayan dakin Ibtisam, nan wasu daga ciki suka taho dan fara shirin gida, su Umma kuma suka tafi kasuwa dan siyo ragowar, haryanzu Umma bata sauko akan abinda Ibty tai ba sai dai tasan wannan aikinta ne.
 Umar kam dama tunda yaji ana fitar da kaya ya shirya tsaf ya tari mai adaidaita ya tsaya daga lungun gidan, suna fitowa yasa abi bayan motar.
 Har kofar gidan akaje sai daya karewa gidan kallo sannan yaja wani tsakin takaici ya naushi iska yace “Wato kinga gidan kudi shiyasa kika wulakantani ko? Wallahi kinyi a banza shi kanshi wanda yai gangancin auran naki ya shirya fuskantar bala’i da masifa.
******
 Hajiya kam tana waje tana kallan yanda aka katange mata bangarenta, kwafa ta saki ta kalli gefe inda ake cigaba da ginin, lalai sai ta nunawa Asma’u ita ba kanwar lasa bace, ta dauka wannan abinda tai zaisa ta bar gidan ko me? Zama a gidan nan bataga uban daya isa hanata ba, sannan malaminta ya aanar mata inhar Aminu hankalinsa bai karkata kan mace ba to fa bazai taba korarta ba. Ita kuwa tanada yakinin babu macen da zata rudi Aminu, ya tabbatar ‘yarta kadai yakeso kuma har abada bazai so wata sama da ita ba.
 Su Ilham suna makaranta, juyawa tai zata koma ciki taji alamun bude gate daga bangaren Aminu, kallan gefe da ba’a karasa ba tai sannan ta nufi gun tana dan leke me Aminu yake a gida bai tafi aiki ba?
 Mamaki ne ya rufeta ganin motar kaya ta shigo, nan ta fara jiyo maganar mata daya nacewa “Kai kai kai! Nan ne gidan? Lalai Ibtisam tayi goshi, wannan hadaden gida in na shiga ai nikam banaji zan iya fitowa.�?

 Gaban Hajiya ne yai wani irin faduwa, me suke nufi da kalamansu, bata gama tunani ba taji wata ta saki guda tace “Kai masha Allah! Wannan tafkeken gida mai kyau haka? Ah Amarya taji dadi Allah yabarsu tarr ya kawo kazantar daki.�?
 Kanta ne taji yayi wani irin sarawa, me take juyowa haka? Jiya taje gun Malam akan a sake rufe bakin su Ummy sam bai mata wannan zancen ba, meke faruwa? Jitai kafafunta na neman kasa daukanta da sauri ta koma ciki, kasa zama tai ta fara yawo a cikin falan gaba daya kwakwalwarta ta kasa daukan abinda ke faruwa.
 Dole ta fita taji meke faruwa, dole kuma ta tafi gun Malam yanzun nan taji meke faruwa.

 Yanda gaba daya zufa ta fara keto mata ne zaisa ka dauka zafi akeyi, hijab dinta ta jawo ta dau jaka ta bude kasan gado ta debo kudi ta zuba a ciki, duk ta rikice da sauri ta zura takalmi sam bata kula wari da wari ta saka ba ta fita ta hau tafiya cikin sauri, sai datai nisa sannan ta tari adaidaita, sai datai canji na adaidaita hudu kafin ta isa canciki wani lungu, sauka tai sannan ta fito ta nufi gidan.

 Yanda gaba daya zufa ta fara keto mata ne zaisa ka dauka zafi akeyi, hijab dinta ta jawo ta dau jaka ta bude kasan gado ta debo kudi ta zuba a ciki, duk ta rikice da sauri ta zura takalmi sam bata kula wari da wari ta saka ba ta fita ta hau tafiya cikin sauri, sai datai nisa sannan ta tari adaidaita, sai datai canji na adaidaita hudu kafin ta isa canciki wani lungu, sauka tai sannan ta fito ta nufi gidan.

*********

 Duk yanda Hajiya taso ta kwantar da hankalinta ta kasa, harsu Ilham suka dawo daga islamiya, takaicinta ya kara karuwa dataga ko waya Aminu baiyi ba, bayan a iya saninta ko tafiya yai yana kira yaji yasu Islam suke.
 Kallansu tai suna zaune a falo suna cin abinci, islam sai zuba takeyi, wani abu ne yazo mata ta sa hannu ta baza ta gumi, ganin basu kula da ita ba yasa tadan fara abin sheshekar kuka, tana dan goge fuskarta da gefen dankwalinta. Ilham cikin damuwa tace “Hajiya jikin ne?�?

 Ai da sauri suka girgiza kai sukace bazamuyi ba Hajiya.
 Dadi ne yadan shigeta dole fa ta shirya kwatan kanta, bayan magani sai ta hada da amfani da jikokinta.

{Nace andaiji asara wallahi an kuma fado ba nauyi🤔}

“Bari mu kira ‘yan sanda sai ka musu bayani.�?

14🤸🏻‍♀�?
 Haka ya dawo ya zauna yana tunanin wai haka zai ta zama a falo ba abinyi? Sannan jiya bacci sam baimai dadi ba dan bai saba kwanan falo ba, an hanashi zuwa aiki sannan gadan ma an hanashi kwana? Shi raini ne bayaso amma da tsaf zai wuce ya kwanta akan gadan inyaso ita ta kwanta a falo tunda dai ita yarinyace basu da matsala da inda zasu kwana.
 Haka yai ta zaune, ya kishingida ya mike duk dai takaici ya isheshi, ganin magrib tayi yasa ya nufi daki dan yin alwala, Ibty kam tana mimike akan gado tanata latse laten waya, inama zataga Iman ta nuna mata wayarta? Itama ganin magrib tayi yasa ta mike tana dan danna wayarta tana nufar hanyar toilet.

 Yace “Ehen?�?

 Kallanta ya kuma yi ta zauna ta ajiye wayar tana murmushin da kana gani kasan na ya ke ne, kai ya girgiza sannan ya wuce toilet, shidai kam zaman nan ya isheshi, bari ai gobe tai ya kira Ummy gaskiya gobe yakesan komawa gidansa.
Harya fito tana nan zaune inda ya barta ta wani kame kamar gaske, shi abin ma so yai ya bashi dariya yanda taci serious haryanzu wayarta tana gefe bata dauka ba.
 Kai ya kauda sannan ya bude jakarsa dake dakin da alama abu yake san dauka.
 Ibty ta daure ta mike tare da daidaita tafiyarta cikin nutsuwa tanayi fuskarta haryanzu a hade, toilet ta shiga sannan ta rufo kofa.
 Da kallo yabi kofar toilet din lalai yarinyarnan, zai gani in zata iya canzawar, ai dai yarintar a jikinta take.

 Da safe Aminu yana cikin bacci akan kujera wayar Ummy ta shigo, mikewa yai sannan yai gyaran murya yana kallan agoggo, lalai rashin abinyi ya kamashi, bayan yayi asuba yau shine da komawa bacci? Sannan gashi bai tashi ba wai har tara da rabi na safe?

Hamdala tai a ranta tunda dai ya amince, haka kawai  ba haukar da zata sakata ta amince da uwar matarsa ta dinga kawo musu abinci, duk rashin wayanta bai kai nan ba.
Su Islam suka hau tsale suka dauko hijab dinsu suka saka, a tare suka fito hannunta rike da hannunsu.
 Gaban Hajiya ne ya fadi dan dama tun bayan ta dawo take leke ta katangar da sauran kadan a karasata, ganin ta gaji yasa ta koma falo, yanzu kam muryarsu Islam kadai dataji yasa ta fito da sauri.
 Ranta yakai kololuwa gun tashi ganin hannunta a cikin nasu Islam suna tafe suna dariya, huci ta fara tana kallan muzurai, ko ba komai ai ta fara aikin ta a cikin abinci, sannan jiya tasa mata laya a kasan katifa, kwafa tai ta koma ciki ranta a matukar bace, ta zauna a falo ta daura kafa daya kan daya tana girgizawa. Bata taba tunanin Aminu zai fara daukarta da yaransa su fita ba daga aure.

********

 Baya suka shiga ta shiga gaba, Aminu na kokarin shiga sai ga mai gadi ya taho da sauri, “Alhaji! Hajiya tayi bako.�?
 Bako? Yai tambayar cikin mamaki, ya dan sake sukunyo na girmamawa yace “Yayanta ne jiya da shekaran jiya dashi aka wuni ana aiki kayan daki, yanzu yazo wai yazo kawo mata abu.�?
Ba tare da ya kawo komai ba yace “Ya shigo mana.�?
Nan ya juya ya tafi kiransa, Umar kam wanda ya samu leda ya cikota da littatafan dayasa Bilkisu ta sa Iman ta dauko na Ibty, sannan ya rubuta mata waskila akan lalai lalai yau karfw goma na dare ta san yanda tai ta fito waje zaiso su wuce Sokoto, karta damu ya tattaro kudin da zasuyi tafiya.
 Shigowa yai, kallan gate tai ganin Aminu shima yana jiran ganin shigowarsa.
Wata muguwar faduwar gaba ce ta fadi mata, da sauri ta fara addu’a duk jitai ta rikice.
Idanunta ta kauda daga kallansa ta juya tana kallansu Islam.
Batai zata ba sai ji tai an kwankwasa glass din window dinta.
Inda Aminu yake ta kalla cikin firgice da tsoro, hannunta na rawa ta zuge glass din.
Umar ya zuba mata ido cikin tsananin kishi, ya dauka yanda ya birkice saboda rashinta itama tana nan tana rashinsa? Lalai yau ya tabbatar yarinyarnan munafuka ce, ba sanshi take ba. Sai dai wlh ko tana sansa ko bata sansa sai ta zauna zaman aure na har karshen rayuwa dashi.
Cikin rawar murya yaji tace “Ya Umar?�?
Idanunsa yai jaa sosai ta danyi yake, tana kallan Aminu daya zagayo ya mikawa Umar hannu, kallan hannunsa Umar yai sannan ya kalli Ibty, bai mika mai hannu sunyi musabaha ba kawai yadan ce “Ina kwanan mu?�?
Aminu ya maida hannunsa aljihu yace �? Ashe kaine, ko ba mijin yayarta bane?�?
Wani abu ne ya tokare mai makoshi ya zabgawa Ibty wani kallo sannan ya kalli Aminu da jajayen idanunsa.
 Aminu ya kalli Ibty yace “ki fito ku gaisa.�?
Daga zaune tace “Ina kwana?�?
Umar mamaki ya kara kamashi wato fitowarce bazatai ba?�?
Aminu ganin yanda Umar ke kallansa yasa ya shiga mota ya zauna, Umar idanunsa nakan Ibty ya a zuba mata wani mugun kallo, itakam ta kasa sake kallansa sai rawa da jikinta keyi daga ciki.
 Aminu na shigowa ta samu kanta da rike hannunsa da sauri.
 Mamaki ne ya kamashi ya kalleta, kallansa tai tare da yin yake. Yanda jikinta ke dan rawa ya kalla, tabbas akwai abinda ke faruwa.
 Umar kam ji yake kamar wanda ake hurashi a cikin wuta, zuro ledar yai baice komai ba ya zabgawa yaran Aminu harara sannan ya juya ya fita.
Islam ta kalli Ilham tace “Wannan sai yai kama da irin mugayen nan ko?�?
Ilham tace “Nima tsoro ya bani.�?
Haryanzu Ibty bata sake hannun Aminu ba, kallanta kawai yake yanda haryanzu jikinta bai dawo daidai ba.
 “Mu wuce?�? Abinda ya tambayeta kenan, ta daga kai alamar eh.
Ya kalleta sannan ya kalli hannunsa, da sauri ta sakeshi sannan ta zauna, nan ya tada mota suka fito.
Daga waje sukaga Umar, Aminu yadan tsaya ganin yana tafiya da kafa yace “Ko zamu rage ma hanya?�?
Ai Ibty gaba daya ta sake rikicewa, Umar ya mai wani kallan tsana yace “Da sayyadata.�?
Aminu yasa hannu a aljihu ya dauko kudi yana neman mikamai, da sauri Ibty ta rike hannunsa dan tafi kowa sanin Umar ya tsani a rainashi, balle akanta. Yanzu saboda kudin nan batasan me zai aikata ba.
 Maidawa Aminu yai sannan yace “To ka gaida su Umma.�?
Umar kam hannun Aminu data sake rikewa kawai yake kallo lalai zai iya yin kisa da akwai wuka a jikinsa, tab.
Gaba sukai tadan juyo ta kalleshi ta madubin mota, Aminu kam mamakin yanda haryanzu tai shiru ta takure a gefe yake, ance gida daya suka taso, meyasa take shakkarsa haka?
Umar kasa motsi yai ya tsaya kawai yana kallan motarsu harta kule, lalai duk wanda yake da hannu a cikin abin nan ya shirya fuskantar bala’i da masifa, ai ko bayan ya dauke Ibty wallahi sai ya dau fansar rike hannun Aminu datai.

{Nikam Ayusher kasa ko magana nai, anya mutumin nan nasa man kai?}

17🤸🏻‍♀�?
 Har suka isa Jifatu muryar Islam da Ilham ce kawai ke tashi, sam ko magana ta kasayi, duk wannan abinda take yana lure da ita, tabbas akwai abinda ke faruwa a gidansu. Baisan ko menene ba amma da alama shine dalilin dayasa mahaifinta ya kawota ba ranar da suka tsara ba.
 Parking yai sannan taji muryar Islam tana cewa “Kawar Aunty mun iso.�?
 Firgigit tai tare da kallan gun, sannan ta kalli Aminu wanda shima yake kallanta yana ganin zata juyo ya dauke kai tare da kallan gefe.
 Fitowa tai sannan ta budewa su Ilham, nan suka fito ta rike hannunsu.
 Kallan cikin motar tai bataga alamar yanada shirin fitowa ba. Dan sako kanta tai saitin glass din motar tace “Ya Aminu muje?�?
 Ya kalleta sannan ya kalli shagon yace “Kece me siyayya ki shiga kafin ki gama daukan abinda kikeso zan shigo.�?
 Mamaki ne ya kamata tace “Yanzu ni kadai zan shiga ga yara ga siyayya?�?
 Kallansu Islam yai kamar zaiyi magana sai kuma taga ya sake daurewa, ya gyara zaman farin glasses din dake fuskarsa sannan ya fito.
 Da gusu Ilham ta kamo hannunsa sannan ta jashi kusa da inda su Islam suke. A tare suka shiga cikin shagon, kallansa tai tace “Ya Aminu cart din da zamu zuba kaya.�?
 Wani kallo ya mata yace “Hannunki fa?�?
Ta kalleshi ta nuna jakarta da hannun Islam datake rike dashi, aransa yace “Lalai yarinyarnan, wannan fa in na kyaleta da alama aiki ma sani zatai.�?
 Ya dago yace “Bazai iya ba intaga dama ta turo�? kawai yaga bata gun. Can yadan hangota harta fara daukan abu tana nunawa Islam suna dariya.
 Wata kwafa yai sannan yaja cart din kai kace shine ya batamai rai. Takaici duk ya ishehi, banda ta rainashi ina zai dingabin mace zagagai zagagai tana siyan kaya shi kuma yana tura mata kayan? Dazun nan ya gama tausayinta ashe zata wulakantashi. Yanzu in wani ya ganshi ai ta gama zubarmai da girma.
 Ibty kam batasan ma yanayi ba, Ilham ta dawo kusa da ita suna tafe suna daukan kaya intaga abin zagin yayi yawa tace a’a.
 Haka suka gama siyayyarsu ya biya sannan suka koma mota, a zuciye ya fizgi mota suka hau kan titi.
 Wayartace ta fara kara, ta dauko tare da yin sallama cikin girmamawa tace “Ummy ina wuni?�? Jin ta ambaci Ummy yasa yadan rage gudun tare dadan kasa kune yaji me zatace.
 Ummy bayan sun gaisa tace “Ibtisam ina Aminu? Na kira wayarsa bansamu ba.�?
 Tadan kalleshi kadan tace “Gamu tare mun fita siyayya.�? Kunya ce ta kama Aminu ji yake kamar ya fizge wayar, shikam yarinyarnan yau ta shirya kaskantar dashi.
 Ji yai ta karamai wayarta a kunne, dan gyaran murya yai sannan ya gaida Ummy.
Cikin jin dadin da kanajin maganarta zaka gane tace “Aminu ko zaku biyo ta gida tunda kun fito?�?
“Ummy inada aiki wlh�?
Tace “In ka koma kayi, sannan ai ba dadewa zakuyi ba, dama naso zuwa ganin gida ne amma tunda kun fito ku karaso dan Allah.�?
 To kawai yace ta kashe wayar cikin jin dadi, dan dama hankalinta ne sam yaki kwanciya tun jiya shiyasa ta yanke shawarar zuwa dan dole ta yiwa Ibtisam gargadi dan sakacinsu ne yaja musu gashinan mata tafi karfinsu.

******
Murna da tsale su Islam suka fara sai da ya kallesu sannan suka zauna, Ibty tai shiru tana kallan wayarta, so take taji muryar mahaifiyarta, da ‘yan uwanta amma ba dama, itakam wannan bala’i dame yai kama?
 Gun parking na cikin gidan ya tsaya, tunda suka shigo ta hango Ainah a tsaye a harabar gun tana rike da kugu tana girgiza ta turo dankwali gaba irin ture kaga tsiya din nan, dariya ce ta kusa kwacewa Ibty.
 Aminu na fitowa ya kalli Ainah, ai tana ganinshi ta ruga ciki a guje, dakinta ta shige tace “Sam na manta dashi ina jiran masifa.�?
 Ibty tana kallan sanda ta ruga ciki, dariya ce tadan sake zuwa, ido suka hada da Aminu tadan maze, kai ya girgiza sannan ya wuce ciki.
 Islam ma a guje suka shiga ciki, itakam sai data kara gyara zaman mayafinta ya rufe kanta sosai sannan ta fito.
 Ainah tana leke ta kofa tana ganin Aminu ya wuce bangaren Ummy ta fito da sauri, Ibty na shigowa ta fizgo hannunta sukai dakinta.
 Rufewa tai ta kara rike kugu tace “Sannu Aminiya wacce bata boyemin sirri.�?
Ibty ta dafata tace “Wannan daurin fa? Bansan kin iya irinshi ba ai da tuni nazo kin koyamun.�?
Ainah ta maze tace “Wai dagaske ke kika auri Ya Aminu? Ni wlh koda Ummy ta fadamin shekaranjiya na dauka karya ne.�?
 Ibty ta dan ture dankwalin kanta tace �? To yanzu me kikeso ayi? Ko muje gun Ummy ta shiryamu?�?
Aina tadan shako wuyanta kadan tace “Allah kinci amanata.�?
Dariya sukai sosai, kafin su zauna a bakin gado.
 Ibty ta kalli dankwalinta daya fadi sannan tace “Banje na gaida Ummy ba kin turen dankwali.�? Gaban madubi ta koma ta fara gyara dankwalinta, Ainah ta matso tace “Wai ya akai wannan abu ya faru? Sannan ina Ya Umar? Allah dai yasa karya kashe min yayana.�?
 Ibty tadan waigo tace “Hmm Ainah nikaina abubuwan dasuka faru jinake kamar a mafarki, ki barni naje na gaida Ummy koma menene zan fada miki ai kinsan bana iya boye miki abu.�?
Tare suka fito suka nufi falan Ummy, su Islam ana zaune kan cinyarta anata zuba.
 Ummy kam kallan Ibty kawai take tana hamdallah a ranta, sauke Islam tai sannan tace “Ibtisam muje ciki mu gaisa.�?
Tai maganar tare da nufar dakinta, Aminu na zaune a kasa yana daddana wayarsa, Ainah ta karaso ta tsuguna tana gaisheshi.
Fuska a hade yace “Dazu gidan dambe kike shirin zuwa?�?
 Tace “A’a Yaya haushi naji Ibtisam bata fadamin komai ba gameda aurenta.�?
 “Sannu Yaya babba, dolene dan kuna kawaye sai kinsan komai?�?
 Cikin dan shagwaba tace “Nifa ya kamata na mata babbar kawa.�?
 Kai yadan girgiza ya maida hankalinsa kan wayarsa yace “Yanzun ma ai sai kiyi bata baci ba.�?
 Tasan magana ya fada mata, ta dan waske ta fara yiwa su Islam magana.
*****
A daki kuwa Ummy bayan Ibtisam ta shigo taga ta mike tasa ma dakin makuli, mamaki ne ya kamata sai dai bata nunaba, zama tai a kasa cikin girmamawa tare da sauke kanta kasa.
 Ummy tagani ta zauna kusa da ita, sannan suka sake gaisawa, hannunta da take wasa dasu taji Ummy ta riko, kallanta tai sannan ta maida kanta kasa.
 Ummy tace “Ibtisam mun miki laifi ko?�?
 Dagowa tai idanunta suka danyi raurau kafin ta girgiza kai tace “A’a!�?
Itama kai ta girgiza tai tace “Bamu sanar dake babbar matsalar dake tattare da auranki ba, Mun rufe miki abu mafi mahimmanci wanda ya kamata ki sani.�?
 Shiru tai a hankali hawaye suka taru a cikin idanunta, Ummy ta dan dago da kanta tace “Bansan meke faruwa ba Ibty, munyi iyayinmu munyi addu’a munyi sadaka sai dai matarnan taki fita daga gidan nan, abin tashin hankalin shine yanzu hartakai ta kawo bama iya mata magana a gabanta, wanda na tabbatar abu ta mana, ga magana a bakina amma ina ganinta bana iya zafafawa.�?
 Mamaki sosai kalaman Ummy suka bata, habawa shiyasa dan dama itakam ta tabbatar ba kalau ba, ina aka taba wannan kwanacalar?
 Ummy ta kara rike hannunta tace “Da Allah muka dogara sai fatan Allah ya baki ikon cetomu daga cikin wannan bala’in.�?
Bakinta na rawa tace “Ni?�?
Kai ta daga tace “Aminu mijinki ne, kinfi kowa hakki akansa, bayan ‘ya’yansa, Ibtisam ki taimakemu ki kwato mana yaran nan daga bala’in dayake ciki.�?
 Kallan Ummy take a tsorace, ita me zata iya? Inhar su manya sun kasa?
 Hannun Ummy taji a kafadarta tace “Kada ki kuskura kisha ko ruwa daga gun matarnan.�?
 Ibty a tsorace take kallan Ummy, “Kada ki bari ta shigar miki dakinki, kitchen ko toilet, duk inda zata zauna to karya wuce falo. Ki taimakemu karki sake bari Aminu yaci abincin matarnan.�?
Ibty gaba daya jitai kanta ya fara nauyi, “Sannan kija mijinki a jiki, Aminu nada saukin kai inhar kika fahimceshi, na sani sarai yanzu bakya gabansa sai dai a matsayinki na mace dole ki mike tsaye ki shawo kan mijinki, kada ki bari kunya ko kuma shakkarsa dan ya girmeki sosai yasa ki kasa kwato mijinki.�?
 Ta kasa ko firta kalma daya sai shiru kawai datai tana kallan Ummy.
“Tare kuke kwana?�?
Kunya ce ta rufeta da sauri tai kasa dakai, Ummy ta jinjina kai tace “Nayi tunanin haka, sai dai Ibtisam inaso kimin alkawari daga daren jiya bazaki sake bari ku kwana daki daban daban ba, inhar ba uzuri bane ya kama.�?
 Kanta na kasa ta daga kai alamar to.
Ummy ta cigaba da yi mata bayani akan Hajiya wanda gaba daya kanta ya gama kullewa lalai itakam tayi aure a wahale, wannan yaki akeso tayi a gidan miji ko me? Haka suka fito tare itadai gaba daya jikinta ba laka, anan sukaci abincin rana sannan aka hada musu abinci a kula wanda zasu tafi dashi na dare.
 Ainah kam tayi mamaki sosai jin yanda auransu ya kasance, tausayin Ibty ya kara kamata dan ta tabbatar Ya Aminu haryanzu matarsa ce a ransa.

*********

 Da karfi ya hankada keken saura kiris ta fadi kasa, tai saurin rike bango.
 Kallansa tai idanunta na zubar da kwalla, ya matso yasa hannu ya shako wuyan rigarta. Tun jiya ciwonta ya tashi, amma ta rasa wa zata fadawa, sai daurewa kawai takeyi.
 Idanun Umar yayi jaa kamar gauta yace “Munafukar banza da wofi daga ke har iyayenki munafukai ne kuma azzalumai ne.�?
 Bilkisu ta share kwallarta tace “Ya Umar kayi hakuri dan Allah.�?
 Ya kara rikota sosai yace “Wallahi sai na wahalar dake wahalar da saikinyi dana sani, ‘yancinki daya shine abinda nake shiri yau ya tabbata amma wlh in wani abu ya kawo cikas to wallahi ki shirya haduwa da bala’i da masifa.�?
 Ya hankadata yai ciki, hawaye ta shiga gogewa, tana tsananin san Ya Umar gani take kome zai mata indai har suna tare to fa zata jure.
 Anayin Isha’i ya fito dana ya hada kayansa yakai gidan wani abokinsa.
 Kai tsaye yawuce unguwar su Ibty.
*****
Hajiya yau dakyar ta iya cin abinci, ta hanke shawarar kwanciya kawai tace batada lafiya dan ta tabbatar Aminu zai dawo hayyacinsa inyaji ciwonta ya tashi.
 Sai dai tunani ya hanata iya kwanciyar, sako ta turamai akan ya taho mata da magani.
 Ai yana gani ya tattaro su Ibty suka fito duk ya rikice, mamaki sosai ya kama Ibty yanzu kam ta tabbatar da kalaman Ummy wannan ba’a hayyacinsa yake ba, yana zuwa ko kofar motarsa bai rufe ba ya nufi bangarenta. 
 Ibtisam ta wuce ciki bayan ta debi wasu kaya, dan Ummy tace abarsu Islam sai gobe lahadi sai su dawo.
 Sam ta manta da litattafan da Umar ya kawo mata, da a kusa da ita ta ajiye batasan ya akai ba taga an maidasu bayan mota, ta tabbatar Aminu ne tunda shikadai ne ya shiga motar.
 Shiru tai bayan ta shiga daki, kalaman Ummy ne keta mata yawo akai, gaskiya bazata zauna kamar mace mara ilimi ba, ko yaran kauye yanzu sunzan yanda ake tafiya da namiji, balle ita.
 Mikewa tai ta farayin wanka sannan ta dauko kayan bacci daya daga cikin na lefen ta dan an kawo mata kayan lefenta, maidashi tai da sauri tana dan dadiya kadan tace “Wannan in na saka ai daukata zaiyi ‘yar iska.�?
 Haka tai ya dubawa sai can ta samo wata doguwar riga ta zura sannan ta saka hijab dinta, falo ta fito tana dan sake gyarawa, tana yi tana kallan window sai yaushe zai dawo? Itakam da gun kishiya yaje ma a kalla tasan inda yaje amma wannan dame zata kirata?
 Haka ta shiga kitchen ta karasa wanke kufunan safe sannan ta jera abincin da suka taho dashi daga gidan Ummy.

Aminu kam sai dayaga tasha magani sannan ya mike yana shirin fitowa.
Kuka tasa wanda ya daga mai hankali, tace “Aminu nikam komawa kauye zanyi kwanan nan tunanin Na’ima ya hanani sukuni, tunda kai aure nake ganin shikenan rayuwar Na’ima ta kare.�?
 Dawowa yai ya tsuguna yace “Hajiya meyasa kike fadan haka? Ko da wasa bazan iya mantawa da Na’imah ba sannan ga ‘ya’yanta nan wanda a koda yaushe inka kallesu zaga tuna da ita.�?
Ta sake sa kuka tace “Na tabbatar matarka ma bazataso zamana a gidan nan ba, dama mahaifiyarka baso take ba, Aminu ka maidani kauye dan Allah.�?
 Tausayinta ya kara kamashi yace “Hajiya ki kwanta ki huta, da safe mayi magana.�?
Haka ya fito duk zuciyarshi ba dadi, da alama auren nan ne yasa duk abin duniya ya dameta, tana ganin wata ta maye gurbin Na’imah.

Mota ya karasa ya shiga da ragowar manyan kayan abincin, tana kitchen tana wanke sink ya shigo, kallansa tai tace “Hajiyar batada lafiya ne?�?
 Ya kalleta sosai yace “Me Ummy tace miki da kuka shiga daki?�?
Kallan mamaki tamai tace “Hirar duniya mukai tamin nasiha.�?
 Kamar zaiyi magana sai taga yana neman fita da sauri tace “Wani abin akace?�?
 Ya juyo sosai yana kallanta yace “Hajiya tace kema basan zamanta kike ba, so na dauka Ummy ta miki zancenta ne.�? Abinda yace kenan ya fita, da kallo ta bishi baki a bude.
 Ibty kinga ta kanki, yaki yana gabanki, dole na maido da hankalin Aminu kaina kafin na san yanda zanyi na sallami matarnan.
 To amma ta ina? Abinda iyayensa suka kasa na isa nayi?

 *******

 Yayi sintiri harya gaji ga uban sanyi da akeyi, ji yake kamar gabobin jikinsa sun daina aiki. 
Karfe sha biyu da rabi? Lalai Ibtisam ta nunamai inda ta ajiyeshi shima kuma yanzu ya dau alkawarin nuna mata wulakantashi datai ba abinda zai jawo mata sai dana sani.
Haka ya koma gida, ya shiga bargo kankace me zazzabin soyayya da rashin Ibty ya rufeshi, wasu zafaffan kwala ne suka zubomai, lalai anci amanarsa kuma wlh bazai yafe ba. Tun yarinyarnan tana yarinya yake santa yake tanadinta yake ajiyarta, sannan wani katan banza yazo ya dauketa? Inya tuna haka jiyake zuciyarsa na wani irin tafasa.

18🤸🏻‍♀�?
 Tsaye tai a cikin dakinta tana tunanin yanda zata tunkari dakin Aminu, ta zauna a gaban madubi tana kallan kanta. Ajiyar zuciya tadanyi mai sauti sannan ta dan gyara fuskarta ta dau turare tadan fesa, shiru ta sakeyi tana tuno yanda Amare ke dan naku-nuku in tana karanta novel, to ita gashi sai dai takai kanta?  Hijab dinta ta dauka ta zura sannan ta nufi bangarensa.
 Agoggon falansa ta kalla wanda ya nuna karfe goma na dare, kwan wutar dakinsa tana kune da alama bayan yaci abinci ciki ya shiga. Gabanta ne ke faduwa akai-akai harta isa kofar dakin, hannu tasa tana neman bude dakin da sauri ta sauke hannunta. In ya korota fa? Gashi tawa Ummy alkawari? Hannu ta kara sawa cikin shakka ta runtse idanta da karfi sannan ta bude kofar, bakinta dauke da sallama.
 Bata kalli inda yake ba dan ta tabbatar suna hada ido zata rasa abinyi, tana jinsa sanda ya amsa sallamar amma kanta na kasa ta nufi gado tana kallan kasan dakin.
 Aminu dake zaune akan gado ya mike kafa yana dane-dane a computer ne ya tsaya cak yana kallanta. Me takeyi? Tambayar datazo mai kenan. Bai gama tunanin me take shirin aikatawa ba kawai yaga ta nufi kan gado inda yake.
 Dan idanta ta dago kadan ta hango kafar Aminu a saitin da take kokarin kwanciya, da sauri ta koma baya ta zagaya ta daya bangaren, shikam idanunsa na kanta yana kallan ikon Allah.
 Kamar zata zauna a gadan sai kuma ta wuce toilet dinshi, brush dinta data dauko ta ajiye a ciki, sannan ta furzar da wata iska, shafar kirjinta tai sannan ta kara fitowa, kan gadan ta karasa, kawai yaga ta kwanta ta juya baya, tsananin mamakin wannan lamari ya hanashi magana.
 Jin baice komai ba yasa tadan saki ranta, hannu tasa saboda ta ji sanyi na ratsa kafarta, tana dan laluban bargo, idanunta a rufe sannan ta juya. Kallan hannun yai sannan ya janye bargon daga inda hannunta zai kamo, ganin ta kasa kamo bargon yasa ta dan takure cikin hijab dinta.
 “Me kike anan?�?
Abinda taji yace kenan, bakinta nadan rawa tace “Anan zan kwana.�?
“Anan zakiyi me?�? Yai tambayar yana kara kallan bayanta cikin mamaki, Ibty tai shiru tare da maida idanunta ta rufe ta fara dan sauke numfashi kadan kadan alamar bacci.
 Abin ma dariya yaso bashi, yace “Anan zakiyi me kikace?�?
 Shiru nan ma ba amsa, ajiye laptop dinsa yai a side bed sannan yasa hannu ya juyo da ita saitinsa, haryanzu idanunta a rufe wai a dole bacci take, saketa yai sannan ya zuba mata ido yana ganin sanda zata bude ido. Ibty kam idanunta kam, jin shirun yayi yawa yasa tadan kasa kunne, motsi taji kamar an sauka daga kan gadon, sai kuma taji alamar rufe kofa, idanta daya tadan kyalla kadan tanasan ganin ko yana nan, ganin bakowa agun yasa ta danyi fifita da hannunta sannan ta dan mike ta zauna tana sake fitar da wata ‘yar iska.
 Gyaran murya taji daga gefen toilet, idanu ta zaro ai bata kalli gun ba tai saurin kwanciya ta runtse idanunta kyam.
 Aminu baisan sanda wani murmushi ya bayyana a fuskarsa ba lalai yarinyarnan.
 Karasowa yai ya zauna yace “Tashi ki fadamin me kike a dakin nan? Ko kuma na kinkimeki na maidake dakinki.�?
 Kin bude ido tai, yace “in irga uku?�?
 Nan taji yace “Daya, biyu, uku!�?
 Hannunsa taji ya sakalo ta wuyanta da sauri ta bude ido tare da cewa “Ya Aminu dan Allah ka bari, tsoro nakeji, bazan iya kwana ni kadai ba.�?

Ta jinjina kai da sauri alamar eh tace “tsoro ne dani sosai, bakaga jiya a gunsu Islam na kwana ba?�?
“Ahhhhhh tsoro ko?�? Ta daga kai da sauri. “Oh! Shiyasa naga sanda kike kwana a daki ke kadai a hotel cikin dare ihu kikeyi kina dawowa inda nake.�?
 Ganin ya kamota yasa tadan hada labanta biyu ta rufe tace “Na kwana ni kadai ko?�?
 Bai amsa mata ba sai kallanta dayai, tadanyi murmushin yake tace “To koma dai na kwana da yanzu tsoro nake.�?

Dan kallansa tai kadan taga yanda ya kafeta da ido, da alama yanzun ma bai kama karyar ba.
 “In nace miki yarinya kiji haushi, yanzu wasu tatsuniyoyin kike jeromin?�?

 “Sai na fadawa Ummy tasan abinda kace�? Baiyi tunanin jin wannan amsar ba sam. Kasa magana yai sai kallanta dayai, eh lalai Ummy ta kara bari yarinyar nan ta rainashi.
 Ko ya sauka ya bar mata dakin? Tunowa yai da kwanciyar kujera dayai a hotel yanda ya dinga tashi da ciwon baya, abinka da shekaru sun dan fara kamawa.
 Yanzu haka zai dinga kwana da yarinya a gado daya? Bai kwanta da ita bama ta rainashi balle sun kwana gado daya? 
 Aikin sam kasa karasawa yai haka yanayi yana kallanta cikin takaici ya kashe wuta ya kwanta, ya dade yana juye-juye kafin bacci ya daukeshi.

Wajen karfe biyu da rabi na dare mumunan mafarki ta farayi, wai gatanan Umar ya rufeta a wani daki a daji, abinci ma ta window yake cila mata. Sam saboda tsoro batasan ta shigo jikin Aminu ba, cikin bacci yaji an rikeshi sosai, idanu ya bude sannan yasa hannu ya kuna fitilar wayarsa, mamaki ne ya kamashi ganinta a jikinsa sosai ta rikeshi. Hannu yasa yana kokarin janyeta daga jikinsa, hawayen dayake gangarowa a idanunta ne yasashi tsayawa yana kallanta, wannan yarinyar tabbas akwai abinda ke cinta, to shi bata fadamai ba taya zai tambayeta? Ko saboda batasanshi ne kullum take mafarki da koke koke cikin bacci? Saman goshinsa ya shafa yana sake kallanta, harya sake kai hannu zai tureta sai kuma ya fasa.
 Hannunsa ya daura a saman goshinsa kawai ya rufe idansa, da alama yanzu da take jikinsa bacci take shikam sam ya kasa komawa bacci, a haka zai cigaba da kwana da ita? Kwanansa nawa kenan raban dayai bacci mai dadi?
  Sai wajen asuba sannan bacci ya daukeshi, cikin bacci taji alamun kiran sallah, idanunta ta bude a hankali, tana neman yin mika.
 Ganinta yai a makale a jikin Aminu, ta sakalo hannunta a tsakanin cikinsa da kirjinsa. Idanu ta zaro ta dan janye hannunta a hankali ta shige toilet.
 Brush tai sannan tai alwala tana yi tana tuno yanayin data ganta, oh itakam shikenan zai ce batada kamun kai. Wannan abin kunya dame yai kama?
 Fitowa tai daga toilet tana gyara zaman hijab dinta, fitilar wayarsa ya hasketa dashi.
 Da sauri ta dan juya baya, yace “Kin gama daddaneni kin tashi?�?

 Kai kawai ya girgiza sannan ya mike yai toilet, wato yarinyarnan da inta kalleshi bata iya magana yanda ya kamata amma yau data kwana dashi harta fara? Lalai wannan yana nuna kwana dashi ne yake kara jawo raini.
 Alwala yai ya fito, ganinta yai tana lafila, nan yadau wayarsa da carbi ya fito daga dakin.
 Harya isa masallaci yana tunanin meke damun yarinya karama haka? Da ko bacci batayi mai dadi? Ko ya tambayi Ainah? Kai ya girgiza da sauri dan ya tabbatar inya tambayi Ainah zata fada mata ko kuma ta fadawa Ummy.
 Harya dawo daga masallaci abin bai bar ransa ba, tunda ya shigo bangarensa yaki jiyo zazzakar muryarta tana karatun qur’ani, har ya nufi dakin sai naga ya dawo ya zauna akan kujera ya dan mike kafafunsa ya kwantar da kansa a saman kujera tare da lumshe ido yana juyo karatunta, baisan tanada murya mai dadin sauraro ba haka, sam baisan sanda bacci yai gaba dashi ba.
 Ibty kam sai data kai karshen Suratul Ma’idah sannan ta kalli yanda gari yai haske, lalai karatu rahama ne, kwanan nan bata samu damar yin karatu ba tana tunanin karta takuramai a hotel, jiya kuma batasan ta tashi su Islam, agoggo ta kalla karfe shida da rabi.
 Fitowa tai tana mamakin yanda akai bai dawo ba, akan kujera ga ganshi, jikinta ne yai sanyi da alama saboda baisan shiga daki tana nan ne yasa ya kwanta anan.
 Sam jitai zuciyarta ba dadi, a rayuwarta batasan taga ta shiga hakkin wani, dan karamin murmushi tai sannan ta koma daki ta kalli bargon dake kan gado tace “Wato a film in naga namiji ko mace suna luluba bargo in ana bacci abin naban dariya yau nima da alama zan kwaikwayesu.�?
Daukan bargon tai ta nufi falo, a hankali ta shiga ya ware bargon sannan ta lulubamai, juyawa tai zata koma sai jitai yace “Dadin ki sake taremin gado ko?�?
 Kallansa tai, mikewa yai tare da daukan bargon yace “Yanzu ai safiyace bakya bukatar kwana dani ko?�?
 Wannan wani irin mutum ne? Ita a film in aka shimfida bargo gani take ana godiya, ko a rungume ka, ko kuma adan kalleka ai murmushin jin dadi, in kuma mutum bai farka ba inya tashi zaiji dadi. To ita gashi tayi abin arziki an bige da fada mata magana.
 Dakinsa ya wuce, gani yai ta gyara gadan, kwanciya yai ya maida idansa ya rufe.
 Ibty kam bangarenta ta koma, dakinta ta shiga ta zauna a kan gado tana kallan dakin, kalaman Ummy ke sake dawo mata, itakam gaba daya yanzu komai shakkarsa take a gidan, batasan taganta ita kadai sai taga kamar wani abin zai sameta.
 Wayarta ya dauko kawai ta koma falo ta zauna tana dane dane a haka har bakwai da kwata tai sannan ta wuce kitchen, me zata dafa? Nan fa ta fara hidimar harhada ingredients tana tunanin yau ce rana ta farko da zaici abincin ta dole ta zake karya sake ce mata yarinya.

 Potato omelette tai sannan tai sauce na hanta, ta dan soya dankali kadan tasa daga gefe, ta dafa tea ta zuba a flask sannan ta shiga tai wanka, lace ta dauko na daya daga cikin lefenta ta saka, tayi kyau sosai, ta gyara gashinta duk dabai bamai tsayi bane sosai amma ba gudil bane.  Tace “Waye?�?

 Bakinta nadan rawa tace “Hajiya mun gode amma na riga nayi abinci.�?

 Lalai kallan kitse nakewa rogo, amma ba tasaka  mata laya ba? Badai tare take kwana da Aminu ba?
Kallan zargi tawa Ibtisam wacce ke wasa da yatsun hannunta tana addu’ar tsari a ranta.
 Hajiya ta juya hannunta dauke da tire, cikin tsananin kunar rai ta isa bangarenta, abincin ta juye a cikin bola sannan ta zauna ta daura kafa daya kan daya tana girgizawa lalai da alama yarinyarnan ba wacce ya kamata ta raga mata bace.

Ganin yanda yai murmushi yasa tadan fadada fara’arta tace “Yaya tsakani da Allah kai babba ne? Irin babban babba?�?
Fuska ya daure sosai tare da yi mata wani kallo, ta dan girgiza hanayenta da sauri tace “Karka fassara maganata wlh ni dariya abin yake ban in naga kanata zancen girma, na dauka mutanen dasuka kai 50 zuwa sama sune suke zancen girma.�?
Sosai ya daure fuska yace “Wato dai so kike kice nima ba babba bane?�?
 Tana dan guntse dariyarta tace “Kai ma ba babba bane nima ba yarinya bace.�?
 Wani kallo ya mata sannan ya cigaba da aikinsa, ya kula in aka tabata to fa akwai magana, shi kuma bai fiya san magana tai tsayi ba.
 Ibty kam samun kanta tai cikin farinciki, a kalla yau sunyi magana mai tsayi, murmushi tai tayi.

********

 Naman cikin farfesun duk ya juye a cikin plate dinsa, kana gani kasan saboda mugunta yai, Bilkisu kam koko tasha ta gutsiri bread tana hadawa dashi, batama taba farfesun da aka aiko musu dashi ba, jiyai ya koshi duk yanda yake turawa, kallanta yai cikin tsana yace “karki taba roman nan ki juyemin a kwano zan kaiwa abokina.�?
A hankali tace to. 
Wani haushi ya kara kamashi danshi ladabin nan konamai rai yakeyi, mikewa yai ya shiga daki ya cire kaya ya watsar a kasa sannan yai wanka, kaya yasa ya fito yana kallanta tanata kokarin gyara falan. Kafa yasa yadan tura keken sannan yace “Dalla ki dinga yi kina kallan hanya, kinso da keke kin cika wannan dan karamin falan, ta ina kikesan mutum ya wuce banda kina kokarin sani na fadi?�? Yaja wani tsaki tare da daukan ledar datasa kwanan a ciki ya fita yana masifa.
Kwallarta kawai ta share ta cigaba da aikinta.

*****
Abangaren Hajiya kuwa tun bayan dawowarta ta kasa zaune ta kasa tsaye, ganin zaman ba inda zai kaita kawai ta fito, abin takaici an gama ginin katangar balle ta leka ta gani ko Aminu nanan.
 Waje ta fito kawai ta hau adaidaita ta wuce gun malaminta.
 Sai daya dade yana dube dubensa sannan yace “Yanzu me kikeso ayi mata? A haukatata ko kuma a mata ture ta shiga duniya.�?
Hajiya tace “in akai haka kowa zargina zaiyi nidai kawai ka ban abinda zan dinga juyata sai abinda nace sai kuma abinda na tsara, bazata taba kallona tamin musu ba. Sannan ka sake tabbatarwa kar wani abu ya shiga tsakaninsu na auratayya. Sannan ka tura mata aljani wanda zai rikitata yasa Aminu ya fara daukarta mahaukaciya ko mara lafiya.�?
Dariya ya bushe dashi yace “Wannan layar da kikasa inhar ta kwanta akai to fa ita dashi har abada dan bazai taba sha’awarta ba, sannan zancen yi miki biyayya wannan abune mai sauki�?
Ya miko mata magani yace ki tabbatar kinsa mata a abinci taci, inhar taci sau uku ke da kanki zaki ban labari. Maganar aljani kuma karki damu zan shirya komai, sai dai yau kudin aikin sai kin hada da irinta kwanaki, dan dole na mika sakon aljani zuwa cikin jikinki.�?
Tafahimci me yake nufi sosai dan sanda zata rufe bakin kowa akanta sai data bashi kanta ya isar da sakon aljani jikinta. Bata damu ba haka ta wuce cikin daki shima yabi bayanta…�?.

{Allah ka mana tsari da wannan bala’i da masifar, ace saboda buri duniya mutane basu dauki zina wani laifi ba.}

20🤾🏻‍♂�?
 Boka ko nace malam sai daya gama biyawa kansa bukata dan dana umarnin aljanun dayake amfani dasu ne akan inhar yanaso su cigaba da aiki dashi to fa sai dai ya dinga kusantar manyan mata, tsohon jini.
Itakam Hajiya mikewa tai dan dama bawai abinda ke gabanta ba kenan, burinta ta samu biyan bukatarta, kuma akan wannan zata iya aikata komai, in tace komai tofa lalai tana nufin komai.
Fitowa tai tana tafe tana tunanin yanda zata sa wannan 'yar iskar yarinyar taci abincinta, dan ta kula kallan kitso ashe takeyiwa rogo.

********
 Abangaren Ibty kuwa ganin sha biyu tayi yasa ta nufi kitchen dan daura abincin rana, gaba daya hankalinta nakan mahaifiyarta, ta rasa yanda zatai ta kirata a waya.
Dabara ce ta fado mata da sauri ta dau wayarta ta fara duba yanda ake boye number in za'a kira waya. Ai nan danan google ya nuno mata, cikin tsananin jin dadi ta saka sannan ta kira Umma, tana ringing tana addu'ar Allah yasa ta dauka. Harta katse ba'a dauka ba, jiki a sanyaye ta sake kiranta, yanzun ma harta cire rai akan baza'a daukaba dan harta sauke wayar daga kunnenta sai taji ance "Assalamu Alaikum!"
Cikin tsananin jin dadi da zumudi ta dauka tace "Ummana?"
 Aminu daya taso dan ya sanar da ita zai fita daga nan zai taho dasu Islam yaji muryarta da yanda take magana cikin farinciki.
Umma tace "Ibtisam? Naga number wayar a boye?"
Tana murmushi tace "Kullewa nai, Abba ne yace karna kira ni kuma hankalina ya kasa kwanciya."
Kalamanta sunyi tsananin ba Aminu mamaki, taya mahaifi zaice karta kira mahaifiyarta?
Bai gama dogon nazari ba yai kamar zai juya sai yaji tace "Umma komai na gida ya dawo daidai? Hankalina yaki kwanciya wlh."
 Dan juyowa yai ya kalli kofar kitchen din yana mamakin abinda yake ji. Umma tace "Ibtisam haryanzu ban huce na abinda kika aikata ba, ko tunowa nai zafinki nakeji, taya zaki bari abu ya rincabe haka? Kina sane da irin farin cikin da mukeyi na ganin munyi hadin auran nan?"
 Idanunta ne suka ciciko da kwalla sai jiyai tace "Wallahi Umma bansan abin zai kai haka ba, ban kuma san a dalilina abubuwa zasu rikice haka ba, dan Allah Umma ki yafemin ke da Yaya wallahi na kasa samun nutsuwa ina ganin laifin duk nawa ne."
 Jiki a sanyaye Aminu ya bar falan yana sake maimaita kalamanta, wayarsa kawai ya dauka ya tura mata sako akan yazo ya sanar da ita zai fita amma yaga tana waya.
 Mota ya shiga yai shiru yana nazarin abinda ke faruwa, tabbas wannan ya nuna a dalilinsa ne take cikin wannan halin, sannan saboda haka ne bata samun nutsuwa ko cikin barcinta.
 Bayan mota ya kalla inda littafanta suke ya dauko dan ya dawo dasu gaba tunda su Islam zasu zauna a bayan, gefen kujera ne yadan bigi hannunsa hakan yasa ya saki lidar, wasu daga cikin litattafan suka fito.
 Hannu yasa ya daukosu yana kokarin maidasu ciki nan wata takarda ta fado daga ciki, kamar karya bude sai kuma ya samu kansa dadan budewa. Kai ya girgiza da sauri ganin bai kamata ba yana kokarin maidawa ya rufe idanunsa suka kai mai rubutun dake jiki. "Ibtisam ki fito waje karfe 10 na dare, zan daukeki mu tafi sokoto.............."
Mamaki ne ya rufeshi matuka tare da wani zargi daya darsu a cikin ransa, wanene yakesan daukarta su tafi? Saurayinta kenan? Lalai wannan ya tabbatar mai akwai wanda take so akai karfin hali aka bashi saboda Baffansa yasan Abbanta.
 Ganin tunani suna neman yi mai yawa kawai yaja mota ya fita daga gidan, yayi mamaki sosai dan yana kokarin shan kwana ya hango Hajiya ta sauka daga kan adaidaita sahu, ina taje? Itada ba lafiya ba? Wucewa yai gida kai tsaye, yaje gidan ya tadda su Ummy sun fita dasu Islam da Baffa, Ainah ce kadai a gida.
 Juice ta kawomai bayan sun gaisa tace "Yaya ina Ibtis....." sai kuma tadan rufe baki tana dariya tace "Aunty Ibtisam."
 Kallanta yai yace "Tana nan lfy." Mikewa tai tana neman tashi taji yace "Kawarki kamar kawarki ce sosai ko?" Tambayar taso bata dariya yanda yai tambayat ta koma ta zauna tace "Yaya wace tambaya ce wannan?" 
Fuska a daure yace "Indai kin gane ba shikenan ba?"
Tace "Kawatace sosai yaya tun secondary school muke tare."
 Shiru yai yana tunanin ta yanda zaiyi tambayar ba tare da raini ba, yadanyi gyaran murya kafan yace "Ita batada wani saurayi ne ta amince ta auran?"
Gaban Ainah ne yadan fadi kadan badai Umar ya tari yayanta ba?"
Aminu yadan kalleta kadan ganin kamar tana neman tuno amsar da zata bashi ne yasa yai saurin cewa "Meyasa bata aureshi ba? Bayan tana sanshi?"
 Ainah tadanyi ajiyar zuciya tace "Data auri wannan ai wlh gwara ta zauna babu aure."
 Zarginsa daya ya tabbata kenan, akan tanada saurayin da take so, yadau juice yadan kurba yace "Dan mutum na talaka shikenan sai aki auransa?" Yai maganar cikin alamar bugar ciki, tai saurin girgiza hannu tace "Wallahi Yaya ba haka bane, sanda yake mata ne ya fara zama hauka, ya hanata sukuni, ya hanata rayuwa, makaranta wannan biyota yake fa? Saboda kishinsa na hauka."
 Dan idanunsa ya runtse kadan lalai ba shakka silarsa ce ta rabasu, da alama kuma saurayin na matukar santa.
 Kallan Ainah yai yace "In tasan yana santa haka meyasa bata sanar dani ba? Ko ta sanar da iyayenta?"
 "Tab! Yaya wlh ita Ibty ba sanshi take ba, a gidan su ya taso ya bi ya takurawa rayuwata kallo kadai in namiji ya mata wlh sai an kwaceshi a hannunsa, wannan Yaya ai ba so bane hauka ne, bakasan takura da masifar da ta shiga a dalilinsa bane, gashi ta kasa sanarda iyayenta saboda dan yayan babanta ne kuma a gidansu yake."
 Shiru yai kenan yaran daya auri Yayarta ne? Meke faruwa kenan?
 Kamar bazaiyi magana ba sai yace "Meyasa ya auri yayarya to?"
Nan Ainah ta sanar dashi abinda Ibty ta fada mata, sannan tace "Ni na bata shawara akan kartai magana kila ya hakura da ita ne, bansan me kuma ya faru ba harta aureka ba."
  Kallan da Umar ya makamai ya tuno da yanda yanayinsa yake, ya kuma tuno da fasamai glass din mota da akai, da kawota da akai ba tare da shiri ba, lalai Umar baisan ba Ibty aka bashi ba kenan sai bayan an daura auran, to amma a ina aka samu matsala? Haka ya zauna sam tunani ya hanashi sukuni, ya daura ya kwance, tabbas tunkararta da maganar shine saukinsa amma matsalar shi baisan ta ina zai fara tambayarta ba, haka ya zauna harsh Ummy suka dawo sannan ya kwaso su Islam suka taho.
