 Da sauri ta fito daga daki sanye da hijab da alama sallar la'asar tai, suna karasowa ta rungumesu, tace "Kawayena kun dawo?"
Islam cikin murna da tsale tace "Kawar Aunty zo kiga abinda Baffa ya sai mana" tai maganar tana jan hannunta.
 Ibty ta biyota tana dariya, Ilham tace "Kawar Aunty na dauka kin koma gida har inajin haushi bazamu nuna miki ba."
 Murmushin yake tai tace "Kwantar da hankalinki Ilham zan kwana biyu anan kafin na koma."
Falan Aminu suka nufa inda suka ga katan kwali guda biyu dauke da hoton keke, murmushi tai tace “Inyee lalai yau dole asha murna.�?
Kallan Aminu tai cikin girmamawa tace “Sannu da dawowa.�?
 Shiru yai yana kallanta, fuskarsa sauke da alamomin tambaya masu yawa.
 Nan fa aka fara murna suka zauna ana bubude kwalin, yana zaune yana kallansu.
 Kallanshi tai tace “Ya Aminu taimaka mana mu hada dan nikam na kasa.�?
 Kamar yace bazaiyi ba dan shi ya tsani a dinga kokarin saka shi abu, sai dai baisan dalili ba ya dan sa hannu ya jawo abin gabanshi yana hadawa.
 Mikewa tai ta fara kawo abinci shi kuma yana hadawa, sukam su Islam sun kafa sun tare suna jira a gama.
Ibty tana murmushi tace “Kofa an gama hadawa sai anci abinci sannan za’a fara hawa.�?
Islam ta shagwabe fuska tace “Dady ni yanzu nakesan hawa.�?
 Bai kalli inda take ba sai Islam daya kalla yace “In kunci abincin sai kuyi.�?
Taji dadi sosai na yadda yabi bayanta dan kallansa tai tare da sakin murmushi. Sai daya gama hadawa sannan suka zauna sukaci abinci tare, Islam sai da Aminu ya kalleta sannan ta daina zuba surutu.
 Ai tana gamawa ta ja kekenta ta fito harabar gida, nan suka fara kokarin yi ita kuma tana nuna musu yanda ake, ba abinda kake juyowa sai dariyar nishadi nasu Ilham da Islam.
 Hajiya dake zaune a bangarenta tana yin farfesun kaza wanda takeso ta gama ta zubawa Ibty magani a ciki, ta kai mata da sunan maganin mata. Yanda take juyo muryar yaran ne yasa ta fito waje, takaicinta daya babu hanyar dazata ga abinda ake ciki, itakam wannan katangar an cuceta da akayi ta, kunne ta kasa sosai tana juyo me suke cewa. Wasa suke sosai sosai tana juyo muryar Ibtisam, kwafa tai lalai yaran nan, duk abinda ta fada musu? Kitchen ta koma ta kafa gumbuda maganin a cikin kwanan farfesun data zuba mata, itadai tana fatan an tura mata aljanun da zasu rikitata dan tsanar yarinyar ta kara shiga ranta matuka.

 *******
 Bayan sallar magrib tana daki tana karatun qur’ani su kuma suna falo suna kallo, kwankwasa kofa taji ta mike ta fito gabanta na faduwa dan ta tabbatar Hajiya ce.
 Budewa tai bakinta dauke da addu’a, Hajiya tana tsaye sanye da dogon hijab dinta, su Islam suna jin muryarta suka taho da gudu suka rungumeta. Ibty ta daure tace “Hajiya ki shigo mana.�?
Kai ta girgiza tana murmushi tace “Islam ku shiga ciki zanyi magana da Aunty au da Mumynku.�?
Yanayinsu ne ya canza, Ibty tai saurin cewa “Aunty.�? Dariya sukai sannan suka koma ciki, kallanta Hajiya tai a ranta tace “Wato ke ‘yar duniya ko?�?
Sai kuma tai murmushi ta ciro dan karamin flask din dake cikin hijab dinta tace “Ina zaune nace bari dai na miki kazar amare, na tabbatar ku amaren yanzu baku damu da kanku ba, ga mijinki ba saurayi ba yana bukatar yajiki yanda ya kamata.�?
 Tsananin mamakin da Ibty ta shiga ya wuce duk tunanin mai sauraro, me matarnan take cewa? 
Hajiya ta dan riko hannunta ta mika mata tace “Daso samuna nane ma a gabana zaki cinye na kwashe kasusuwan na binne, a matsayin ‘yata na daukeki shiyasa nake san na miki duk abinda uwa ya kamata tai, ko dan taimakon da Aminu yamin dole ne kuma hakkinane na kula dake. Naga haryanzu bakizo kinga inda nake ba balle na baki acan shiyasa na taso.�?
 Kallan flask din tai tama rasa abin cewa, Hajiya tai murmushi tace “Kimin alkawari zaki cinye tas.�?
 Ta kalli kwanan sannan ta daga kai batare da tace komai ba, Hajiya tai murmushi sannan ta koma.
 Haka Ibty ta shigo da flask mamaki duk ya hanata sukuni, kitchen takai kawai ta ajiye sannan ta fito, dakinta ta koma ta zauna fuskarta ciki da mamakin wannan al’amari.
*****
Karfe takwas su Islam sukai baccic ta gyara falonta sannan ta wanke ragowar kwanuka, bude flask din tai sannan ta rufe, ita ko da ace ma akwai abinda ya shiga tsakaninsu da Aminu ai batasha maganin nan ba balle ta tabbatar Aminu babu so balle sha’awarta a tattare da shi.

 Karfe sha biyu Aminu na kokarin kwanciya yaga tana girgiza kai da karfi, da sauri ya karasa inda take yadan dagota kadan, yace “Me nene?�?

 A jikinsa ta ganta a kwance ya ririketa sosai, hawaye ne suka gangaro daga idanunta, idanta yayi jaa sosai tace “Ya Aminu wasu mutane ne ina gudu suna bina, bama mutane bane dan ban taba ganin irin hallitarsu ba.�?
 Tausayinta ne ya kamashi yace “Hala bakiyi addu’a ba kika kwanta?�?
 Tace “bansan bacci ya daukeni ba, inasan ka gama shan tea ne ko sai da safe na maka.�?
 Haryanzu tana kwance a jikinsa yana kallanta, can ya daure yace “Kin tabbatar ba mafarkin tsohon saurayin ki bane yake kokarin jefaki cikin wani hali?�?

 Kwayar idanunta ya kalla, wanda ke zubar da kwalla, kamar zai sake magana sai yaga kamar shi bai dace ya dameta da maganar ba, da alama ita kadai tasan halin da take ciki.
 Kwanciya yai tabi bayansa da kallo tana share kwallarta, kwanciya tai suka dan juyama junansu baya.
 Kamar wanda sukai bacci, itakam addu’a kawai takeyi, sai data gama ta shafe jikinta sannan tace “Kayi hakuri Yaya na boyema abu kafin ka aureni.�?

 “Kayi hakuri a dalilina na ina neman hanaka kwanciyar hankali.�?
 Yanzu kam juyowa yai ya kalli bayanta da take kwance da alama hawaye take dan yana jin muryarta tana dan rawa. 
“Kayi hakuri in har nice…�?..�?

21
 Kasa tai da idanunta cikin jin nauyin sa, a hankali shima ya zare hannunsa daya riketa dashi sannan yai dan gyaran murya yace “In ance miki yarinya kiji haushi.�?

  Tana sallar asuba tai karatunta harya dawo daga masallaci sannan ta fito daga dakin ta nufi dakinsu Islam ta tashesu ta musu wanka sannan ta dauko musu kayan makaranta ita kuma ta wuce kitchen tana harhada breakfast.

 Zama sukai sunaci ita kuma ta wuce toilet tai wanka, doguwr rigar atamfa ta saka bata tsaya kwalliya ba tasa turare sannan ta fito falo. Ta tarar sun gama, haka ta kwashe kwanukan takai kitchen, ta rakosu har waje hannunta rike da Islam da Ilham.

 Ta daga kai sannan ta dora da �? Eh jibi ne.�? 
“Allah ya kaimu.�?
A hankali tana murmushi tace “Ameen.�?
 Nan suka ja mota suka fita, da kallo ta bisu kunyar kanta ce ta kamata lalai fa yanzu ta zama matar gida, sai take ganin abin kamar wasa.
Aminu ta glass din mota ya kalleta ba shakka tanada hankali da tunani, ya tabbatar zamansu zaiyi dadi duk da ba santa yake ba, a kalla ya kula tasan yanda ake kula da gida tana kuma da dogon tunani.
 Ibty kam Ciki ta koma ta gyara gidan sannan ta mike akan kujera tana kallo.
 ****
A bangaren Hajiya kuwa jiya sam bata samu baccn kirki ba, haka kawai jikinta ya bata Ibty batasha farfesun nan ba, karin bakin cikinta kudin hannunta sun fara kasa, gashi jiya kwata kwata Aminu bai shigo ya dubata ba, a kalla yaci ace yazo yaga ko ta warke ai.
 Kwafa tai tace “Wato har karuwar nan ta fara dauke mai hankali?�?
Mikewa tai bayan taji fitar mota tahau yin faten dankali da wannan ragowar maganin, tana gamawa ta zuba a flask ta fito waje, gun mai gadi ta nufa suka gaisa ta mikamai ledar flask din tace “Gashi ka mikawa Amarya kace inji Ummy, kasan Mamman ya saba da bangarena can yakai.�?
Ya amsa ba tare da wani zargi ba ya wuce ciki, ciki ya shiga ya kwankwasa kofar gidan.
Ibty tana kwance har bacci yadan fara daukarta taji ana kwankwasa kofa, gabanta ne ya fadi badai matarnan bace? Mekewa tai jikinta nadan rawa ta nufi kofa, a hankali tace “Wanene?�?
Daga waje yace “Bala ne.�?
Wata nanauyiyar ajiyar zuciya tai sannan ta bude kofar, gaisheta yai sannan yace “Sakone Ummy ta aiko dashi da alama Mamman bangaren Hajiya yakai.�?
Kallan mamaki tadan mai sannan ta amsa tai godiya, ciki ta shiga ta wuce kitchen. Budewa tai taga fatan dankali, murmushi tai sannan ta kalli kular Hajiya ta jiya, batasan asarar abinci sai dai ko almajiri taba gani take batai imani ba, tunda har jikinta yabata akwai wani abin a ciki yanzu inta ba wani shi kuma wani abin ya sameshi fa?
 Kamar zataci dankalin sai kuma ta rufe tace “Naci anjima yanzu bari nadanyi bacci.�?
Komawa falo tai sannan ta kwanta, kankace me wani bacci yayi gaba da ita.
******
Karfe sha biyu ta tashi nan ta wuce kitchen ta fara girkin rana, karfe biyu Mamman ya dawo dasu.
Ta fito suka rungumeta cikin murna, har Mamman zai shiga mota tace “Mamman nagode da sako.�?
 Kallan mamaki ya mata yace “Sakon me Hajiya?�?
Tadan kalleshi tace “Sakon da Ummy ta baka ka kawo da safe.�?
 Kai ya girgiza da sauri yace “Ni kuwa? Ni ai banzo ba sai yanzu.�?
Gabanta ne ya dan fadi, tadan daure tace “Ayya! Kila mistake Bala yai.�?
Ciki ta koma sunacin abinci tana mamakin wannan abin, batasan dogon zargi sai dai babu tantama Hajiya ce kenan ta aiko mata da abincin, wato bayan na jiya ba shine ta aiko mata?
 Haka harsu Islam suka tafi makarantar Islamiya, hankalinta ya kasa kwanciya da wannan abin mamakin daya faru.
Hajiya kam ta rasa abinyi, gashi ta kira Malaminta ya duba yace haryanzu bataci ba.
 Waya ta dauka ta kira Aminu, yana dauka kawai ta sa kuka.
 Hankalinsa a tashe ya fara tambayarta abinda ke faruwa, kin magana tai sai kuka kawai da takeyi.
Bai tsaya wani abu ba ya nemi uzuri tare da fitowa daga gun aiki.
 Kai tsaye ya shiga bangarenta hankalinsa a tashe kai kace Ummy ce ke mai kiran gaggawa.
 Sallama yai daga ciki ta amsa cikin kuka, ya shiga ciki hankalinsa a taananin tashe. A zaune ya ganta ta gama cika akwati da kaya, ga kuma wani akwatin nan tana hadawa tana hawaye.
Taahin hankaline karara ya bayyana a idansa, yace “Hajiya meke faruwa?�?
Ta girgiza kai kawai tana hada kayanta, cikin kulawa da damuwa yace “Hajiya! Wani abin akace miki?�?
Kallansa tai tare da zama a bakin gadan tana share kwalla tace “Aminu wannan abin nake gudu tunda kai aure shiyasa na nemi barin gidan nan, yanzu kam dan Allah ka barni ko banda inda zanje zan fita daga nan gidan, Na’imah ta tafi ta barni banda kowa a duniya, yanda ta tafi nima fatana Allah ya dau raina.�?
 Tsananin tashin hankali ne ya bayyana a idansa, tuno sanda Na’imah zata rasu yai kalma daya take roko daga gareshi akan ya kula da mahaifiyarta.
 “Meya faru?�? Yai tambayar cikin kakkausar murya.
 Ta girgiza kai cikin damuwa tace “Aminu tunda kai aure matarka ta kwashe su Islam, na fahimci wannan dole ne inhar muna bukatar ta shaku dasu, banyi magana ba. Na kai muku abinci ta dawo dani tace bataso na dinga shiga abinda bai shafeni ba, girki da kula daku aikinta ne, nan ma ta dawo dani dauke da tulin abinci haka na dawo na sa abinci a gaba wanda rashin jin dadin yanda ta zubamin munanen kalamai ya sa ciwo na ya tashi, shima ban sanar dakai ba saboda banaso ku samu matsala, da daddare nai farfesu naga abin kwalama ne bansan na cinye ni kadai nakai muku shima ina kallo ta shiga dashi kitchen babu alamar taba su Islam, yanzu nayi faten dankali ganin su Islam naso na zuba musu a flask nan ma ta hanasu akan bata yarda da abinci na ba. Su Islam zasu Islamiya sun biyo na tambayesu sukace bata basu ba.�?
 Kuka tasa sosai, tace “Zan yarda na shanye komai amma banda kokarin rabani da jikokin da su kadai suka ragemin a duniya, Aminu kafin zaman nan ya koma na rashin dadi ka………�?
Yanda ya mike a fusace ne yasa ta fito itama tare da kallan turaren wutan data hada domin shi wanda yasha magani, tana kallo ya fita.
Kwafa tai tace “Kedin banza ke din wofi? Ni da kika gani bala’in dana shiga da yasa yanzu bana shakkar ko wani tsinane, ke din banza?�?

*******
Tana sallar la’asar taji alamun mutum a cikin dakinta, addu’arta ta shafa sannan ta juyo, cikin mamaki ta kalli Aminu dan bataji alamun dawowarsa ba.
Mikewa tai fuskarta a sake tace “Yaya Sannu da dawowa.�?
 Yanayin fuskarsa kadai zaka kalla kasan yana cikin bacin rai.
Sallaya ta ninke fuskarta dauke da damuwa tace “Yaya?�?
Fitowa yai daga dakin ba tare dayace mata komai ba, kitchen ya nufa kai tsaye ya fara bubude flask, can gefe ya hango flask din Hajiya, nan ya nufi gun ya bude, daya farfesu ne daya fatan dankali, babu alamar an taba dan farfesun ma harya fara tsami.
 Ibty kam kallansa take dan bata fahimci me yakeyi ba. Waigowa yai yace “Me yasa ba’aci wannan abincin ba har daya ya fara tsami?�?
 Ta rasa mema zatace, cikin dauriya da dan tsoron abinda zai je ya dawo tace “Yaya jiya da daddare aka kawoshi lokacin su Islam sunyi bacci ni kuma na koshi.�?
“Shine ba’asashi a fridge ba aka barshi a waje?�?
Shiru tai tana tunanin abinda zatace, itakam ta inda ake cutarta kenan, in rai mutum ya baci bata iya yin bayani ba, sai taji duk ta rikici.
 Takowa yai yazo inda take daf har tana iya juyo numfashinsa, wani irin faduwar gaba ya sameta akai akai.
“Na dauka na sanar dake matsayin Hajiya a gareni ko? Ko kina tunanin harkin kai matsayin da zaki shiga rayuwar gidana? Kina abu a hankali ina tunanin eh a kalla ko banasanki, zan iya zaman aure dake saboda bayan yarintarki kina da hankali da sanin abinda ya kamata.�?
Ya mata wani kallo yace “Sai dai me? Ashe kema daya kike da munafukan mata wadanda ke nuna Allah a waje shaidan a zuci, ke bakya ko tausayin baiwar Allar nan? In kin rabata da jikokinta me kika mora? Wannan wani irin bakin hali ne? Ko sati bakiyi a gida ba kina neman hanata ganin jikokinta?�?

 Dagowa tai cikin matukar mamaki da jin zafin kalamansa, a hankali wasu zafafan hawaye suka zubo idanunta, ta kasa ko magana sai hawaye kawai da takeyi.
“Ki tsaya a matsayinki inhar kinasan zama a gidan nan, sai yanzu na fara fahimtar dalilin dayasa saurayin can ya takura miki da alama yasan wasu daga cikin halayenki ne yake tsoro.�?
 Juyawa yai cikin matukar bacin rai, Ibty kam zubewa tai a kasa kawai tasa kuka.
 Haryakai karshen falan ya juyo ya kalleta, yanda take kuka yasa kasan ransa ya soso, meya sameshi? Duk da tayi laifi ya kamata ya zaunar da ita ya mata fada cikin sanyi, meyasa ya rufeta da fada haka?
 Yafi minti uku yana kallanta sannan ya wuce daki, kansa yadan dafa dake ciwo sannan ya wuce toilet ya shiga wanka. A wanka kam ruwa yai ta zubawa kansa yana jin haushin abinda yai, yanada zafi sai dai bai iya rufe mutum da fada haka ba, sannan wasu kalamai daya mata sun sa yaji haushin kansa.
 Ibty kam ta dade tana kuka kafin ta mike, abincinsa ta dauka takai mai, tana kokarin fitowa ya fito daga daki sanye da doguwar riga, idanunsu ne suka hadu da juna, idanunta duk sun kumbura.
 Kai yadan kawar da kansa sannan ya karaso, zama yai ta zubamai abinci sannan ta fito daga falan gaba daya jikinta ba karfi.
Haka yaci abincin gaba daya shima zuciyarsa ba dadi, komawa yai ya zauna yadan kunna tv.
Ibty ta dawo ta fara daukan kwanuka, sai datazo na karshe sannan ta kalleshi, haka kawai taji wasu hawayen sun zubo mata.
 *****
Gaba daya ba abinda ya sake hadasu harsu Islam sukai bacci, ji take kamar karta kwana a dakinsa sai dai alkawari ta dauka ba yanda zatai.
Dakinsa ta nufa bayan tayi wanka tasa kayan bacci, idanunta haryanzu a kumbure suke, sallama kawai tai sannan ta wuce ta kwanta.
 Dakin yayi shiru sai kara keyboard na laptop dinsa dake tashi, duk yanda taso tai bacci ta kasa saboda kalaman Aminu dake dawo mata.
 Juyowa tai saitinsa tana hawaye tace “Yaya a matsayinka na babba bai kamata ka fara bincika taba kafin ka dau hukunci akaina?�?
Jikinsa ne yai sanyi, sai dai baisan me zai ce ba. Ta dan share kwallarta tace “Bansan tsakaninku da Hajiya ba bankuma san shakuwarku ba sai dai a kalla nima a bani hakkina aji ta bakina, bansan laifin danai ba ka rufeni da fada.�?
 Kallanta yai yanzun ma baisan me zaice ba, juyawa tai kawai ta cigaba da share kwallarta.
 Hannu yasa ya dauko tissue din dake gefensa ya dan taba bayanta, juyowa tai ta kalleshi. Nan ya mika mata tissue din yace “Kukan ya isa haka nan.�?
Amsa tai ta dau biyu ta ajiye ragowar sannan ta share fuskarta.
Shiru tai can taji yace “Rainane ya baci bansan ya akai ba na rufeki da fada ba tare da jin ta bakinki ba, sai dai inaso ki sani banasan zuwanki ya zama silar rugujewar farincikin gidan nan, bance sai kinyi komai daidai ba but banasan ki takurawa tsohuwar da batada kowa sai ni, wacce batada lafiya take neman mai kula da ita.�?
 Shiru Ibty tai lalai itakam taga ta kanta, har tana murna zamansu ya fara dadi sun fara fahimtar juna yanzu shine matar nan zata fara yi mata sharri.
 Sunyi shiru jiki a sanyaye tace “Kayi hakuri�?
Harta dauka bazaiyi magana ba sai taji yace “Kema.�?
Haka ta kwana da bacin ran abinda matarnan ta aikata mata, gashi batasan yanda zatai ta huce takaicinta ba.

Aminu sam baccin ranar baimai dadi ba, ya rasa yanda zaiyi ya amshi laifinsa, da asuba kam mikewa tai kawai ta fito daga dakin dan ko sallah ba’a dakin tai ba. Yana dawowa daga masallaci ya dauka zai juyo karatunta yanda ya saba yaji shiru, dakin ya nufa bata ciki, hakan ya tabbatar mai bama anan tai sallah ba, nan ya zauna bakin gado tare da dan murza saman goshinsa, baisan meke damunsa ba, tabbas yasan bai kyauta mata ba sai dai baisan ta yanda zai bata hakuri ba.
 Bayan ta shirya yara ta kawo abinci, kallanta yai sanye take da riga da skirt na lace, ya mata kyau sosai “Ina kwana?�? Abinda kawai tace kenan ta zauna suna magana dasu Islam, yanayinta ya kalla, haka yaci abincin duk jikinsa a sanyaye, nan suka fito ta rakosu har mota sannan ta koma ciki. Da kallo yabita harta shiga ciki sannan yaja mota suka bar gidan.
 Ibty kam gyaran gida ta gama ta zauna tai shiru a falo tana tunanin kalaman Aminu, tabbas an dade ba’a fada mata munanen kalamai irin haka ba, hawayenta ta share, sannan kalaman Ummy suka fara dawo mata, lalai inhar ta bar abin nan batasan nan gaba me matarnan zatace ma Aminu akanta ba, “matsoraciya!�? Kalaman Aminu ne suka daki dodan kunnuwanta, kwanciya tai tana tunanin mafita, ganin damuwa da takaicin abin sunki barinta kawai ta mike ta nufi kitchen, kwanukan Hajiya ta kwaso ta zubar da abincin sannan ta wanke, awarar indomie datai da safe wanda yai ragowa ta zuba a daya daga cikin kular sannan ta dau leda ta zuba kulolin a ciki ta dau wayarta ta kira Aminu.
 Yana zaune yana duba wasu takardu yaga kiranta, cikin dan hanzari ya daga tare da yin gyaran murya, sallama tai yana amsawa tace “Zan shiga naba Hajiya hakuri akan abinda ya faru.�?
Cikin sanyin jiki yace “Karki damu komai ya wuce ai.�?
Kai tadan girgiza kamar yana ganinta tace “Yanda rankanya baci jiya ina ganin gwara na bata hakuri da kaina.�?
To kawai yace dan baisan abinda zaice ba, tunanin abinda zaice ya fara kawai yaji an kashe wayar, lalai yarinyarnan dan taga baiji dadin abinda yai ba shine take mai abinda taga dama?
 Ibty kam hijab dinta ta zura kawai ta fito, duk da tsoro da fargaba dake damunta sai dai wannan karan bazata bari a maidata wawiya ba, Umar ya koya mata hankali, ita kadai tasan wahalar datasha agunsa.
 Dayake gate dinta ba rufewa take ba, turawa kawai tai ya bude, bakin kofar shiga ta tsaya ta dade tana addu’oi kafin ta kwankwasa kofa.
 Hajiya tana zaune ta baje akan kujera tana kallan tashar arewa, gefe kuma ga cincin nan da fanta ta kwalba, tana jin wani nishadi, yanzu ma tunanin abinda ya kamata tayiwa Amarya takeyi, murmushi tai ganin ta gama hada abinda zata mata wanda ta tabbatar Aminu sai ya kusa marinta.
 Kwankwasa kofa taji, mikewa tai ta bude dan tayi tunanin almajirin datake aika ne, yanda ta zabura kadai ya tabbatarwa Ibty tayi mamakin ganinta.
 Cikin dauriya tace “Amarya?�?
 Ibty ta gaisheta, nan fa suka fara kallan juna, Hajiya ta daure tace “Shigo mana.�?
Sallama tai ta shiga ciki ta zauna a kasa, sannan ta sake gaisheta.
 Hajiya ta amsa tana dan yake ta amsa, Ibty ta miko mata ledar kwanuka tace “Dama kwanukanki na kawo miki, sai abin kwalama danai da safe.�?
Wani kallo ta mata, aranta tace “Badai nima maganin ta samin ba?�?
 Ganin yanda take kallan Ibty yasa tai dan kasa dakai tace “Kiyi hakuri bansan ranki ya baci ba, sannan jiya nidai Bala cemin yai dankalin daga gidan Ummy yake.�?

 Ibty tai murmushi tace “nima nayi tunanin haka, sannan farfesun mata da kika kawo abinda yasa bansha ba, banasan abubuwan mata daga aurena, a gida ma da aka bani bansha ba, amma tunda bakiji dadi ba kiyi hakuri.�?
Hajiya tadan canza murya tace “Aminu dai sai a barshi, nifa sam bancemai naji haushi ba, sannan ni danake gidana ta ina zansan baki ci ba, muna hira ne fa zancen ya ratsa.�?
Ibty kanta na kasa dan ta kula inta hada ido da ita bata iya maida murtani yanda ya kamata, tace “Allah sarki, amma dai duk da haka nasan ke kika mai zancen dan shi baima san kin kawo abincin ba, kinga maganin mata dama bai kamata ya sani ba, sannan na jiya kuma da aka kai ance Ummy balle yasan daga nan ne�?

Itama Ibty tai murmushi tace “Nayi tunanin haka dama dan nasan haka kawai bazaki hadamu fada dashi ba, tunda dai ke a matsayin uwa muka daukeki.�?
 Hajiya rasa abin cewa tai wato lalai yarinyar nan ba kanwar lasa bace, lalai sai ta tashi tsaye ta ci kwal uban yarinyar nan.
 Ibty ta mike tare sa yi mata sallama ta fito, da wata muguwar harara tabi Ibty, lalai wannan tsinaniyar yarinyar wuyanta yayi kwari.

  Umar ne ya kalli wani dalibin da ya fito daga makarantar yace “kenan sai wani satin za’a fara registration?�?

 “Bandawo ba, tunda aljani kika gani bani ba, ni wai dan Allah kwakwalwar dakikai gareki ko me? Wace irin ‘yar iskar tambayace wannan? Wai Ka dawo? Ban dawo ba ina waje.�? Yaja tsaki sannan ya zauna akan kujera ya dau remote ya kuna music, jiki a sanyaye ta hau kan kujerarta, batada lafiya tun jiya take fama, abinci ta daukomai ta ajiye a gabanshi.
 Wani kallo ya watsa mata yace “dan Allah inkin ajiye ki matsa daga nan, sam banasan ganinki.�?
A hankali taja kekenta ta wuce daki, ya kara zabga wani tsakin sannan ya lumshe idanunsa, fuskar Ibty yake hangowa tana mai murmushi, hannunsa ya dunkule ya dan naushi kujera, wallahi sai Allah ya sakamai, kuma dole ne ya dawo da Ibty gunsa.

*******
 Bayan yara sun tafi islamiya wajen karfe biyar da kwata Aminu ya dawo, hannunsa dauke da manyan ledoji na fruits, yaba Bala karamar ledar yace yakai bangaren Hajiya sannan ya wuce ciki.
 Tunda ya shiga yake juyo kamshin turare, lumshe ido yai dan tabbas yanasan kamshin nan, bangarenta ya nufa, bakowa a cikin falan, kitchen ya wuce ya ajiye ledar sannan yadan kalli dakinta.
 Kamar zai wuce sai kuma ya nufi dakin, sam a rayuwarsa bayasan shiga hakkin mutum shiyasa tun jiya abin ke damunsa.
 Knocking yai, ba’a amsa ba hakan yasa yadan tura kofar dakin, Ibty ta gama wanka kenan saboda jinin al’ada daya zo mata dazu, dataji kwankwasawa yasa ta daura towel ta fito tana mamakin har lokacin tashinsu Islam yayi ne?
 Fitowa tai sanye da towel din ta iya gwiwa, turus ta tsaya shima ya kalleta, haka kawai yau baisan dalili ba yaji wani abu yadan tsirgamai.
Ibty kamar zata koma toilet din sai kuma ta dake tace “Sannu da dawowa.�?
Gyaran murya yai sannan yace “Na shigo naji shiru ashe wanka kike.�?
 Kai ta daga kawai batace komai ba, juyawa yai yana tunanin da alama da saninta take mai wulakanci, tun jiya inya mata magana sainta dinga amsamai kamar bataso, ya dauka ya bata hakuri? Ita tace kayi hakuri shima yace kema. Wannan bai isheta ba take nufi ko me? Shifa ya tsani a dinga neman juyashi.
 Ibty kam kaya ta saka sannan ta danyi kwalliya sama sama, sai yanzu take fahimtar meyasa mata suke zama ‘yan duniya a gidajensu, wato wasu mazan sai su dinga nuna kallo ma baka ishesu ba, bawai so take ya sadu da ita a dole ba amma a kalla mijinka ya nuna maka kaima macece.
 Lalai ko saboda Hajiya dole ne ta shawo kan Aminu yasota, dan ta tabbatar da yana santa bazai rufeta da fada jiya ba tare da bincike ba.

23⛹🏻‍♀�?

Su Islam an dawo bayan sunci abinci sunyi sallah ta zauna dasu ta koya musu homework, suna zaune tana dubawa ko sunyi daidai ya leko falan, dan yana zaune a falanshi yaji shirunsu yayi yawa. Shine ya leko yaga ko harsun kwanta ne.
 Ganinta zaune tana dudubawa tana sake musu gyara yasa yai shiru tare da dan jingina da bango yana kallansu, shifa ko homework in sukace anbasu ce musu yake bashida lokaci suje suyi da kansu, shiyasa shi sam baisan ma me suke a skul ba, sai jarabawa tazo in aka kawo result basuyi kokari ba ya hausu da fada. Kallansu yake sosai cikin tsananin sha'awa abinda ya sake birgeshi sam babu alamar bacin rai a fuskarta in bash gane ba sake koya musu take.
 Jin kamar ana kallansu yasa tadan juyo, ganin tana kokarin juyowa yasa yai saurin dagawa daga jikin bangon yana neman wayancewa, hannayensa yadan harde tare dayin gyaran murya yace "ku cigaba, dama naji shiru ne na leko inga ko sun kwanta ne."
 Bata amsa ba sai dai idanunta na kanshi, haka kawai ta kasa yakice kalaman daya fada mata a ranta.
 Islam cikin jin dadi ta dau book dinta ta nufeshi tace "Dady kalla kagani, wannan ne kawai banyi daidai ba shima yanzun nan zan gyara." Tai maganar cikin tsananin farinciki.
Murmushi yai tare da shafan saman kanta yace "Lalai Aunty ta iya koyarwa ko?"
Kai ta daga cikin jin dadi, yace "wannan kokarin dole nayi kyauta, tunda akwai sauran time kafin ai bacci ko muje dominos?" Ai nan Ilham ma ta taso tana tsalle, suka hau murna suna eh Dady.
 Kallan Ibty yai yace "In a shirye kike muje?"
 Cikin mamaki tace "Harda ni?"
 Yace "Keda kikai koyarwa ai dole harda ke." 
Dan murmushi tai sannan ta mike ta shiga daki, fuskarta ta gyara ta daura dankwali sannan ta sa mayafi, kayan lefenta ta dauko wani akwati ta dinga duba turaruka, jin kamshin wani yafi wanda take amfani dashi yasa ta dauko ta fefesa, dan dole ko kadan kadan ne ta fara jawo hankalin mijinta kanta.
 Tare suka fito sun rike hannunta, bayan mota ta bude musu suka shiga sannan ta shiga gaba itama ta zauna. Kamshin turaren ta sosai yamai dadi, wanda harsai daya dan kalleta. Zama tai fuskarta haryanzu ba a sake kamar yanda ta saba ba, yana jinsu tanata hira dasu Islam shidai yai shiru dan ko wannan fitar saboda ita yayishi, yana fatan zai wanke laifinsa ta haka, shifa raini ne bayaso da alama ta kula yana neman bata hakuri ne shiyasa take dadaga mai kai.
 Sun isa ya kalleta bayan yayi parking ganin bata da alamar fitowa, kallansu Islam yai yace "Muje?"
Nan suka fito suna murna, Ibty kam datake jira yace suje kawai taga sun fara tafiya, badai ta wuce gona da iri ba? 
Fitowa tai itama tabi bayansu, ita datake neman karkato hankalinsa kanta in tun yanzu aka bangare ai ansamu matsala.
 A ciki sai daya fara order sannan ya juyo yace "Akwai abinda kikeso?" Murmushi tadanyi tace "Kome aka siya yayi." A zahiri bata taba zuwa shagon ba, batasan ya abinsu yake ba, kartaje garin salo ta kwafsa shiyasa ma bata soma iyayi ba, dan Abba haka kawai bazai kawo su nan ya kashe kudinsa ba.
 Sai da aka gama hada musu suka hada da ice cream sannan suka fito, inka gansu abin sha'awa, haka suka shiga mota suka koma gida.

Hajiya kam tunda taji alamun fitar motarsu take leke ta gate, ina suka je bayan magriban nan? Bakin cikin duniya ya isheta da ta dauka shi kadai ne ya fita sai dai data tambayi Bala yace mata dukansu ne, sai suntiri take a tsakar gidan, jitai gwiwowinta sun fara amsawa, jingina tai da jikin gate din tana dan sake lekawa, jin alamun Bala zai bude gate yasa ta dan leko kanta tana lekensu. 
 Ibty dake zaune gaban mota ne idanunta yakai jikin gate din, mamaki ne ya kamata ganin ta hango kan mutum yana leke saboda fitilar motar daya kuna. Lalai matarnan takai makura gun sa ido, kauda kai tai kamar bata ganta ba ta juyo tanawa su Islam magana. 
Hajiya ta koma ciki tai kwafa cikin tsananin kunar rai, wato Aminu baiji abinda tacemai ba kenan ko? 24
 Ganin yayi shiru yasa tai shiru tana tunani, juyawa tai cikin ranta tana cewa “In har na danne tsoron tsohuwar nan dan me zan zauna Umar na ban tsoro?�? Ran Juma’a suna tafiya school ta hau aikin gida tai wanka ta dauko wani dankakareren lace mai tsada ta saka, dinkin an mata shi mai kyau dan asali dama shi ta ajiye zata saka washe garin daurin aure to sai kuma aka samu matsala, tayi kwalliya sosai dan dama ba laifi ta iya kwalliya, Ibty ta fito das da ita kai kace ranar auranta ne, turaren wuta ta kunna wanda ya kara kama kayanta.

 Ajiyemai tai a dinning sannan ta nufi dakinsa dan sanar dashi, ya cire rigarsa kenan tai sallama, juyowa yai ya kalleta, ta dauka zai maida rigar sai taga ya karasa cire ta daga jikinsa tare da kallanta. Ibty tai kasa dakai tare da yin shiru tana dan wasa da hannunta.
 Sam batasan ya tako ba sai ganin kafafunsa tai yana nufo ta, gabanta ne yadan fadi kadan bata matsa ba haka kuma bata dago ba, daf ya matso da ita tana juyo numfashinsa, hannayenta tadan matse saboda yanda ta tsinci kanta, idanunta ta lumshe dan ta dauka rumgumeta zaiyi, ganin jikinsu ya hadu da juna, hannu yasa bayan ya matsota suna gogan juna, kofar data bari a bude yasa hannu ya rufe, sannan yadan kalleta, gefen kunnenta ya matso yace “A dinga rufe kofa saboda akwai yara a gidan, in kika shigo kika manta dasu su kuma suka taho nan fa?  Haka ya fito yasa yadi fari sun mai kyau sosai sannan ya fito falo. A waje ya jiyo muryarsu da alama su Ilham ana keke ne, zama yai ya bude abincin, kamshin butter ne ya dan bigi hancinsa, vegetable soup ne wanda akai amfani da butter a maimakon mai, abinci yasa yana ci yana dan murmushi dan yana juyo hirarsu daga waje. Wata ajiyar zuciya ta bakin ciki ta saki sanda taji ana bude gate din gidan. Mamaki ne ya kamata dataji kwankwasa kofa yanzu, kafin ta mike har tajuyo murya da gudunsu Ilham.
 Da sauri ta bude kofar ashe ba tare zasu fita ba? Da alama kawo mata su zaiyi ya fita. Kofa ta bude da sauri tana dariya tare da tafa hannu.
 Da gudu suka karaso suka rungumeta, dadi ne ya kamata. Jin alamun bude kofa yasa tadan dago, Aminu ne ya shigo, haba wani irin dadi ya sake rufeta, ta bude baki zatai magana kawai taga Ibty ta biyo bayanshi.
Yanayin fuskarta ne ya canza lokaci daya, daurewa tai ta fara lale lale.

Ko ubanwa ya tambayeta oho. Da karfi ta makonkofa sannan ta koma ciki, zama tai ta baza tagumi tana tunani kodai ta canza malami ne?  Sake kokarin zare jikinsa ya sakeyi cikin sanda kamar irin munafikin nan, dakyar ya samu yadan janye hannunsa daga jikinta, yana kokarin zare gangan jikinsa ne itama tadan bude idanunta, da yake yadan kunna fitilar wayarsa yana san ganin yanda zaiyi dabarar janyeta. Nan fa idanunsu suka hadu da juna, cikin sauri ta nemi mikewa ganin yanayin da suke, shima cikin sauri ya nemi janye jikinsa ji kake kum sun mauje gushinsu, cikin jin azaba ta saki dan kara, da sauri yasa hannu ya toshe mata baku, idanunta ta sadda kasa tana neman mulmula gun dan taji zafi. Shikam idanu yadan runtse kadan, bayan ciwon daya ji mata yau yanzu ma ya sake wani. 
Daurewa yai cikin maganar rada yace “Yi mana uzuri da ihun nan kar Iman taji.�?
Kai tadan jinjina, sake bakinta yai a hankali taji yace “Sorry!�?
Kallansa tai harya mike yai toilet, sannan ta hau shishitu tana sosa gun, garin ya suka kwana a haka?
Sai kuma ta rufe fuskarta da sauri tuno yanda jiya ya dinga sarrafata lalai jiya ya manta da shekarunta, dan san girma irin na Ya Aminu da yai yawa.
Nan tadan ja bargo ta shige ciki ita kanta kunyar abinda ya faru jiya takeyi.
Shikam a toilet bayan yayi alwala tsayawa yai yana dan sauke iska, dolene ya daure fuska ya nuna kamar wani abu bai faru jiya ba. Sai dai anya zai iya? Dan tabbas a yanda yake jinsa baiki yanzu ya jawota jikinsa ba.
 Kai ya girgiza da sauri tare da sake zuba ruwa a fuskarsa lalai Aminu, wai kaine ka koma haka? Sa’ar Ainah ce fa? Banda girman daka zubar jiya yanzun ma neman zubar da wani girman kake?
 Haka ya d’aure fuska ya fito daga toilet, ko kallan kan gadan baiyi ba ya fito daga dakin, itakam sai dataji alamun rufe kofarsa sannan ta fito daga cikin bargon tare da yin murmushi da kyar ta sauko daga kan gadan, ta dade tana sake yin tsarki da ruwan dumi dan haryanzu zafi gun yake mata.

Juyowa yai yadanbi bayanta da kallo, ganin yanda take tafiya yasa kunya ta sake rufeshi, idanunsa ya maida ya rufe, shidai kam tabbas ya tabbatar ciwo yaji mata dan Na’imah ba abinda tai, ko jinin da ake cewa anayi ita batai ba, daya mata magana tace ai ba kowace mace keye ba, sannan ya tafi da ita cikin soyayya ne shiyasa wai duk wacce tai jini to karfi aka mata.  Iman tana dawowa daga school ko zama batai ba ta ja Ibty cikin dakinta, a bakin gado suka zauna ta kalli Ibty cikin kulawa tace "Yaya dama matarnan a jikin gidanki take?"
Yanayin fuskarta ne ya canza ta danyi murmushin yake batace komai ba, "hauka kike Ya Ibty? Taya zaki bar matarnan a kusa dake haka? In taimako yake san mata ya nemar mata gida a wani gun mana dole sai daf dake haka?"
Shiru Ibty tai dan itakam batasan me zatace ba, hannu Iman tasa tadan riko hannunta tace "Ki cire wannan sanyin naki ki kwaci mijinki, wlh in bakiyi wasa ba sai tasa an sakeki, yanzu yau zamu tafi ta ba Ya Aminu abu a flask, kina zaune a gida tana doramai abu, kinsan da wace zuciyar takeyi?"

Mikewa Ibty tai tace "To ya kikeso nai? So kike inje ince ya koreta ko me?"
Iman ta mike itama tace "Wai ke dan Allah kamar wata mara wayau? Ki ja Ya Aminu a jiki yanda zai manta da komai sai ke, daga sanda ya mato a kanki kisamu ya canza mata gida, shikenan."

Baki ta bude tace "Tashi ki fita! Lalai karatun novel yasa gaba daya kinzama fitsarariya."

Tausayinta ne ya kamata tace "Allah dai ya miki albarka, nasa anata sauka insha Allahu duk wani kadarin matarnan ya kusa zuwa karshe, kedai ki kara hakuri ki kuma rungumi mijinki."

Ummy dake kokarin zama ta hango Ibty na tahowa, cikin kulawa ta mike da sauri, Ainah ta dan rike Ibty dan tafiyarta kadai zaka kalla kasan ba karfi a jikinta, tana kokarin zama a kasa Ummy tace “Zauna kan kujera�? nan Ainah ta zaunar da ita, karasowa tai ta zauna kusa da ita tare da taba jikinta, cikin tsananin damuwa wanda ya bayyana kiri-kiri a fuskarta tace “Ibtisam? Kinsha magani kuwa? Zazzabi haka?�?
 Dan gyara zamanta tai tana tattaro dauriyarta tace “Nasha Ummy! Sannan bawai sosai yake wahalar dani ba.�?
 Sallamar Aminu ce ta dan katseta dan Islam yanzu ta sanar dashi zuwan su Ummy, yana daki baiji ba.
 Fuskarsa dauke da fara’a sosai yace “Ummy! Irin wannan zuwa ba sanarwa?�?
Kallansa tai tadanyi murmushi tace “Ba dole nazo na dubiya ba, banmasan zazzabin ya kamata haka ba.�?
Bai kawo komai ba yace “Wani ne ba lafiya?�?
Yai tambayar yana kallan mutanen falan, Ummy ta daure fuska sosai, mamaki ya rufe kowa na falan banda Ibty da batama dago ba, Ainah ta kalla tace “Keda Ummy ku shiga dasu daki.�?
Nan yaga anja su Islam cikin mamaki ya karaso ya zauna yana gaisheta tare da cewa “Wanene ba lafiya?�? Ya karasa maganar yana kallan Ibty, da sauri ya dauke idansa dan sam ji yai bayasan ko ganinta.
 “Kanasan cemin bakasan matarka batada lafiya ba kenan ko me?�?
Kallan Ibty ya sakeyi da sauri, nan fa wata kiyayga ta sake ruruwa yai murmushin takaici yace “Karya kika koma kuma? Ko kuwa neman hadani da iyayena kike?�?
Mamakin kalamansa ne ya rufe Ummy baki a bude tace “Mene?�?
Ya kalli Ummy rai a bace yace “Banda munafurci Ummy ina zaune da ita a gida bata fadamin batada lafiya ba amma gashi can tana sanar dake batada lafiya?�?
“Munafurci? Aminu? Kanka daya kuwa? Kai dana sani baka fadar munanan kalamai yau hankalinka daya kake abu haka?�?
 Wani banzan kallonya mata ya kalli Ummy tare da dafa goshinsa yace “Ummy yarinyarnan ta isheni wlh, shiyasa tun farko nace muku banasan auren nan, sam ko ganinta banasanyi.�?
Gaban Ummy ne ya fadi, tabbas Aminu ba kalau yake ba, nan fa ta fara Inalilahi wa ina ilaihi raji’un a ranta, anya batai sake ba?
 Cikin dakewa tace “Fadamin menene laifinta?�?
“Batada kunya sam, gashi ta iya musu bazaka taba fada mata abu tace to ba, sannan ga karya na kamata tana abu amma sam tabure batasan zance ba.�?
 Ba shakka Aminu ba’a hayyacinsa yake ba, uta ta haifeshi a cikinta ko a mafarki tasan idansa bazai rufe ya dinga kushe mutum haka ba.
 Kallan Ibty tai wacce hawaye kawai ke gudu akan kuncinta, tsananin tausayinta ya kamata. Maida idanunta kan Aminu tai tace “Naji na kuma fahimta yanzu fadamin kalma daya datai na rashin kunya?�?
Shiru yai da alama wai tunani yakeyi, sai can yace “Batadai da kunya Ummy, tamin rashin kunya ta kuma yiwa Hajiya, in ni ta rainani ai ita uwa ce.�?
 Idanunta tadan runtse, ta fara addu’oi a ranta tace “To yau yau cikin daren nan matarnan zata bar gidan nan.�?
Fuskarsa dauke da murmushi yace “Gwara ta koma gidansu inda wannan mahaukacin dake jiranta yake.�?
Dagowa Ibty tai da sauri tana hawaye rabuwar aure ko wata da aure ba’ai ba? Itakam ta shiga uku.
“Ba matarka nake nufi ba wacce bakada hadi da ita nake nufi. Yau ko me zai faru kafata kafarta a gidan nan.�?
Wani dan zabura yai yace “Wai Hajiya ce zata bar gidan? Ummy sai yaushe zaki fahimci matarnan amana ce a gareni?�?
Yanayin fuskar Ummy ne ya canza zuwa damuwa sosai, da alama zurfin ciki gareta tunda har take kokarin boye abinda ke faruwa.
Tai wani banzan dariya tace “Amana? Ta wuce ni dana dauki cikinka na wata bakwai na kuma haifa?�?
Fuskarsa a hade sosai yace “Saboda wannan ta zugaki yasa kika fadi haka Ummy?�?
“Ya Allah! Na rokeka ka kwatomin d’ana daga wannan bala’in.�?
A fusace Ummy ta mike ta fito daga falan, biyota yai da sauri yana bata hakuri akan kar ta kore Hajiya, ai bata tsaya ta kanshi ba tana tafe tana addu’oi akan yau Allah ya bata nasara. Kai tsaye ta bude kofa ta nufi ciki tana bubuga koga.
Aminu na kusa da ita yana bata hakuri, Hajiya daga ciki duniya ta mata dadi dan tsire ma ta gama ci tasha yoghurt tana kallo a tv. 
Tana bude kofa sukai ido hudu da Ummy, hankalinta ne yai mugun tashi gashi batasa kwallinta ba wanda ke hana Ummy yi mata musu.
 Ummy ta cusa kai cikin falan, kallan ledar dake kan centre table tai ta danyi murmushi tace “Duniya ta miki dadi, sai dai am sorry to say yanzu zata juya miki baya.�?
Hajiya ta dake tace “Nikam Asma’u mena tsare miki a rayuwa? A iya sanina ban miki komai ba, Aminu shiyasa a koda yaushe nake ce maka mahaifiyarka batasan zamana a nan……�? ta karasa maganar tana wani matsar kwalla, aifa nan hankalin Aminu ya tashi ya kalli Ummy yace “Ummy wai dan Allah meyasa kike haka? Ga wacce ya kamata kiwa fada can kinzo kina yiwa Hajiya? Menene laifinta?�?
“Laifinta kake tambaya? Ina uwar arziki zata zauna a karkashin mijin ‘yarta? Bayan rasuwarta? Ki tashi tun muna mu biyu ki tattara kayanki mu wuce dake goben nan ki tarkata ki koma kauyenku, gidan da nake neman a sai miki ma yanzu na fasa, tunda babban burinki a rayuwa bai wuce ki hana Aminu zama cikin kwanciyar hankali da matarsa ba.�?
Kuka ta fashe dashi tace “Aminu na taba magana akan matarka? Nikam bazan iya wannan bakin cikin ba ka barni kawai na tafi.�?
Aminu yakai karshe gun bacin rai, yace “Inhar Hajiya tabar gidan nan to itama yarinyarcan yau auranta xai mutu zata koma gidan su.�?
Mamaki ne ya rufe Ummy tace “Ni kake fadawa haka Aminu?�?
Ya dan kauda kai yace “Ummy na kasa gane meke damunki, akan me zaki dinga bin bayan yarinyarcan kina wulakanta Hajiya?�?
Bacin rai ne karara a fuskar Ummy tace “Ni kakewa magana haka?�?
Shiru yai baice komai ba, Ummy tace “To sai ta bar gidan nan yau, Ibtisam ni na kawota ko? To zan tafi da ita yanzun nan.�?
Hajiya ta dauka zataji Ummy ta sauko sai dai jin kalamanta yasa ta dago a zabure, ya zatayi tasa kwallinta? Wannan tsinaniyar mata me take shirin yi mata tana murna komai ya fara tafiya daidai?
 Aminu ya zubawa Ummy ido yace “Wai Ummy…�?.�?
Hannu ta dagamai, yau kam sai godiyar Allah bata taba tunanin zata samu damar magana haka ba, dan haka dole ta tabbatar matarnan ta fita daga gidan nan kafin ta kasa komai.
“Idan ka sake magana wallahi sai ka fuskanci bacin raina. Malama ke kuma tashi ko? Muje zan biya miki kudin hotel ki kwana yau gobe ki dau hanya. Sannan kar kiyi tunanin  dawowa gidan nan domin a goben nan zan nemo masu zama a ciki koda kuwa kyauta zan basu gun zama.�?
 Gaba daya Hajiya ta sususuce, zubewa tai a kasa ta dan hade hannaye tace “Asma’u naji koma menene zanyi amma ki bari sai zuwa gobe yanzu dare yayi.�?
Ummy tai wani banzan murmushi tace “Dame dame kikayi kafin ki cimma burin raba Aminu da matarsa?�?
Hajiya tadan dafe kirji tace “Wani saban sharri ne wannan? Nikam mena miki kike min kazafi haka?�?
Ibty dake waje dan ta biyosu ganin maganar ta dau zafi yasa ta tsaya a waje, jin kalaman Ummy yasa hankalinta yakai kunun da akaba Aminu, tabbas daga shan kunun nan ne ya fara canzawa, lalai matarnan bala’i ce.
 Aminu ya dan dafa goshi wata tsanar Ibty na sake tasomai, tabbas komai saboda ita ne yake faruwa.
“Koma me kikai Allah na tare damu, yanzu tashi ki hada kayanki, na tabbatar kinada magungunan da kikesan boyewa in kuma kin ban dama inje ni in hada miki kayan.�?
Hajiya ta kalli Aminu, tace “Aminu?�?
Kallan Ummy yai wacce ta nunamai yatsa tare da tamke fuska sosai tace “Wallahi ka kuskura kasa baki ka shirya daina amsa gaisuwata har nabar duniya.�?
Idanunsa ya runtse baice komai ba sai kawar dakai dayai kawai ya fito daga gidan cikin kumar zuciya.
 A waje yaga Ibty ta dan hade hannayenta biyu alamar tana addu’a, kallansa tai tare da sauke hannu, matsowa yai ya sa karfi ya riko hannunta sosai, zafin matsar taji idanunta na hawaye tace “Yaya hannuna.�?
“Hannunki? Ni kinsan halin da kika jefani? Ke ko kunyar munafircin da kika hada bakiji ba?�?
Ta daure tana dan motsi da hannunta dake zafi tace “Komai laifinane kenan?�?
Yace “Yanzu fadamin laifin waye? Haka kawai ina zaune zaman zamana daga auranki kina neman ruguzamin farinciki.�?
“Ka tabbatar da can a farinciki kake?�? Tambayar datamai kenan ita kanta batasan dalili ba.
Jikin bango ya dan karasa da ita haryanzu hannunsa na rike da nata, idanunsa sunyi jaa sosai yace “Me kike nufi?�?
Kai tadan girgiza tadan sauke kai kasa saboda azabar ciwo da kanta yakeyi, dago habarta yai da karfi yace “Tambayarki nake?�?
Tace “Na tabbatar sanda na aureka ba fara’a a fuskarka, ba jin dadi na kwanciyar hankali, aiki kawai kake sai kula da abinda ya zama dole.�?
 Yarfe hannunsa yai yace “Yanzu fa? A kallanda ina aiki cikin kwanciyar hankali yanzu fa? Kin ruguzamin rayuwa.�?
 Idanu tadan runtse kadan tace “Daga baya zaka fahimci ban taba neman ruguza maka rayuwa ba.�?
 Sauke hannunta yai cikin karfi ya juya ya fita daga gidan, a waje ya tsaya ya kira Baffa ya sanar dashi abinda ke faruwa yana rokarsa akan yawa Ummy magana, Baffa yace “Shi dakanshi zai karaso a tattara Hajiya dan ya tabbatar Ummy tanada kwakwaran hujja.�?
Jin wannan kalamai yasa zuciyarsa ta sake tafasa, cikin bacin rai ya shiga gida ya bude motarsa ya zauna yana tunanin abinyi.
Ummy kam tana tsaye akan Hajiya duk yanda Hajiya taso ta sa kwalli ta kasa, haka ta hada kaya da niyyar wallahi goben nan zata dawo dan bataga mai korarta ba.
 Haka Ummy ta sata gaba suka fito, Hajiya yau kam ji take wannan ranar tafi kowace rana bakin ciki, lalai in ta tashi yiwa Ummy magana wanda yafi ko wane muni zata aikata mata.
****
Suna fitowa waje Ummy taga Ibtisam, Hajiya ta kalleta wato itace ta sanar da Ummy abinda ya faru kenan? Wani murmushi tai a ranta tace “Kema ki shirya ganin bala’i da masifa.�?
 A waje sukaga Aminu da mota, Ummy ta kalleshi tace “Ina zaka?�?
“Zan kai Hajiya hotel din dazata kwana.�?
Tai wani murmushi tace “Bansaka ba ni da kaina zan kaita, kace Mamman da Ainah su fito mu tafi.�?
Takaici ne ya kara kamashi ya zabgawa Ibty wani kallo yace “Ita kuma yaushe zata barmin gida?�?
Wannan karka damu, zan zo na dauke abata goben nan, ai dai bakabar yaranka ba kowa bako? Xan zo na dauketa na kuma dauke jikokina kai sai kai zamanka kai kadai.�?
 Ciki ya juya cikin kunar rai, Ummy ta kalli Ibty tadan dafa kafadarta a hankali tace “Karki damu, yau ranar farinciki ce a rayuwata sannan ba wanda zai rabaki da gidanki.�?
Ta kalli Hajiya tace “Duk tsubun mutum Allah yana tare damu, kuma ke da Aminu mutu ka raba, kafin wani abu ya faru insha Allahu sharrin wacan mara imani da tsoron Allahn ya karye, kedai kiyi kokarin ci gaba da addu’a sannan kija mijiki a jiki.�?
Kanta ta sunkunyar tare da daga kai, cikin sanyin murya tace “Ummy sai yanzu na tuna tunda ta bashi kunu ya tafi office dashi ya canza halayensa.�?
Murmushin takaici tai tace “Ai ni ina kallan d’ana nasan ba kalau yake ba, Aminu ko ya tsani mutum baya nunawa kiri kiri haka.�?
Kallan Hajiya tai tace “Yau ko zaki mutu kin bar gidan nan kenan, addu’ar shekara biyar bata fadi ba.�?
Hajiya ta kallesu zuciyarta na wani tunzura, lalai ko zata koma a sake kusantarta koma ta ina ne sai taga bayan mutanen nan guda biyu.

36
 Hajiya gaba daya ta kasa yin wani, duka ya kamata tawa Ummy ta koma ciki? In tai haka kuma ai ta kara lalata alakarta da Aminu, ‘yar iskan yarinyarcan wai harta samu baki tana fadawa Ummy ta ba Akinu abu, lalai dole ta tozarta yarinyar nan.
Ummy ce ta kalleta tace “Ke banda ma dai ba kunya a harkarki ki hau adaidaita ki bar nan mana? Ko gidan d’anki ne?�?
Hajiya ta kafe Ummy da ido tana mata wani kallo, Ummy tai dan karamin tsaki tace “Yau baki samu damar yimin maganin ba? Ko kuwa wanda kikai da ne ya karye? 
Hajiya takai makura, Ummy ta kalli Ibtisam tace “Ki koma ciki ki kwanta a gidan mijinki, gidanki, in zaki wuce kicewa Bala yazo ya siyomin kwado dan dakaina zan rufe gidan da kuma kaina zan tafi da makulin saboda irin wadannan mutanen da basa tsoron Allah bakasan me zasu aikata ba.�?
Ibtisam duk da ciwon dake damunta hamdalla da sake addu’a kawai take akan Allah yasa matarnan tabar gidan kenan har abada.
Tana kokarin wucewa taji an jawo rigarta ta baya iya karfi, wanda yasata yin kakari.
 Ummy ta kalli Hajiya wacce ta jawo Ibty gabanta kafin suyi wani yunkuri Hajiya ta tsinkawa Ibtisam wani gigitacen mari, da alama neman inda zata huce takeyi dama.
 Ummy a fusace ta matso ta rike hannunta data rike rigar Ibty tace “Malama kanki daya kuwa?�?
 Hajiya ta daga hannu tana neman sake zuba mata Ummy ta hankadeta da karfi, Ibty mamaki ya rufeta ga wani azabar zafi da kuncinta yakeyi, ai Hajiya kamar jira take nan fa ta balle, “Allah idan har yarinyarnan bata bani hakurin abinda ta aikatamin ba ta shirya fuskantar bala’i da masifa.�?
Ummy ta bude baki zatai magana kawai taji Ibtisam tace “Allah na tare dani, kuma duk wanda ke neman d’an uwanshi da sharri to ya shirya fuskantar wannan sharrin a kansa.�?
Baki Hajiya ta bude tace “Ni kike fadawa haka?�? Ta nufo Ibty kamar wata bijima, kofar gate aka bude nan suka maida kallansu kan kofa, me zasu juyo su gani? Hajiya ce yashe a kasa tana kuka.
Ummy ta kalleta ta kauda kai dan ko batai magana ba tasan sharri take shirin yi, Ibty kam mamaki ne ya rufeta tace “Lafiya?�?
Ai Hajiya bata kulata ba sai kuka kawai da take tana sa hannu akai, Aminu cikin sauri ya nufi har yana neman yin tuntube, ya karaso yace “Hajiya? Meke faruwa?�?
Ta girgiza kai tana kuka tace “Aminu matarka da mahaifiyarka ne suka hadu suka dakeni, daga nace da Na’imah na duniyar nan na tabbatar baza’a tozartani haka ba, Aminu banda kowa a duniyar nan sai kai to dama Na’imah ce ta hadamu gashi yau sun ra…�?..�? ta kwace da kuka, ai Aminu a zabure ya dago yana neman shararawa Ibty wani marin, cikin tsawa yaji ance “Kana sauke hannu a fuskar nan wallahi zai kayi dana sani mai karfi.�?
 Ibtisam ta kalleshi kawai ta juya ta wuce tare da sanar da Bala sakon Ummy na kwado, nan ya taho ya amshi kudi ya wuce siyowa.
Aminu nan fa ya tsuguna yana lalashi Hajiya ita Ummy abin ma dariya yaso bata, suna nan tsaye itadai Ainah tana tsaye tana kallan ikon Allah.
Bala ne ya dawo da kwado, Ummy da kanta ta datse gidan tasa kwado daya a ciki daya a gate, sannan ta kalli Hajiya tace “Ko mantuwa kikai ki sanar dani zan zo na aika miki kauyenku.
 Suna kokarin shiga mota sai ga Baffa ya iso, da sauri Aminu ya karasa kamar wani zarare yana rokar Baffa akan ya taimaka ya ba Ummy hakuri, ai Baffa hannu kawai ya dagamai bai tsaya mai magana ba ya nufi gun Ummy.
 Murmushi tai tace “Yallabai an taso ka?�?
Ya kalli Hajiya yace “Ina zata da daren nan?�?
Kafada ta daga tace “Dama hotel nake tunanin nema mata ta kwana gobe ta tattara ta nufi inda ya dace.�?
Kai Baffa ya daga yace “Hakan yayi, mu wuce?�?
Ta girgiza kai alamar a’a tace “Ina bukatar mu wuce dasu Islam ne daga gida su dinga zuwa makaranta, na sati biyu, sannan inda hali Ainah kicewa kanwar Ibtisam ta biyomu mu wuce tare gobe zan sa a maidata gida.
Baffa bai nemi jin dalilinta ba dan ya tabbatar tanada hujja, haka suna nan a tsaye Hajiya tun tana kokan karya har ta fara zuge jaka tana neman kwalli ko turare.
Hankalin Ummy ne yakai kan abinda take, Ummy ta kalli Ainah tace “Kwashe kayan kisa a bayan mota.
Hajiya ji take kamar ta shake Ummy dan takaici, haka suna tsaye aka kwashe suka jira har aka hado kayan su Islam itama Iman ta hado nata suka shiga motar Baffa, ita kuma Ummy daga ita sai Hajiya suka shiga motar Mamman.
Haka Ibty na jikin gate har suka wuce, addu’a ta sakeyi sannan ta kalli Aminu wanda ya dafa goshinsa gaba daya kana kallansa kasan yau ankaishi makura gashi bashi da wata mafita.
Waigowa yai fitilin gaban gidan suka haske mai Ibtisam wata irin tsana ta kara kamashi, juyawa tai ta koma ciki ba tare da ta tankamai ba.
 Shigowa yai a fusace, yana neman tarar mata, kafin ya karaso ta wuce falanta, nan ya nufi falanta yana neman ta ganin batanan ya nufi daki, yana hankade kofa ya ganta zaune da Al-qur’ani tana karatu, to kodai bashi da imani ai bazai je ya finciko ta tana karatu ba, ya kuma tabbatar munafirci ne yasata daukan qur’ani a wannan lokacin, da karfi ya banko kofar kai kace ballata zaiyi.

*****
Sukam su Ummy suna tafe Hajiya tana zazzagawa Ummy magana “Yanda kika tozartani kema sai Allah ya tozartaki dama batun yau ba nasan kin tsaneni amma ban dauka tsanarki takai ki tattaroni cikin dare da mutuncina da kimata ba, wato Na’imah ta mutu bakwa ganina da daraja, ai ko baku daukeni a bakin komai ba kwa duba jikokin dake tsakaninmu.�?
Ummy tana jinta ta kwantar dakai tare da lumshe ido tana godiya ga Allah daya nuna mata wannan rana.
Hajiya ganin da gaske fa Ummy take ta waigo ta kalleta tace “Naji na bar gidan yanzu ku kamamin gidan da tunda kukai niyya, badai kusa da Aminu bane bakwaso na zauna? To na hakura ku nemamin wani gun……�?
Ummy duk yanda taso ta daure batasan sanda ta kwashe da dariya ba, ta bude ido tace “Gida? Yanzu? Sanda nake rokarki ki zo mu baki wani gun kin kula mu? Yanzu kuwa ai korar kare muka so yi miki akuma dalilin me zamu baki gun zama?�?
Hajiya ta juyo tace “In baku bani ba ina kuke so na zauna? Ni ba kudi ba?�?
“Wannan damuwa tace? Ki wuce can kauyenku a taimaka a baki gun zama ko da can ba’a kauyen kike ba?�?
Hajiya gaba daya zufa ce ta fara karyo mata, tunowa tai da dubu darin da Aminu ya bata harta cinye dubu biyar, yanzu me ta ba mai magani sannan ina zata samu gun kwana na kwana biyu kafin ta dawo gidan ta? Dan ko za’a mutu bamai rabata da gidanta.
 Ummy bata ko bi takanta ba sai da suka isa wani dan karamin hotel wanda kana gani kasan na wanda yake da dan rufin asiri ne.
 Wani kallan kyama tawa hotel din tace “Badai anan zaki ajiyeni bako?�?
Ummy balle jakarta ta ciro dubu goma, ta mika mata tace “kwana daya dubu biyar ne, ragowar kyaci abinci kiyi kudin mota. Sannan zan kara sanar dake makulin gida na gurina kuma goben nan zan sa mutane a cikin gidan, in ma kina tunanin zuwa gun Aminu ne dan ya taimaka miki ki har wahalar da kanki.�?
Huci kawai Hajiya takeyi, Ummy ta dan mika hannu tace “Kawo wayarki nasa miki sabuwar numberta ko zaki kira ki fadamin kin isa riga?�?
Kamar bazata bada ba sai dai gwara ta bada yanda in boka ya nemi wani abin zatai amfani dashi.
Tana mikowa taga Ummy ta balle bayan waya ai nan Hajiya ta fusata ta fara kokarin kwace wayarta, Ummy ta cire sim din kamar masu kokarin dambe ta cila jakarta ta sa a kasa sannan ta mika mata wayarta, Hajiya ta hau zage zage tana tsinewa Ummy akan wallahi sai taga bayansu yanda suka mata itama sai ta ninka musu abinda suka aikata mata.
Nan ta fito ta kalli dan iskan hotel din kawai tahau adaidaita tabat gun dan ko za’a mutu bazata kwana anan ba.

Wayarsa ya dauka kawai ya rubuta mata sako akan zai fita.

 Cikin baccinta banda mafarke mafarke ba abinda ta dinga yi, a zabure ta farka ta sauke ajiyar zuciya ta bakin ciki sannan tai kwafa, bari dai maganinta yaci wallahi wannan karan kwatar kudi zatai sosai yanzu ina amfani ace duk shekarun nan ko kudin gida bata tara ba?

 Mikewa Hajiya tai ta dan kwantar da kanta akan gado, lissafi takeyi menene zai ceceta? Ko dai ta yadda da abinda boka yace?  Abba yai shiru a falo gaba daya kansa ya dau zafi, koda wasa bai taba tunanin Umar zai kwabamai magana haka ba. Umma ce ta fito daga dakin fuskarnan tata ba annuri sam, tana daga tsaye tace "Yanzu Abba abinda ka aikata yayi daidai?"
Juyowa yai ya mata kallan mamaki, tacigaba " A wani dalilin zamu yadda Ibtisam ta wargaza rayuwar Bilkisu? Shikenan ita baza'a barta taji da rashin lafiyarta ba sai an kara mata da bakin cikin kanwarta? To in har auran Bilkisu ya mutu itama Ibty a kashe nata susu su zauna tare Allah ya basu wasu mazajen."

 Idanu ya ware sosai yana kallanta dakyar ya daure yace "Me kike cewa?"
Ta kara tsuke fuska tace "Haryanzu ban sauko daga abinda Ibty tawa Bilkisu ba, wani irin san kai kake nunawa? Taya zata ruguza rayuwar 'yar uwarta sannan ita taje tai rayuwar da takeso?"

Wani irin rashin hali da rashin kirki ne zaisa ki dinga nuna banbanci tsakanin 'ya'yanki?"
 "Kai ma ai banbancin kake nunawa, taya zaka manta da halinda Bilkisu take ciki? Da dalilin daya jefata? Haba! Dame yarinyarnan akeso taji? Da rashin lafiyarta ko da abinda ake mata."
 Mikewa yai ya kalleta a fusace, kamar zai cigaba da fada sai kuma ya fasa kawai ya fita daga falan yai ya wuceta ya shiga daki, kana kallan fuskarsa zakasan ransa yayi mugun baci.
Zama yai a bakin gado yana sake takaicin abinda kunnensa suka jiye mai, hankalinta daya kuwa? 'Ya'ya duk naki ne amma kiri kiri san kai yasa idanunki su rufe?
 Umma kam ko a jikinta, dakin Bilkisu ta wuce cikin kunar rai itama.
Bilkisu na kwance a kan gado kuka kawai takeyi kai kace an rabata da wanda yake tsananin kaunarta ne.
Zama tai a kusa da ita tadan bubuga bayanta alamar lalashi tace "Ya isa hakanan, ke kukan me kikeyi? Bakiga Umar ba sanki yake ba? Me zaisa ki cigaba da wahalar da kanki akanshi?"

Shiru Umma tai tana tsananin tausayinta, itakam bazata yarda a lalatawa yarinyar nan rayuwa ba ga jinya ga bacin rai, ita Ibty a kalla lafiyarta kalau ai.

 Ibty kam bayan ta fito daga wanka ta bude drawer dinta, dole fa ta tattaro hankalin dan mijinta, ga asiri ga shi tana kunya ai abin sai yai yawa, yanzun ma kamar dazu riga da wando ta saka, sun mata kyau sosai sai dai wannan sun kamata sama da na dazu, ta tufke kanta, ta fesa turare bata ko daura dankwali ba ta fito ta nufi kitchen, me zata dafa maidan sauki? Dan haryanzu bata gama ware wa ba gashi dama su biyu ne a gidan.

Hajiya gabaki ɗaya ta rasa abinyi, banda safa da marwa da takeyi a gaban hotel tana kuma jan numfashi babu abinda takeyi. Tunda boka yace mata kullin da akai tun zamanin Na'ima ya warware taji hankalinta gaba ɗaya yaki kwanciya. 
  Ya akai haka? Wane shegen ne yai mata wannan danyan aikin? Sai da akaga ta kusa samun Aminu a hannunta sannan za'a ruguza mata komai? Duk yawan shekarun data dauka tana juyashi ana neman tarwatsa mata rana tsaka?
Aikuwa bazai sabuba, duk yadda zatayi sai ta yi wajan karkato da hankalin Aminu. Babban tashin hankalinta ma yanzu shine gurin kwana, masu hotel din sun koreta wai dakin wari yake saboda sun raina mata hankali. To wai ita yanzu ina zataje? 
  Gidan Aminu ko gurin boka da yace mata sai an sake shiri sannan komai zai koma yadda yake? Taja tsaki yafi ashirin saboda rashin mafita ga batada kudi.
Bari dai taje gurin bokan taji abinda zai yi mata shima tsohon dan iskan, gabaki daya batasan yadda zatai dashi ba akan harkar da yake sata dole, ita dama da Aminun aka ce zata yi sai ta fijin dadi dan shikam namiji ne dan camas a kalla ta huce takaicin dadewa datai ba aure.
  Kuma a kalla taci riba dan ko a baya da ta fara ganinshi sai data san lalai Na'ima tayi sa'a, jinta dingayi inama itace? Kawai dai ta hakura ne dan tasan ba za'a yarda suyi aure ba tayi masa girma.

Juyawa tayi tana kwafa tana zagin mutanan hotel din, ta janyo ma'ajiyar kudinta wanda yanzu gaba daga dubu hudu ne a ciki, oh yanzu idan suka kare ba'a gama yi mata aiki ba ya zatayi? Ko kudin zuwa gun bokan batadashi kenan? Kodai gidan Aminu zata fara zuwa ta gani ko zata dace ya bata wasu?
  'Karki soma Hajiya, kinaji boka yace komai ya ruguje, kinga kila yanzu ma nemanki sukeyi.' Kuma yin kwafa tayi, tana tarar napep, yana tsayawa ta shiga ta sanar dashi inda zai kaita. Jin unguwar da zata yasa yace "Hajiya gidan karbar magani zakije ne?" Taji mai napep din yana tambayar ta bayan sun hau hanya sosai. Ko ina ruwansa? Ta fada cikin ranta ba kuma ta bashi amsa ba. 
  "Zaki karbo maganin mallakar wani ko wata kike so tabi duniya Hajiya?" Gabanta ne ya bada wani irin ras. Karfa turo shi akai yasa mata ido. Zuciyarta ta shiga kitsa mata hakan. Waye zai turo shi? Ummy! Hajiya taji zuciyarta na sanar da ita, a tsorace tace dashi.
 "Turoka akai kasa min ido?" Yace, "A'a mafi akasarin mata idan sunce a kaisu unguwar da kika fada, to gidan wani boka suke zuwa." Hajiya ta sauke ajiyar zuciya ganin ba sa mata ido aka turo yayi ba. Karamin tsaki ta saki tana cewa.
  "Ni wazansa tabi duniya yaro? Ai ni na iya zama da mutane ba tare da boka ko malam ba. Ko yana bada maganin ciwon daji?" Hajiya ta masa tambayar cike da rainin hankali. "Ya za'ai na sani Hajiya? Nifa ban ma taba ganin fuskarsa ba, wani ne yake ciwon?" Dadi ya kama Hajiya taji yace besan shi ba. "Yatace take fama dashi muna ta neman magani har yau bamu dace ba. Ko zaka fara ajiyeni a gidan na tambaya ko Allah zaisa a dace yana badawa, sai na karba." mai napep ya dan sai ta madubi yana kallo Hajiya ta ciki, karaf suka hada ido ta yatsina fuska.
  "Yaro na fadi wani abu na rashin dadi ne?" Ya cigaba da tuki. "A'a gani nayi nace miki boka ne kuma kina cewa a kaiki. Keda yanzu ya dace kuyi ta istigfari kuna tuba harta Allah ta kasance."
Hajiya ta rintsa ido, kaji tsinannan yaro. Cikin hade rai tace.
  "Ai zaka rigani mutuwa dan ba a girma da shekaru mutuwa take ba." mai napep najin haka ya fara tangal-tangal dasu, aifa nan Hajiya ta fara ihu tana surfa masa zagi, ta ririke napep tana cigaba da zunduma ihu, ganin bashida niyyar bari ta fara bashi hakuri tana rokarshi akan yayi hakuri ya taimaka ya daina.
Ta madubi ya kalleta yaga yanda idanunta suka firfito yai kwafa tare da murmushi....

(Anya Hajiya Nason mutuwa? To ba ruwa na ita da mai napep😂)

"Tsaya na sauka, idan baka tsaya ba zanyi sanadin mutuwar mu harda kai." Mai napep na jin abinda Hajiya ta fada, cikin ransa yace, 'daga wasa? Afili kuma sassauta tukin yayi yana cewa.
  "Allah ya baki hakuri Hajiya, irin wasan nan ne na kaka da jikanta." Ware ido tayi tana kallansa hannunta dafe a kirji. "Karka raina min hankali kaji, Kaga nayi kama da kalar kakarka ne da zaka min wasa da rai? Yata dana haifa bata kai ka ba bare har ta haifo min jika kamarka."
  "Allah ya baki hakuri." Ya fada yana shan kwanar da tace ya sauketa. Sai ta ya gaba parking sannan ya juyo yana kallanta yace. "Hajiya kudinki dari hudu."
   "kace mene?" Ta tambaya cikin jin karin bayani.
  "Kudinki dari hudu zaki bada." Tace, "Ai da yake daga Lagos ka dakkoni ko? Ni na zatama cewa zakai na barshi." Da sauri yai wata irin juyowa, kallan juna sukayi yace.
  "Tab four hundred naira zaki miko Hajjaju, fetur tsada yake." Ta zaro ta mika masa tana cewa. "Kayi hakuri ita kadai garan." murza kudin ya shiga yi cikin bacin rai yace, "Haba Hajiya dari biyu?" Ta fice daga napep din tana cewa.
  "Ko na tsaya ka maidani inda ka dakkoni ne?" Haushi ya kama mai napep sai dai kuma babu yadda zai yi da ita, ya burga napep din tare da bula mata kura yabar wajan. "Shegen dan sa ido, gobema ka sake dakkoni kayi min maganar da bance ba." Hajiya ta juya ta nufi gidan boka ranta duk babu dadi.

*******

Yana rike da jakarsa ya rasa abinyi, tunani kawai yake wacce makoma zai fada? Ya rasa Ibty wacce ta zamemasa tauraruwar zuciyarshi.
  Tabbas Aminu ya dace da samun mata mai kyau, Abba ya bashi ibty abagas tunda yana can inuwa yaji an bashi mata. Shi kuwa yayi rainonta ya bata kyakkyawar kulawa amman karshe ya kare a nakasasshiya.
"Bilkisu ina san yayarki, hakika bake nake so ba amman ya zanyi?" Umar ya fada a fili yana furzar da iska a bakinsa. Jin wata mota tana tambayar inda zashi ne ya fara tunanin kodai ya koma? Dan shi yanzu ba komawa kauyen ba a'a rashin ganin Ibty kadai ya isheshi, ga kuma noma dan shikam yafi karfin yin noma yanzu.
  Idan ya koma kauyen mezai yi acan? Bilkisu. Yaji zuciyarsa ta ambaci sunanta tana sake sanar dashi.
Ka koma wajan Bilkisu tunda tana sanka da yawa. Ibty fa? Zuciyar shi ba zata taba yarda ta dinga ganin ibty tare da wani kato bashi ba. "Ina sanki Ibty, me yasa ke bakya so a?" Ya fada yana rike kafadun kwandasta, jin an saki tsaki yasa Umar kallansa.
  Da sauri ya dauke hannunsa ganin kwandasta. "Wai kai a haka soyayya ce ta ratsaka? Wato baka gane wuta da aljanna ko? Ware ka bamu waje muna da aikin yi." Kwandastan ya fada yana ture Umar gefe.
  Akan idanunsa suka ja motar su, Anya mutane sun san yadda yakeji akan ibty? Shifa yana ji a cikin zuciyarshi ake hada duk wata wuta a duniya saboda radadin rashin Ibty. 
  Banda rainin hankali kuma an barshi da wata nakasashiya, gashi ta nace masa itama kodan tasan ba wata moruwa zatayi ba? 
  Yaja tsaki yana kuma kallon gefe da gefensa kamar mai jiran wani.
Shawara ya yanke kawai tafi koma menene ya faru. Yana tsaye har wata motar tazo ya tara ya hau zuciyarshi cike da tunani.

********

Kwance tayi akan gado tana faman kuka a hankali, duk zuciyarta babu dadi ta rasa dalilin da yasa take masifar san Umar gashi shi batatata yake ba.
  Amman me yasa baya santa? Kamar tana magana da wani a kusa sai ji tayi ance mata. Ke nakasassa ce, ibty kuma lafiyayya ce. Ta fashe da kuka a fili ta fara magana ba tare da tasan da hakan ba, ji kawai tayi tana magana cikin girgiza kai.
"Kaddara ce, wallahi kaddara ce Allah ya doramin. Please Ya Umar dan Allah ka soni koda bekai na wacce kake so ba. Ina sanka zan kuma kasance da kai a cikin kowanne irin yanayi, dan Allah karka rabu dani Ya Umar, idan kayi hakan ka ruguza duk wani ragowar farin cikin da nake tare da shi."
  Cikin tausayawa Islam ta goge kwallar data zubo ta bata mata fuska, tabbas Ya Bilkisu tana cikin jarabawa. Babu abinda ya fi ciwo irin kana so ba'a sanka, halin da Bilkisu take ciki kenan.
"Dan Allah Ya Bilkisu kiyi shiru, ki nemi zabin Allah. Idan har Ya Umar alkairi ne agareki zai dawo, idan ba alkairi bane Allah ya hada kowa da rabansa." Bilkisu kallon Iman tayi cike da mamakin kalamanta, batayi magana ba ta maida kai gefe tana dafe da saitin zuciyarta.
 "Kizo kici abinci inji Umma." Kai Bilkisu ta girgiza tana cewa. "Nakoshi." Iman ta sauke numfashi "Kina jin yunwa ya kamata kici abinci, karki sawa kanki damuwa, in har kika rame Ya Umar zai kuma tsanarki. Amman idan yaga kinyi fresh dole zai rage wani wulakancin."
Maganganun Iman gaskiya ne, sai dai taya zata iya cin abinci bayan Umar din ta ya bada sharadi akan auran su. Aminu ba zai taba sakin Ibty ba bare harta sanya ran cigaba da kasancewa dashi. Kuma idan Ibty ta bar Aminu kenan aurenta da Umar ya tabbata tsinkakke, tunda tasan sai ya rabu da ita ya auri Ibty tunda ba abin aure daya shiga tsakaninsu balle.
 "Ki tashi dan Allah Ya Bilki kidena damun ranki, addu'a zamuyi Allah ya dawo da hankalinsa gareki."
Bilkisu ta mike zaune ta kamo hannun Iman, cikin wani yanayi tace. "Kiyi min addu'a karya rabu dani, wallahi ina jinsa har cikin jinina." Iman ta kuma matse hannun Bilkisu cikin na ta "Karki damu kullum kina cikin addu'a ta." Daga haka ta janyo kekenta, da taimakon Iman ta hau suka fito, Iman tana mamakin wannan mahaukacin san, tabbas da Bilkisu kanwartace da dukan tsiya zata mata a dakin nan.

******

Sai da ya gama cin abincin kafin ibty ta gyara wajan ta maida komai kitchen ta gyara. Lalai yanzu kam ta tabbatar ba mafarki take ba. Amma Ya Aminu a hayyacinsa yake kuwa? Yana zaune duk inda tai sai ya bita da kallo. Sai da ta gama komai sannan ta tsaya a wajan kitchen tana tunanin abinyi. Kota koma wajansa? Idan taje me zata yi? Ko hira? Toh tace masa mene? 
  Yana hangota shima a nashi bangaran yana ta tunanin me zai yi mata yanzu?
Dawowa tai ta tsaya tsakanin falanshi da nata tana dan lekensa ta labule.
 "Idan kin gama leken kizo." Ibty taji Aminu ya fada. Saurin komawa ciki tayi tana zaro ido. Ya akai ya ganta? Kai wannan mutumin yana da ido sosai.
"Bazaki zo ba?" Ibty taji yana kiranta, rintsa ido tayi tana murmushi. Kunyarsa takeji sosai, yanzu menene abinyi? Taje ko ta zauna a falanta tace bataji shi ba?
 Bata gama tunani ba sai kawai ganinsa tayi tsaye a bakin kofar, suka hada ido tai saurin kawar da nata tana dariya kasa kasa. "Ya haka madam?" Ibty ta tsuke baki. "Muje." Ya fada yana mika mata hannunsa. Ibty ta mika masa nata a hankali ya mikar da ita, yana rike da ita suka shiga fallonsa sannan suka nufi dakinsa. Itadai da kallo kawai take binshi.
A zahirin gaskiya Aminu neman maida hankalinsa da tunaninsa yake kokarin yin gun Ibty dan sam bayasan ya zauna shi kadai saboda ya tabbatar tunanin abinda ya faru ne zai dinga dawomai, matar daya dauka duk duniya ba kamarta lokaci daya ya fahimci ba haka ba? Uwar 'ya'yanshi? Sam bayaso ya cigaba da zargin abubuwan data aikata a kanshi, gwara ya maida hankalinsa kan matarsa kila hakan ne zai samar mai kwanciyar hankali.

**********

Tunda ya sauka a bakin titinsu mai jar kasa yake ta yatsina fuska, ya juya tare da hango yadda maza ke zazzaune a inuwa sun kishingida, daga masu shan rake sai masu fushe gyada suna jefawa a baki, yaja tsaki tare da gyara jakar bayansa ya fara tafiya. A da dayake hutu yake zuwa bai damu da komai ba amma fa yanzu da yaga alamar zama ya ganshi sai yake ganin mutanen garin wasu kucakai kauyawa.
  "Umaru ya dawo." Yaji wani yaro daga can gefe yana fadawa abokan wasansa. Wai Umaru gatsal, yaro dan karami yana fadar sunansa saboda raini. Gasu Ibty can su Bilkisu Ya Umar a gayance suke fada amman nan kamar suna kiran wani dabba. Ko takan yan majalisar bebiba ya wuce yana ta wani kwambo shi a dole ya dawo daga birni.
  Yana shiga cikin gari kuwa yan matan kauyen nan suka fara yi masa caaa kamar sunga gasasshen nama,  dan wannan abin yana daga cikin abinda ke sashi jin wani girma in yazo kauyen nan, ya tabbatar dan sunga dan gayu ne. Kota kansu beyi ba ya shige gida yana zaginsu shi a dole yafi karfin su.
"Wallahi duk wanda aka cewa dan kauye an cuce shi." Wata mace dake karya kara tana turawa cikin murhu ta kalleshi. "Umaru? Mazan fama, saukar yaushe kenan? Waye ya tabo mana kai a hanya?" Yaja tsaki tare da naushin wata akuya dake gabansa.
  "Su Hansai ne, yadda kika san basu sanni ba, banzaye gajoji kawai." Cikin sanyin jiki matar tace. "Haba mazaje, ya kake yi musu wannan maganar? Ko babu komai su Hansai yan uwanka ne." Umar cikin sauri yace. "Yoni menace? Abu daya kawai na sani ba wacce zan aura saboda ina da mata a can birni. Kima gaya musu dan wallahi zan iya fasawa yarinya baki." Dama ya dawo da bacin rai ga ana neman sake batamai rai.
  "Dama basu ce suna sanka ba ai, kai daga dawowarka sai jaraba da neman fitina." 
Umar bai koma cewa komai ba ya bude wani dan daki ya shiga. Bango ya dafa yana karewa dakin kallo, wanda yake dauke da 'yar tabarma da wani filo duk ya kare, dakinsu ne shida yayansa, tsaki yaja yana tuno yadda ya baro narkeken gadon Bilkisu da lumtsuma-lumtsuman kujerun da aka zuba, nan da nan ya fara jin kamar ya cuci kansa. "Anya bazan koma ba?" Ya tambayi kansa yana sosa haba. 
Ai daga waje ya fara jin ana cewa "Umaru ne da gaske?"
Jin muryar Babansa yasa gabansa ya fadi, tabbas zaici duka in mahaifinsa yasan abinda ga aikata, ita kanta mahaifiyarsa bazata yarda ba.
Sum sum yadan leko yana washe baki ya tsuguna yana gaida mahaifinsa.

*******

Gyara mayafi Hajiya tayi tana kuma waigawa baya, ji take tamkar ana binta idan ta juya kuma bata ganin kowa. Rai a bace ta shiga sai faman jan numfashi take, Allah dai yasa kar yace sai ta bashi kanta, idan yai mata haka tabbas zai ilatata, sakamakon wani irin zugi da takeji gurin nayi mata tun wancan zuwan nata.
  Yana ganinta ya fara dariya yana kuma dukan cinya, tai masa kallon mamaki batace masa komai ba har ta samu wajan zama. Ji tayi yace.
"Daman nasan dole zaki zo Hajiya, akan Aminu na tabbatar zaki bar duk wani abu kizo wajena. Sai dai k..." Cikin takaici ta dakatar dashi tace. "Menene abin yi?"
Shiru yai yana kallanta yace "na fadamiki sai kin samu macen da zata dauke hankalinsa daga kan matarsa, a da can abu bai yiwu ba balle yanzu da komai ya wargaje."

 Shiru tai tana tunani, cikin bacin rai tace "Kafi kowa sanin banda kowa a duniya banda Naima ina......."
Sai kuma tai shiru ta kalleshi ta dan kauda kai tana motsa baki, kallanta shima yai kafin ya bushe da dariya yace "Badai ke kanki kike tunani ba ko?"
Wani kallo tamai tace "Me kake san cewa? Ai ko da ba auransa ba zan iya dauke hankalinsa daga kan tsinaniyar 'yarnan, na kai makurar da ba abinda bazan iya aikatawa ba.
Dariya ya bushe dashi wanda ya kasa dainawa har abin ya fusatata, yau na tabbatar da abinda na dade ina zargi.
Ta hade rai sosai tace "Me?"
Na dade ina tunanin meyasa bakyasan Aminu yai aure? Bayan zaki iya neman maganin mallakar matarsa ki zauna lafiya? Sai dai yau kin nunamin dalili.
Ni babu wani dalili da nake nema ka bani magani da mafita  wacce zatasa Aminu hankalinsa ya kasa kwanciya sai ya bani gida da motar hawa, ni yanzu su nake san ya siya min sai na barshi  da wannan figaggiyar matar tashi." Yayi dariya yana nuna mata kusa dashi, tayi kamar bata gane ba yace.
 "To tunda dai kin dage a barshi. Yanzu kizo nan dan dama Shine dalilin da yasa nace kizo din. Wannan aikin da za'a yi miki babba ne, kedai kawai kibi umarnin abokan aiki wato aljanun da suke yi." Hajiya ta kama baki, ta kuma kallan inda yake tace.
"Menene umarnin nasu?" Boka ya matsa kusa da ita, Hajiya kamar ta fashe da kuka yace. "Zaki kwanta dani, kiyi min abinda ko a mafarki bantaba jinsa, karkiyi korafi ko ki dinga jin haushin abin, yin haka zai sa aikin da za'a yi yaki yin kyau." Ranta ya sake baci tace.   
 "Wannan aikin kam ya isheni, gabaki daya kun ruguza min guri kun maida shi kamar wani bakin kofi kowa yazo yasha ta wajan. Ni kam babu wata hanyar sai wannan ne?"
Mamaki ya kamashi, ta gaji kenan? Yanajin dadin matar nan gashi ta fara nuna gajiyawarta. Kodai yai mata asiri ta yadda komai yace mata ba zatai masa musu ba? "Nace babu wata hanya sai wadda ka fada?" Muryar Hajiya ta katse masa hanzarinsa.
  "Idan har kina san Aminu yai miki abinda kike so, dole sai kin kasance da mutumin nan dani na kwanaki bakwai." Hajiya ta rintsa ido tanajin wani irin bakin abu a wuyanta, haushi da takaici yasa tace masa, "Sai kai tayi." Murmushin jin dadi yai tare da janyo wani ruwa ya mika mata. "Fara shan wannan, ruwan farin jinine. Idan kika sha muka gama tarayya aka gama sauran aiyukan zakiga aiki, Aminu da kanshi zai nemoki." Banda tana san aikin nan da ba zata taba yadda tayi wannan aikin bakar wahalar ba.
  Dakyar ta shanye maganin saboda tsananin baurin da yake yi, ga wani wari kamar na kwata.....
