A can bangaren Umar kuwa tunda ya shiga cikin dakin ya kasa fitowa, ya zauna sai zabga tsaki yake tamkar wani karamin yaro.
  "Ga tuwon dawa nan miyar kuka, sabuwa ce ma Larai ta kawo min ita kyauta."
Umar yabi langar tuwon da ido, ganinsa bakikirin yasa shi kawar da kai. Shifa dama in yazo kauye da kudinsa yake tahowa ya dinga siyan abinda ransa ke so sai dai wannan karan ya taho ba kudi saboda Abba bai barshi ya shirya ba, gashi ya saida abubuwan kasuwarsa ya tattaro kudin ya boye a daki yana so in ya sace Ibty su gudu da kudin, sam ilimin maidasu banki baizo mai ba.
Kallan Goggo yai yace "Gwaggo bakuda fried rice? Ko 'yar shinkafa? Naga da ana kokarin yin abinci mai dan dadi?" Ya tambaya yana sauke numfashi. "A ina take mazan fama? Mace ce ko namiji?" Umar ya kuma jan tsaki dan yasan tasani sarai tsabar ya kona mai rai ne take wannan zancen, sau nawa tana zuwa gidan Abba tana ci? Cikin jin haushi ya dago tare da cewa. "Abinci ne fa." Gwaggo ta bude ido. "Ai kaji, ni ina nasanshi banda kinibibinka? Idan zakaci tuwo kaci shi garemu, idan bakaci ka barshi akwai mabukata." 
 Umar ya sake bin tuwon da ido tare da cewa. "Kai haba ina! ni gaskiya bazanci wannan bakin tuwon ba wallahi."
  "Iyyy ba shakka, to karkaci mana." Gwaggo ta dauke ta fita da kayanta sai fada take yi. "Karyar banza karyar wofi, indai kana cikin kauyen nan kuwa sai dai ka mutu da yunwa dan banga inda zaka samu irin abincin da kukeci ba a birni. Ai ni tunda naga rashin darajarka a biki nasan lalai jin dadi da rashin godiyar Allah ne ya maka yawa.
Umar naji ya dafe goshi tare da tuno abinda yake yi, cikin isa da iko zai ce da Bilkisu ta dafa masa abu, tana rawar jiki take masa kuma mai dadi. Dan in yaso mugunta ko an dafo abinci daga bangaren Umma sai yace shi bayasan wannan abincin sai dai ta sake wani.

  "Anya zan sakeki Bilkisu? Na rasa hasken zuciyata Ibty, wannan shegen mai kama da aljanu ya rike ta kam. Allah ya isa ban yafe ba, nasha wahalar kulawa dake amman aka cuceni. Har abada bazan yafe maka ba dan banza mai kan agwagwa."
Har aka yi sallar isha Umar yana daki yana shakar bacci, tun Gwaggo na zuwa lekashi harta kwanta abinta ta rabu dashi. Wajan karfe goma sha daya na dare ya farka da wata masifafiyar jin yunwa.
  Alwala yai yaje daki, sallah yai ya fito yana ta raba idanu da alama neman inda aka ajiye tuwon dazu yake. Wajan girki ya karasa yanata bude-bude, karar motsi yasa Gwaggo fitowa daga daki tana haska wajan.
"Wace irin fitinace wannan? Mekake nema a wajan?" Cikeda rashin damuwa yace. "Tuwon." Gwaggo ta rike baki, "Tuwo! Aikuwa babu." Ta fada tare da komawa zata shiga daki yace. "Tuwon ne babu?" Tace "Kwarai ma kuwa, ko kana shakka ne?" Kafa yasa yai shuri da murfin tukunya yana masifa.
 "Ya za'a ce babu tuwon bayan ansan banci ba? Wannan ai ba daidai bane wallahi. Haba Gwaggo ya zaki cinye?" Mamaki ya kamata. "Wai dama zakaci? Bana kai maka ba ka tsaya kana cewa wai kai kafi son menene? Toh tuwo dai ya kare sai kasan abinyi."
  Tana kaiwa nan ta shige dakinta, Umar kuma yai kwafa ya koma daki. Kenan yau da uwar yunwa zai kwana, dama ba wani abu yaci ba. Duk ya rasa abinyi dan ko kudin siyan awara bashi dasu ballantana yace zai siya, kodama yana da kuɗin yanzu babu kowa a gari saboda dare ne duk antafi gida.
"Idan ka gama kunkunin kazo ka karba, ai da na zata balangun Dan Bale zakaje ka siyo tunda ba zakaci tuwon dawa ba." Umar yaga Gwaggo na miko masa langar yai saurin karasawa zai karba ta rike. "Bazakai min godiya ba?" "Nagode Gwaggo." Ya fada yana cizon lebe.
  "Ato gobe ma kace min bazakaci tuwo ba kaga karyar banza." Umar yaki magana ya koma ya zauna tare da faracin tuwon yana lumshe ido saboda dadi.
Tas ya cinye tuwon ya kura da ruwan sanyi na randa tare da sakin katuwar gyatsa yana faman hura hanci. 
  Saboda baccin da yai hakan ya hanashi yin na dare, ga duhu ga sauraye sai kuka suke wasu kuma suna gasa masa cizo yana ta buge-buge da riga. Haba malam birni lafiya ne, idan babu nepa ga inji, idan babu kuma ga sola duk dan kar a zauna cikin duhu. Gashi zuciya ta kawoshi kauye yazo sai wahala yake sha. 
Haka yai kwanan zaune gashi ko tunanin yin nafila da istigfari beyi ba, sai tunanin yadda ibty take mannewa jikin Aminu yake, daya rintse ido zai hangesu kwance kan gado suna soyayya a zabura zakaga ya mike yana sauke huci sai kuma ya jawo filo ya nausa yace "Allah ya isa tsakanina dakai mugu, azzalumi dan bakin ciki."
  Babu ruwanshi da yadda ya baro Bilkisu bare ya tausaya mata, yasan dai kawai zai bar auransu ne saboda shima yaci gaba da morar arzikin da yakeci. 

**********
Aminu ya zaunar da ita kan gado kallansa kawai take itakam mamaki duk ya isheta, gani tai ya koma gefe ya zauna, cikin sanyin jiki ta dan sunkuyar da kanta kasa, ita dama tayi mamakin yanda ya canza ashe ba gaba daya ba......"
Jin an jawota ya kwantar da ita akan cinyarsa ne yasata kallansa cikin wani yanayi, hannu yasa yadan zare hijab din kanta sannan ya cire hularta ya sa hannu asaman gashinta, kallan mamaki kawai takemai sai wani irin faduwar gava da takeji akai akai, shiru ne ya ratsa dakin saboda abu ne da basu taba yi ba, itakam lumshe idanu tai kawai tana addu'a Allah karya kawo abinda zai sake shiga tsakaninsu.
 Cikin wannan yanayi taji yace "Kiyi hakuri ki yafemin."
 Idanunta ta bude a hankali ta saukeshi akansa, dan murmushi yai cikin sanyi yace " I don't know what happens to me, gashi kiri kiri zuciyata nasan tausaya da baki kulawa sai dai jikina baya iyawa, haka kawai in na kalleki sai inji duk duniya ba wanda na tsana irinki, sai zuciyata tai ta fadamin abubuwa daban daban."
 Idanunta ne suka dan ciciko tana kallansa, ganin ya kalli idanunta yasa tadan sauke idanta kasa, sunkuyowa yai wanda hakan ya kara sa kata faduwar gaba, a hankalinya sumbaci idanunta, sannan yace "Wannan yayan ya saku kuka sosai ko?"
Cikin wani yanayi tace "Eh."
Murmushi yai yace "Madam yaushe na tambayeki?"
Dan baki ta turo tace yanzu mana."
Ya dan girgiza mata yatsa yace "ni ga wanda na tambaya nan bake ba.."
Ta kalleshi tadan juyo ta ciki saitin cikinsa tana san yin magna sai dai haryanzu kwarjini yake mata "Ya ya? Har an gama maganar dani? Bayan ina kallan yanda kika dage kina magana da Bal......"
Batasan sanda ta dago tasa hannu ta rufe mai baki ba, dan baya ta turashi hakan yasashi kwanciya akan gadon ita kuma ta dan daneshi da hannunta a bakinsa, kallansa tai da sauri ta cusa kanta cikin kirjinsa cikin kunya, "Ni dama nasan abinda kike san aikatawa kenan."
Dagowa tai cikin mamaki tace mene?
Yanda ta dadaneshi ya nuna mata yace "Gashinan, so kike ki kwanta a jikina.
Kokarin mikewa ta fara yasa hannu ya rike kugunta, yace "Daga baya kenan, yanzu wayancewa kike shirin yi."
Kallan shagwaba tamai tace "Allah ni....."
Kai yadan karkatar alamar ko?
Hakan yasata yin murmushi, a hankali ya kwantar da ita a kirjinsa shiru suka sakeyi, can cikin wata murya yace "Me kike tunani akan abinda aka gani a bayan hoton can?"
Shiru tai bata dago ba sai dai tace "Banida kwakwaran hujjar dazan bada akan abinda banida masaniya a kai."
Yanayinsa kadai xaka kalla kasan lalai yanacikin wani yanayi mai wuyar fassarawa.
 Murmushi yai, dakin yai dan shiru kowa da abinda yake sakawa a ransa, "Abinda ya faru dazu yana nuna san da Na'imah ke min da wanda nake mata ba tsakani da Allah bane?"
Ibty ta juyo tana kallansa dan ya bata tausayi sosai yanda yai maganar, mikewa zaune yai ya dan yi ajiyar zuciya yace "ina tunanin na girma nasan duniya, ni babba ne ashe babu wanda ya kaini kankanta."
Ibty ta bude baki zatai magana taga ya mike kallanta ya juyo yai yace bari nai meeting, karfe uku ne gashi ban kula ba har ukun tayi."
Kai ta dagamai a hankali tare da binshi da kallo harya fita, komawa tai ta kwanta rufda ciki s kan gadan tace "Wannan wani irin bala'i ne?"

*******
Washegari.....
Abin duniya duk ya ishi Hajiya, alakarta da boka ya fara isarta musamman da yanzu take jin idan beyi ba kamar ana tsunkulinta a sassan jiki, ga wancan tsinanen mutumin da yafi boka danshi inya fara ganayin kusan awa biyu kafin ya gama, takaicinta ma sam ta kasa sanar dashi bata so ta gaji. Dan karamin daki ko ince dan karamin gida wanda daga daki sai bandaki suka ware mata daga kusa da gidan Malam ya take zaune a ciki suna abinda suke so da ita, gaba daya ta kasa nuna rashin san da takewa wannan rayuwar, duk ta rame ya jeke, wai kuma duk cikin nemo kan Aminu ne.
  Tana zaune tayi zugum kamar wacce taiwa sarki karya, wayarta tai ringing jiki na rawa ta dakko. Dan dazun nan mutumin nan ya kawo
Mata sabuwar waya ya kuma sa an mata welcome back na sim dinta, dan tun jiya take rokonsa akan ya taimaka mata, sai daya sadu da ita sau biyu s lokaci daya wai kudin yin welcome back da waya.
Ganin Aminu yasa zuciyarta harbawa, haka kurum taji yau bata san daga wayarsa, dan haka harta tsinke bata samu damar dagawaba. Gefe daya na zuciyarta yana gaya mata aikin boka ne ya fara aiki. 
Kiran ya sake shigowa ta dauka jiki na rawa kamar tana gabanshi muryarta har tawa takeyi.
 "Waalaikas salam." Tana fada ta sa kuka cike da makirci. Aminu dake zaune a falo bayan ya 
dawo daga aiki, da sauri ya dafe goshi, gaba daya haushinta yakeji, abu daya yasa shi nemanta dan yanasan jin meke faruwa sannan dazu Islam da Ilham suka tambayeshi tana ina? Dan suna dawowa dazu suka nufi bangarenta suka ganshi a kulle. Wai Suna san ganinta. 
"Ina wuni Hajiya?" Hajiya ta hadiye kukanta, mamaki ya kamata a da idan har yaji tana cikin damuwa rudewa yake balle kuka, amman yanzu tana kuka yana miko mata gaisuwa.
  "Dama tambaya zanyi kina ina?" Hajiya ta zaro ido 'Mezai mata? Ko gidan ya siya mata ne? "Hello." Taji ya fada tai saurin dafa bango.  "Ina gidan wata kawata, ince dai lafiya ko Aminu?" Aminu yace, "Ina so nasan inda kike yanzu." A razane tace, 
"Lafiya dai ko?" Yace, "Zanzo gurinki ne." Ta zaro ido tamkar tana gabansa, Toh me zai yi mata? Gabanta ne yai wani mumunan faduwa badai maganin da akace an bude komai ya karye yake kokarin Tuhumar ta akai ba? Ya san Na'ima ta aikata ne? ko kuma sha'awar ganinta kawai yakeyi? Tana san tasan mezai kawoshi dan tasan abin fada masa. 
  Muryarsa ce ta katseta yace "Hajiya kinyi shiru." Tace "Wallahi ina fama da ciwon ciki ne, sai dai ka bari wani lokacin sai mu hadu. Kaga ba dadi nace kazo bayan matar batasan zakazo ba, ni kuma bazan iya fita ba saboda ciwo."
Kafin ya bata amsa tayi saurin cewa "Ina su Ilham? A gaishe min dasu."  Kafin Aminu yai yunkuri magana yaji ta kashe wayar, abin yai matukar bashi mamaki, ya kuma sake tabbatar da cewa Hajiya tasan da wadannan layun ta kuma san dalilin dayasa yake nemanta sarai.
Tsaki yaja cike da haushi ya ajiye wayar, yana jin tsananin kunya da tsanar kansa yanzu a kalli Hajiya ace surukarshi ce, shi kuwa mai ya kaishi hada zuri'a da wadannan mutanen? sannan su Islam su kalleta suce kakarsu ce, wannan abin yana ci masa zuciya.

**********

Da kallo Abba ya bita, Bilkisu kuwa kamar ta sani taki yadda da dago kai sai wasa tske da wajan key din kekenta. Umma ce ta fito daga kitchen ganin Bilkisu yasa ta saki murmushin jin dadi, saboda bata san ganinta cikin damuwa.
  "Kin fito kici abinci?" Bilkisu ta daga kai batace komai ba dan har lokacin ranta babu dadi.
"Zonan Bilkisu." Cewar Abba yana ajiye wayar hannunsa. Ta gungura keken zuwa gurinshi bata kallesa ba yace.
 Yadan ja numfashi yace "Kina tunanin Umar zai yadda da aure tsakaninku? Ada ina yiwa Umar kallon mai hankali da sanin yakamata. 
Amman yanzu tunanina ya kauce daga wannan kyawawan zuwa akasin su. Kina ganin zakici gaba da zama tare dashi har tsawon rayuwarki?"
  Shiru Bilkisu tayi dan ji take ko hauka Umar yake zata iya zama dashi, ballanta kuma yana da hankali kawai soyayya ce ta rufe masa ido kamar yadda ta rufe mata duk wani aibunsa. Kuma yanzu ma maganganun da Abba keyi akan shi, ji take kamar ta mutu saboda aibata mata miji da yakeyi.

Cikin sanyin jiki tace, "Abba lokacin da kuka yiwa ibty aure batasan waye mijin ba, kunyi mata an kai ta gurinsa, ya taya har ta janyo suka fara kula juna duk bamu sani ba. Abba nima ina so ku bar aurena dana Ya Umar har zuwa lokacin da zai manta da wacce ke cikin ransa."
 "Muna so mu karbar miki yancinki ne Bilkisu, barin Umar a haka ba zai sashi ganin mutunci da darajarki ba. Ki bari mu nuna masa ko wacece ke a gurin mu ta yadda nan gaba zaiji tsoran takaki yadda yakeso. Hakan zai yiwune idan har kin bamu hadin kai Bilkisu." Tayi shiru tana share hawayen da suke bata mata fuska.
"Shikenan Abba amma dan Allah karkuyi yadda zai sakeni, idan Ya Umar ya rabu dani zan shiga damuwa dan Allah Abba."
 Tausayinta ya kama Umma, ta karasa wajanta tana lallashinta cikin kulawa "Ya isa kukan, Insha Allahu yadda kike san yaran nan haka shima zai soki wata rana, zanci gaba da yi miki addu'a Insha Allahu ba zaki kuma rasa wani abu ba, kiyi shiru ki kuma yi hakuri." Abba ya amsa da "Amin ya Allah." 
Shiru Abba yai cikin wani yanayi yace "Iman fa?"
Tace "Tana gidan koyan dinki."
"Kin kira Ibtisam din?"
Tace "Ita da hankalinta yake a kwance? Kana ganin gidan da aka kaita ga mijinta na santa, zan kirata gobe, tunda kaji shiru bata kira ba ai lafiya ce, bakaji hausawa sunce inka ji shiru lafiya ba ce?"
Kallanta yai kawai baice komai ba, yana mamakin yanda sam take nuna banbanci karara akan 'ya'yanta, sam bata nunawa Ibtisam kulawa, haka ma Iman to anyi sa'a ita Iman da kanta take nemawa kanta abinda takeso ba kamar Ibty ba.
Daga haka suka samu Bilkisu taci abincin sannan suka samu ta fara walwala ana ta hira da ita sama-sama, duk da haka zuciyarta na wajan Umar da kuma ina zashi? Wannan abun yana damunta sosai, tana so tasan yana wane wajan ko hankalin da yake ciki.

*******
A kwana a tashi asarar mai rai.....
Yau sati biyu kenan Hajiya na zaune cikin wannan dakin kwani irin ciwon ciki take fama dashi wanda in har ya motsa mata duk irin maganin da zai bata batajin dadi har sai mutumin nan yazo yayi mu'amala da ita ta baya kamar yadda ya saba yi.
  Kwananta goma a gurinsa saboda ciwon da yake damunta yasa bata tafi ba, kuma ko ya bartama bata da wajan zuwa. Hajiya kwance tana ta faman murkususu abin har ya fara bawa bokanta tsoro, yace ta shirya zata bar musu gida saboda yana tsoran ta mutu a gurin shi ace ai shine ya kasheta tunda kowa yasan wannan dakin da take nashi ne. Cikin azabar ciwo tun abun na cinta a hankali yanzu ko zama bata iya yi saboda wajan ciwo yake sai dai tayi goho. Babban tashin hankali mutumin da yake zuwa safe da rana da daddare yau kwana biyu kenan ko lekota baiyi ba, dan kayan dadin dayake kawo mata suma duk sun kare, ga yunwa ga azaba ga babu kudi.

**
Tana kishingide gun sai uban zugi yake mata amma duk da haka dan bala'i neman mutumin nan takeyi ko shi ko boka wani yazo ya biya mata bukata.
A fusace ya bude labulen dakin da take a kwance ya kalleta cikin jin haushi. "Dama da nace ki shirya zaki barmin guri ashe bakiji ba?" Cikin wahalar ciwo Hajiya tace. "Ina zanje?" Ya rike baki "Da ina kike zaune? Koda a karkashina kike? Ni da ba mahaifinki ba bakuma mijinki ba ina zan dauki nauyin muhalinki?"
 Cikin zafin rai Hajiya tace " To kai in bazaka kula dani ba ka sanar da wanda yake bukatata ai yanada kudi tunda har gida yaso sai min, ka fadamai ya sai min gida na koma, a wannan matsiyacin gidan naka kake cin zarafina?"
Dariyace ta kusa kwacemai lalai batasan har mutumin da take magana ya gama aiki ya manta ma da ita ba? Dan harya samu wata?
Jiyai tace " Wai ma naga ai yanzu aiki kake min ko har an gama? Dan banga Aminu ya susuce yana nemana ba?" Ta fada tana sake cije lebe saboda azabar da takeji. Wayarta ya hango yaje ya dauka ya fice tana ta kiransa Amman yaki kulata.
Lambar Aminu ya nemo, jikinsa har rawa yakeyi saboda san ya kirashi. Dan shikam tsoro yake gashi har an fara gulmar sa akan ya ajiye babbar mace yana bushasha, kenan kiris zai sa ace shiya kasheta.

Acan bangaren Aminu kuwa Ibty na zaune kusa dashi lokacin suna zaune a falo wai kafadarsa ke ciwo ta ce zatamai tausa, tana farawa ya jawota ya dawo da ita kan cinyarsa sannan ya kalleta yace "Shikenan kafada na ciwo itama kafar neman karyamin ita ake san yi."
Baki tadan bude tace "Yaya kaifa ka jawoni."
Ya danyi shiru sannan yadan girgiza kai yana dan kane ido irin mamakin nan yace "Oh ni yaran yanzu, ba kunya in su Islam suka shigo suka ganki a haka kuma kice musu me?"
A firgice ta nemi mikewa yasa hannu ya riko kasan cikinta, kallansa tai tace "Yaya Allah karsu shigo su ganmu a haka."
Kafada yadan daga yace "Wannan kuma laifinki tunda kece kikai fitsarar zama a jikina."
Zatai magana wayarsa tai kara.....
Bugu biyu ya daga tare da yin sallama, dama ya dauke kai akanta ne yana jira har duniya ta koya mata hankali sannan ya sata a gaba ta sanar dashi duk abinda ke faruwa.
A rayuwarsa bayasan zargi shiyasa gaba daya yake kokari kauda kai akan abinda ke faruwa, a koda yaushe ya tuna ji yake kamar zaiyi kwalla, ya yarda da Na'imah iya yarda bai taba tunanin zata aikata mai haka ba, har zama yake yana san yai tunanin wai wani irin so ya mata? Sai dai baya iya tunawa, wanda hakan ya sake tabbatar mai lalai wannan san bana Allah bane.
Sabanin muryar da yake tsammani sai yaji ta namiji.
 A dan zabure ya mike yace "Lafiya dai ko? Ina mai wayar?" Yadda Aminu yai magana ne yasa boka zaro ido, sosai yaji tsoran su hadu a zahiri dan daga ji zai yi matuƙar kwarjini.
"Batada lafiya munga sunanka ne na karshe wanda sukai waya da ita shi yasa muka kira mu sanar dakai." Aminu ya shiga mamaki kafin yace " Batada lafiya?"
Cikin dauriya yace "Ina Hajiyar yanzu?" Bokan yaja numfashi, yamai kwatancen can wani guri sannan ya katse. 
****

Kallansa Ibty tai cike da sanjin bayani, riko mata hannu Aminu yai yana dan matsawa yace.
 "Babu komai." Ibty ta kalleshi sosai, ganin kamar tana masa kallan makaryaci ne yasa shi yin murmushi tare da cewa "Hajiya ce wai bata da lafiya." Cikin sauri tace "And?" Yaja numfashi ya sauke tare da cewa. "Ana jiran naje inda take."
"Bazaka ba, trap ne, wannan matar Allah akwai abinda ta shir......."
Labanta ta hade saboda tuna kalaman da takeyi, a cikin ranta tace Banda rainin hankali meyasa tun lokacin da ka kirata ba'a ce kaje ba sai yau? Bazakaje. Yanda ta dage kan cewa bazai je ba ne ya bashi mugun mamaki ta dauke tace "Allah nidai A'a haka kawai su sake rikita ka, daga rashin kallo a koma tsana mai karfi, dan Allah ni nasan kunun da aka baka ne yaja ka tsaneni lokacin, yanzu wa yasan me za'a baka?"
Kallanta kawai yake tana fada yace
 "Madam calm down! Zanje ne saboda su Islam." Zuciyar Ibty tayi sanyi amman dai bata san yaje gani take tamkar munafuncin Hajiya ne. "Karki damu Insha Allahu babu abinda zai samu mijinki kinji?" Sunkuyar da kai tayi tana wasa da gefen rigarta da hannunta.
Tashi Aminu yai tsaye yana gyara maballin riga. Ganin ya dauki mukullin mota yasa Ibty tai saurin tashi ta dauki hijab dinta tana turo baki. Aminu ya kalleta da mamaki yace. "Yah?" Sai da ta kawar da kai sannan tace dashi "To nidai muje tare, ni gaskiya bazan bari kaje gurin wannan matar kai kadai ba."
  Aminu ya janyota a hankali ya sanya ta a jikinsa, ya dan sumbaci goshinta tare da bubbugawa bayanta alamar lalashi tabbas ta damu dashi tana kuma san kare masa lafiyarsa, daidai kunnan ta yai maganar cewa. "Karki damu, kici ba da min addu'a Insha zanje lafiya na dawo." Babu yadda ta iya dole ta kyaleshi ya fita ya shiga mota.....
Da kallo ta bishi idanunta kamar zasu kawo kwalla, nan danan ta koma ciki tai alwala tadau Qur'ani, bataso wani abu ya sake shiga tsakaninsu, dan yanzu rayuwa suke mai cike da sha'awa tana san Aminu har cikin ranta ji take kamar bazata iya rayuwa yanzu babu shi ba.

*46*


Tunda ya shiga daki ta koma ta zauna akan kujera dabas gaba daya mamaki ya hana Ibty yin motsi, tasan za'ayi haka tabbas dan tunda yace gurin Hajiya zashi tasan da wahala ba'a sake hargitsa mata shi ba. Haushi duk yabi ya kamata saboda ita ta barshi ya tafi, inda ta dage akan cewar tare zasu tasan zai yadda. Wai har sai yaushe ne Hajiya zatabar Aminu yai rayuwar jin dadi? Daga auran yarki shikenan sai abu ya zama masifa da bala'i? Kodama auran ai ba dasaninsa ba.
 Ibty kam ganin zancen zuci bayi mata zai yi ba yasa ta koma daki, wajan madubi ta nufa tana kallan kanta. Ko har sai yaushe zasu samu kwanciyar hankali kamar ko wane ma'aurata? 
Kwana biyu ta huta da matsalar Umar, amman ta kasa rabuwa da matsalar Hajiya. Ibty ta kuma matsar da fuskarta tana karewa kanta kallo tace "Ada ta dauka uwar miji itace matsala sai dai a nata bangaren ta rasa da me zata kira Hajiya, wannan bala'i dame yai kama" Kanta ta dafe, ada ta bari Umar ya takurawa rayuwarta, nan ma Hajiya ta takura ma rayuwar auranta sai dai tana ganin yanzu bazata bari a kara shiga tsakaninta da Aminu ba, shine farincikinta, turare tasa hannu ta janyo ta feshe jikinta dashi sannan ta fito. Dakin Aminu ta nufa, cikin shahada ta dan murda tana fatan Allah yasa ya bude kofar, jin kofar ta bude yasata yin wani ajiyar zuciya, a hankali ta tura kofar gani tai bakowa a kan gadan, dan tura kofar ta sakeyi yi  sosai, me zata gani? ganin kasan dakin tai duk kaya hakan yasa tai saurin shiga.
 Turarukan kan madubinsa ne duk ya watsosu kasa, sannan takardar hutansa shi da Na'ima wanda glass din ya fashe Aminu duk ya yaga shi, yana tsaye rike da kai alamar ya rasa mai zaiyi. Ibty ta kalli Aminu zuciyarta na bugawa dan tana tsoran ya juyo ya ganta yai mata wulakanci. Amman duk da haka sai da ta nemo juriya ta dake sosai ta karaso ciki ta tsaya a bayansa tayi masa magana cike da kulawa.
"Zuciyar da aka ginata da hakuri babu abinda ya kamaceta a koda yaushe sai hakurin, Abban Islam kana cikin jarabawa mai matukar wahala, sai addu'a da fatan Allah ya baka ikon cinyewa. Kayi hakuri ka fitar da kanka daga jikin Hajiya shine kawai zai sanya ka samu lafiya da nutsuwar zuciya."
"How?" Ibty taji ya fada cikin juyowa a fusace, kafin ta kuma magana yaci gaba da cewa.
"Taya zan yi nesa da ita bayan akwai Ilham da Islam a tare damu? Meyasa Hajiya zata min haka? Ta cuceni ta cuci yarana da zasu yi mata kallon kaka. Wallahi nayi da..." Sai kuma ya kasa karasawa ya kalli ibty yana cizon labansa.
Taku daya ta kara ta kwantar da kanta a gadan bayansa, dan yanada tsayi duk da itama tanada tsayi amma bata kaiwa samansa, idanunta tadan kumshe ta daura hannu a gefen kanta tace "Kayi hakurin daka saba yi, Habibty........." Kalamanta sun matukar sanyaya masa zuciya har besan lokacin da ya juyo tare da kamo kafadunta ya rike ba ya rungumeta a jikinsa tsam.
"Thank you." Ya fada, dagota ya sake yi yadan karkatar dakai yace "A tabbatar an cigaba da kirana da wannan sunan."
Labanta ta hade jitai ya sunbaci saman goshinta. Tadanyi murmushi tace "Ni kunya nakeji in kiraka haka a gabansu Islam."
Kunnenta ya matso yace "Ya zakiyi?"
Tace "Dame?"
Yai wani murmushi "Dole ki kira ni haka, dan in bashi ba bazan amsa ba."
Baki tadan turo, yatsarsa ya dan hade kamar sai rankwashi goshinta hakan yasata runtse ido da sauri, ji tai ya jawota jikinsa ya rungume, "Gobe kinada skul ko?"
Kai ta daga, ya jinjina kai yace "Yara fa?"
Suna daki suna wasa, yadan daga kai, sannan ya juya kallansa tai taga yanda yanayinsa ya canza, jiki a sanyaye ta kalleshi dan yaki sanar da ita abinda yake faruwa da Hajiya ita kuma batasan tambayarsa, shikam a ganinsa shi kadai ya dace yaji wannan abin.

Sanye take cikin riga da skirt na lace maroon color, tayi kyau sosai, Iman ce dake koyan kwalliya ta fente mata fuskarta, ta fito das da ita, sai murmushi takeyi ita kadai. Kekenta ta gunguro har zuwa falo ta tsaidashi a inda ta saba, wayarta ta ciro a gefen wani aljihu dake jikin keken ta fara daukar selfie, tana juyar da wayar zuwa wajan kofa sai kawai ta ganshi ya daga labule. Kai kuma an baka ka raina ko? Toh ba zaka bar gidan nan ba sai da takardar sakin Bilkisu a hannuna."

"Karya ne, nasanka sarai Umar. Ko ka manta rashin albarkar daka dinga gabza mana saboda kana ganin mun rabaka da rabin ranka?"

"Zanyi shawara tukunna." Tana kaiwa nan ta shige daki tabarshi a inda yake. Umar ya cije lebensa ya tashi ya fita dan jiran Abba ya dawo ya karbi mukullin bangaransa. Wai akan wacen nakashashiyar ake wani juyashi? Tsaki yai ya zauna akan benci yana tunanin yanda yasha love dinsa da Ibtisam, lalai duniya ta juyamai baya dole ne ya lalaba Bilkisu.

"Haba Abba." Cewar Umar yana turo baki.
"Nayi karya ne?"
"Abba meka maidani?"
"Oho maka."
Tashi Umar yai kawai sukaga ya wuce zuwa dakin Bilkisu, babu wanda yabi ta kanshi ganin fitina yakeji da ita yau din. Zaune ya sameta tana ninke kaya, tunda ta kalleshi sau daya bata kuma ba duk da cewa ba haka zuciyarta taso ba. Shima Umar karewa dakin kallo yai wanda ke faman kamshi. Nashi dake kauye ya tuno banda warin akuyoyi da kaji babu abinda yake yi.

Yadan rike hanci kamar mai neman yin kuka, jikinta ne yai sanyi, ta kuramai ido tana tunanin abinyi, jitai ya jawo kekenta ya rungumeta wai shi a dole kuka yake, rikicewa tai tace "Na hakura Ya Umar dan Allah karkai kuka....."

"Abba mukullin." Wani kallo Abba yamai, Bilkisu tace "Abba dan Allah."
Babu musu ya dakko keyholder dinsa ya ciro ya mika masa yana mai wani kallon tuhuma.
"Zamu tafi bangarenmu tare da matata." Umar ya fada kai tsaye. Suka hada ido da Umma ganin batace komai ba yasa Abba yi masa sallama da hannu. So hana ganin laifi, kiri-kiri soyayya ta rufewa Bilkisu ido ta yadda bata ganin komai sai Umar koda kuwa zai kaita wuta.

Hajiya kam jiki yayi tsanani, anata yi mata magani amman da ta farajin sauki sai wani ciwon ya bullo. Hajiya duk lokacin da take cikin sauki bata da zance sai na tambayar likita yaushe Aminu zai zo? Likitan ya sanar da ita cewa shima baisani ba. Shi kuwa Aminu yau tsab ya shirya tana dawowa daga makaranta yace da ibty ma ta shirya zasuje unguwa.
 Murna ta cikata dan tasan gida zai kaita, bayan ta fito daga wanka tsayawa tayi gaban wardrobe yatsanta a cikin bakinta sai ciza takeyi, tunanin kayan da ya dace tasa takeyi, amman duk wanda ta kalla sai taji be dace da yau din ba. Tafi minti biyar kafin idanunta ya tsaya kan wata doguwar riga ta atamfa golding brown an jera stones sosai a jiki, Ibty ta janyo ahankali ta warware tasa.
Murmushi tasaki ganin tayi kyau sannan ta daura dankwalin tabi jikinta da turaruka. Tana cikin saka lipstick da wayarta cikin handbag Aminu ya shigo, ta dago tana kallansa sukai ido hudu. Wani irin yanayi Aminu ya samu kanshi, zuciyar shi ta shiga cikin wani irin shauki. Yana mamakin yadda a hankali yar yarinyar nan take sake mallakar duk wani sashe dake jikinsa, yana mamakin yadda karamar yarinya kamar ibty take neman sarrafa duk wani tunani da yake dashi.

"Wai kin dauka lunch zamuje wani gurin?" Ya fada yana dan matso da ita kusa dashi. Ibty ta zaro ido.
"Ina zamuje to? Nazata gida zaka kaini mu gaisa dasu Umma?" Aminu ya bude ido.
"Gida kuma? Ba gida zamu ba unguwa zaki rakani."
"Daga unguwar fa sai ina?" Tayi maganar tana shagwabe fuska. Murmushi yayi tare da sumbatar saman lebenta yace.
"Sai mu dawo nan gidan."
"Kai Yaya."
"Dagaske nake miki." Ya saketa yana juyawa.
"Idan kin gama ki taho ina mota."
Ibty ta bubbuga kafa a kasa irin na yan yara, yana jin ta amma yaki waigawa sai dariya da yake yi har ya bar dakin. Mayafinta ta dauka tasa takalmi tabi bayansa. Tunda ya hangota tana tahowa ya kasa dauke ido daga kanta har sai da ta iso wajan, yasa hannu ya bude mata kofar ta shiga ta rufe sannan yaja suka fice. Tai maganar cikin wata murya wacce tasashi kunshe dariyarsa.
Aminu yaci gaba da tuki jefi jefi ya kan kalleta a haka har suka karasa asibiti. Ya samu guri ya ajiye motar suka kalli juna yayi murmushi, itakam kallan asibitin take tana tunani waye ba lafiya?
"Fito muje." Babu musu ta fito ya rufe suka jera. Tun daga bakin dakin din suke jiyo ihun Hajiya, hakan yasa Aminu saurin murda kofar yaga nurses suna ta kokarin riketa gashi tayi kaca-kaca da gurin da kashi da fitsari. Su kansu a dole suke ajiye da ita a asibitin kowa ya tsani zamanta, gashi da alama kudi ake bawa mai asibitin shiyasa shi ko haushin abin basuga yanaji ba.

Dakyar ta daure tace "Ummy!"
Jin muryarta yasa tace "Lafiya?"
Bakinta na rawa tace "Hajiya ba lafiya, ciwon ba dadi......"

Babu bata lokaci kuwa sai ga Ummy da Aina, mamaki ya kama Aminu ya kalli Ibty wacce ke gefe tare da Ainah wacce ta rike hannunta ganin yanda yanayinta duk ya canza.

 "A'a Ummy."
 "Kai nake sauraro, kar kuma kayi min karya kasan dai matsayina a wajanka. Sannan nan asibiti ne zan iya neman likitan ya sanar dani."

Sai da Ummy ta gama tabbatar da cewa sun tafi sannan ta tasa keyar su Aminu suka tafi. Ibty kam gabaki daya ta dinga baya-baya da Aminu dan tasan tabbas haushinta yakeji, da ya sauko amma abinda Ummy tai ta tabbatar ya kara hawa.

A can kauye kuwa ana kai Hajiya gidan sukace fir bazasu amsheta ba, wai mayya ce da alama kuruwar wani taci aka kamata, sukam masu bus suka ajiyeta a waje sukai tafiyarsu.
Kan kace me? An cika taf a kofar gidan ana kallanta, Kawu wato yayanta ne ya dawo daga gona cikin sauri saboda wayar da Aminu yamai sannan ya turo kudi masu yawa akan a kula da ita.

"Allah ya yafe mana baki daya. Amman ya kamata ki dinga shan maganin saboda wahalar ciwo ake gudu bawai mutuwa ba."

Daurewa yai a hankali ya dago fuskarta, idanunta a lumshe taji yayi mata wata kyakkyawar runguma a faffadan kirjinsa. Sake dagota yai yana kallanta kafin ya maida fuskarsa saman kirjinta, luf tai, tanajinsa ya daga ta ya kawota kan gado kafin a hankali ya sa fuskarsa kan hancinsa, nan ya karaso ya kai bakinsa cikin nata…�?

A can gidansu Umar kuwa basu dawo ba sai magrib lokacin har Ibty ta tafi, haushi duk ya kamashi dan ko kwakwaren kallo bai mata ba.

Ya tambaya yana lankwashe kafa, bude kular yai, fried rice tai da soyayyen naman kaza, dadi ya kamashi, tabbas ko saboda wannan dole zai lalaba nakashashiyarnan, gashi daga gidansu ake kawo kayan abinci ba ruwanshi dayin cefene sai dai yaci ya koshi, wani sa’in ma daga gidansu akeyin abincin.
Nan fa ya cika katan plate ya juye duka naman, ya hau ci.
Wannan abin ne haryanzu bai mata dadi yanda in yazo baya tambayarta ko taci ko bataci ba kawai juye naka yake ya cinye. Sai dai gani take zai daina daga baya.
Sai daya cinye tas sannan ya nufi daki ita kuma ta gyara gun, sallar isha’i yai dan yanda ya koshi ko masallaci bayajin zai iya zuwa, danewa kan gadan yai ya bubude kafa yana tuno yanda yaga su Ibty sun wani rike hannu.
Kwafa yai ya runtse ido jin alamun hannu yai akan nashi hannun, ya dauka mafarki yake, yadanyi murmushi tare da rike hannun, lalai yanasan Ibty wato ma mafarkinta yake shima ya rike hannun da akamai gori dazu.

Duk da zuciyarta ba haka taso ba, ba zata iya bude masa ba saboda kamar yadda Umma ta fada ne Umar bazai ga darajarta ba in har bata nuna masa kuskurensa ba.
Yadda yake mata magana kamar zai yi kuka shine yasa ta toshe kunnanta tana rintsa ido cike da zafin rai, ta jawo pillow din dake kan gado ta rufe kunnenta dashi da karfi tana zubarda kwalla, yanzu kenan laifinta ne yai sanadiyar zubar cikin ta, dan da ta kira Umma tun farko bata tsaya jiran Umar ba kila da bai zube ba.
Tana juyo shi daga waje yana cewa “Billyna zoki bude min dan Allah, wallahi bansan kin kirani ba dan Allah ki bude min yake abar kaunata. Kinfi kowa sannin yanzu zuciyata a mamaye take da kaunarki, dan Allah Billyna.�?
Gaba daya ta rasa abin yi a hankali Bilkisu ta taho wajan kofar saboda zuciyarta tana neman fin karfinta, har ta kama handled din da nufin budewa sai taji kiran wayarta, haka ta gungura kekenta taje ta dauka, ganin Umma ce yasa tai saurin dagawa inda tace.
“Kada ki sake ki budemai kofar nan kinji na fada miki, idan ba haka ba zai daukeki babbar banza kuma sakarai, ki bari ki karbi yancinki wannan fadan naki ne ba nawa bane ba kuma na Abbanki bane. Da alama tun farko dan ina shiga fadanki ne yasa kika saki jiki bakya nemawa kanki ‘yanci da kanki.�?
Hawayenta ta share tace “Umma yana ta hadani da Allah na rasa ya zanyi." Tai maganar cikin kara sakin wani kukan.
Umma cikin dan kakkausa murya dan ta shiga hankalinta tace “Nadai fada miki Karki kuskura ki bude, ke ko haushin abinda ya samu jaririnki bai isa ki nunamai kuskurensa ba? Allah yaga zuciyarki yaga tashi, dan haka ki dena ma sauraransa."
Bilkisu ta jijiga kai sannan ta ajiye wayar tana dora kai saman cinyarta.
Ganin taki budewa ya tabbatar masa da cewa itama taji haushin abinda ya faru, hakan kuma ya sake tabbatar masa da cewa Bilkisu zata iya rabuwa dashi. Ya matsa kusa da kofar yana mata magana cikin magiya
"Shikenan Sahibata tunda ba zaki bude min ba  zanje kan titi na kwanta har katuwar mota tabi ta kaina tunda kema kin tsaneni yanzu." Umar na gama fada mata haka yabar wajan jiki babu kwari.
Da sauri Bilkisu ta nufo kofa ta bude sai dai bata ganshi ba alamar yabar wajan. Wani ihu ta saki wanda yasa su Umma fitowa da gudu suna tambayarta.
"Lafiyar ki Bilkisu? Menene haka?"
"Umma! Na shiga uku Umar zai kashe kanshi, dan Allah Umma kubarshi yai min duk abinda yaga dama, wayyo Allah Umma wallahi ina sanshi."
Tsabar takaici yasa Umma kasa yi mata magana, ji take kamar ta shaketa saboda haushi. Harara kawai ta zabga mata tare da wucewa ta koma daki tana ta masifa. Iman ma tsayawa tai tana kallan Bilkisu wacce ta gunguro kekenta ta rike hannun Iman cikin kuka tace “Iman ku tausaya min, ku gane cewa Allah yayi Umar a haka ne, koda Ibty ya aura halinsa ne a haka, dan Allah ki bishi kice ya dawo." Saboda ta kyalesu yasa Iman cewa.
"Bari naje." Ta fita can bayan wasu lokuta sai gata ta dawo inda ta samu Bilkisu a falo tace.
"Na sameshi a bakin gate yace shikenan amman ki bari Umma ta huce sai a sasanta." Bilkisu ta sauke wata nannauyiyar ajiyar zuciya tana lumshe ido.

Umar kam yana waje yana safa da marwa yana kallan wayarsa tare da leken ko zaiga wani yazo bashi hakuri.
Ganin Iman yasa yai kwafa tare da yin wani murmushi yace “Ai nasan bazata iya rayuwa bani ba.�?
Yana kokarin bude kofa cikin kasaita da isa kawai yaga Iman tayi shawaginta ta koma ciki.
Mamaki ne ya rufeshi kamar ya kirata sai kuma ya tsaya yana kallan abinda take ko komawa tai ta dawo?haka ya karashi zamansa ya koma ciki, kitchen ya shiga ya dinga bude drawers yana neman abin ci, wannan wani irin iskanci ne? Ace ko abinci babu dafafe?
 Bai iya ko kunna gas ba sai abokinsa ya kira a video ya koyamai, haka yai haukar dafa farar taliya ya zuba mai da yaji ya zauna yana ci yanda kasan ya samu nama, wannan wani irin masifa ne? Yanasan fita yaje ya sai abu amma yana tsoron karya kashe dan kudin hannunsa a zo amai korar kare. Ai dai ance abinci ya kare ko? Gobe dai dole a kawomai in kuma baso ake a kasheshi ba.

Washe gari har wajan karfe goma yaga ba'a kawo mishi abinci ba, ga yunwar ji yake kamar zai mutu, yaso dafa wata taliyar amma babu, ita kenan ya samu ‘yar kadan jiya, yana mamakin dama Bilkisu kullum w gidansu take amso abin dafawa kenan ko me? Rai a bace, ya tashi ya fita zuwa sashen su Umma. Yayi sa'a Abba ya dawo da alama jiya da daddare ya dawo, cikin jin dadi ya kalleshi ganin yana karin kumallo, sum-sum ya shiga ya duka yana gaishe, Abba bai tanka mai ba karahe ma yi yai kamar baisan da zaman sa a gun ba.

Yadan sassauta murya kamar mutumin arziki, yace “Abba kaima fushi kake dani?" Abba ya ajiye kofin shayin hannunsa.
Kamar bazai amsa mai ba sai kuma yace “Ba dai ni kake yiwa wannan maganar ba ko?�?

Umar yai kalar tausayi yace “Abba ina san Bilkisu na rantse da sarkin dake busa numfashi ina santa, duk abubuwan da suka faru tsautsayi ne, bada sanina bane wlh Abba inaji a jikina asiri akamin, sam bansan sanda nake aikata wasu abubuwan ba.�?
Ya dan nisa cikin sanyin murya yace “Idan har kuka raba auran nan Abba rayuka ne zasu baci."
"Anzo gurin, daidai wajan ma kuwa, Umar dan Allah wane rai ne baka gama batawa ba a cikin gidan nan? Ashe akwai ragowa ban sani ba?" Umar yai saurin cewa “Ba haka nake nufi ba Abba, wallahi baka fahimceni bane."
"Tashi kabani guri, ba ance Bilkisun tace ta hakura da auran ba? Inaga ai an hutar da kowa  ko? Allah ya hada kowa da nashi rabon."
Lallai mutanan nan sun shirya raba su da Bilkisun shi, bema san yanacin arzikinta ba sai yanzu da yaga tabbas za'a rabasu. Ya zauna ya dinga bawa Abba hakuri har Umma tazo wajan, ya hada da ita yana ta magiya kamar makahon da ya rasa hanyar gida. Umma na shirin magana ta hango Bilkisu a can gefe tana hawaye, hakan yasa ta hadiyar yawu mai daci, babu yadda zatai dole ta hakura kafin Bilkisu tawatsa musu kasa a cikin ido.
  Umma ta daure cikin halin ko in kula tace. "Ni babban bakin cikina yadda kake mata kamar ka samu dabba, Umar ka manta wacece Bilkisu a wajan mu? Bilkisu ‘yarmu ce ta cikin mu, ƴar uwa a gurin IBTISAM idan har kana da mutunci zaka lallabata fiye da yadda zamuyi, in har da gaske san Ibtisam din kake ai bazakaso abinda zai illata yayarta ba ko?�?
Yai shiru yana sauraren Umma, jikinsa duk yadanyi sanyi.�?
Umma ta kalli Abba sannan ta kalli Bilkisu tace “Zan sake baka Bilkisu a karo na karshe, idan har ka kuskura tayi kuka ko ranta ya baci kasan da cewar sai ka bar gidan nan koda kuwa shine karshen rayuwar Bilkisu." Tana kaiwa nan taja tsaki tare da barin wajan.
Abba yai masa nasiha sosai sannan yace zai bashi jari yai sana'a ya rike Bilkisu da kuma kanshi saboda halin rayuwa karya saba ana bashi randa Abban ya mutu yazo su rasa yadda zasu yi.
Farin ciki ya lullubeshi tare da daukar alkawari zai riketa kamar kowane miji a gurin matarsa. 
Yana kokarin mikewa sukaji muryar Bilkisu tace “Abba ko ka bashi ni bazan koma ba, takardata nake jira.�?
Gaba daya mamaki ne ya rufesu suka kalleta cikin mamakin abinda ta fada, tabbas tana san Umar kamar ranta sai dai gani yanda iyayenta ke lalabashi suna kashemai kudi saboda ita, kuma duk da hakan babu alamun so a tattare da shi yasa taji ko zata mutu yanzu kam zata hakura dashi, ta tabbatar iyayenta basa farinciki da wannan auran, gashi a dalilinta ta rasa ‘dan cikinta.
Cikin mamaki Abba ya kira Umma wacce ke zaune a bakin gado rai a matukar bace.
Fitowa tai cikin kunar rai, sai dai jin kalaman Abba yasa gaba daya ta tattare hankalinta kan Bilkisy.
Cikin dauriya ta kalli Umma sannan ta kalli Umar tace “Ya Umar dan Allah ka bada takardata kafin ka fita, in kuma kana bukatar biro da paper ne yanzu Iman sai ta kawo maka.�?
Ta maida kallanta kan Abba tace “Abba dan Allah in ya rubuta aba Iman ta kawomin.�?
Bata jira mai zasu ce ba ta shiga daki ta kulle, kuka kawai tasa tana dukan kirjinta ita kadai tasan irin masifar san da takewa Umar a rayuwarta.
Gaba daya falan yayi tsir kowa mamakin abinda ya faru yake, Umar a firgice ya sa gwiwowinsa a kasa ya hade hannaye yace “Abba wallahi Allah na tuba dan Allah ku yafemin.�?
Mikewa Abba yai ya nufi daki, Umma tabi bayanshi.
Hankalin Umar yayi mugun tashi tabbas yanzu abin ba wasa bane, ya shiga uku? Ya zaiyi?
Duk yanda yai magiya Bilkisu taki yarda, gashi Abba ya kule bangarensu, dakyar Abba ya yarda ya koma dakinshi nada, haka kullum zaizo yana bada hakuri amma Bilkisu ta dage tace sun gama zaman aure, ita kanta duk ta canza ta rame amma ta kafe tace bataso.

IBTISAM kuwa a nasu bangaren cikin yazo mata da laulayi sosai, Aminu kuma ya sake zurmawa cikin shafin kaunarta, gaba daya ya kasa sukuni saboda rashin lafiyarta, in ya tafi aiki yana kira sama da sau biyar ya dinga jin ya jikinta da sauransu.
Ibty gaba daya ba ta iya cin abincin da ta dafa sai na gurin Ummi shima daga danwake sai tuwo da kunun gyada. Kullum driver yana hanyar gidan Aminu kai abinci, idan sukaji shiru kuma sai Aminu yaje da kanshi ya amso mata in yana gida, ita kuma takan jure wajan yiwa Aminu da su Islam girki amman ita ba zataci ba.
 Yau aka samu matsala Ummy sun tafi gaisuwar wata yar aminiyarta ta rasu, ibty na gida kamar zataci babu saboda yunwa. Aminu ya shiga dakinta ya tarar da ita sai juyi take akan gado, cikin sauri ya isa wajanta ya dagota zuwa jikinsa.
"Habibty ya akai? Me yake damunki?" Hannu ta saka ta riko shi tasa idanunta cikin nashi kamar zata fashe tace “Ummy bata gida, gashi yunwa nakeji wallahi kamar zan mutu haka nakeji." Gyara zamansa yai tare da kwantar da ita saman kirjinsa, bayanta da gashin kanta ya shiga shafawa cikin lallashi yace “To ko ni zan zake dantse na daure na dafa miki ko indomie ce kici? da kaina ma zan dafa miki, am sure zakiji dadinta."
Kallan mamaki tamai tace “Kai din?�?
Ya dan hadiyi yawon bakinsa shi kansa fada kawai yai dan bai san ma ya ake ba, kawai dan zolayarnan ne.
Mamaki ne ya kamashi dayaji tace “Banacin indomie, taliya ma bana so sai dai kaimin danwake." Aminu ya zaro ido yana kallanta yace “Danwake?�?
Tadan shagwabe tana daga kai, yanzu ne lokacin da zaki dan moreshi ko ruwan tea tunda akai aurensu bataga ya dafa ba, Aminu fa an shiga tunani. Yanzu ina yasan yadda ake danwake? Abu daya ya sani ana saka kuka itama besan a wane lokaci ake amfani da ita ba. Ibty da taji yayi shiru sai ta juyo ta kalleshi, ya sakar mata murmushi kamar gaske, cikin wasa kai yace.
"Wanda zan miki sai yafi na Ummy dadi, zaki sha mamakin dadinsa, kwanta ina zuwa." Ya kwantar da ita shi kuma ya fice daga dakin. Yana shiga dakinsa ya sauke numfashi yana furzar da isa daga bakinsa. Kodai ya kira Aina ne ya sata ta dafa sai ya amso ya kai mata? Amman kuma idan ya kirata shi kuma cikin yace baya so dole fa shi din ne zai yi ko? To idan shima yayi cikin yace baya so fa?
"Kai ya subahanallahi." Ya fada yana dafa bango. Wayarsa ya janyo ya kunna data, YouTube ya shiga ya nemo yadda ake danwaken, cikin sauri ya tashi ya nufi kitchen din duk abinda yaga an fada a hadin sai da ya dakko a ajiye a gefe, wanda be gani ba zaije ya tambayota inda ta ajiye, Allah-Allah yake ya gama tun kafin yara suzo su ganshi dan baisan me zai ce musu ba.

Ta YouTube video Aminu ya kalla yai mata danwaken, shi kansa sai da ya burge kanshi ganin yadda komai ya kammala, sai dai fa ya bata kwanuka sunfi nawa gaba daya kitchen din yayi kacha kacha ko ina flour da kuka.
Ya zubo a cikin dan bowl ya dakko tire ya dora yaji da mai da ruwan sha ya dauka ya nufi dakin. Lalai Aminu wai mantawa kake da shekarunka? Abinda zuciyarsa tace kenan, ya dan yi murmushi in banyi ba yanzu sai na tsufa zanyi? Da wannan ya shiga dakin, ya tarar da ita ta fito daga wanka saboda tunda cikin nan ya sameta buni-buni tayi wanka ta zama tamkar wata kifi. Sai da ya ajiye tire din a kan gado sannan ya karasa ya taho da ita ta zauna.
Kallan bowl din tayi mamaki ya bayyana saman fuskarta dan ta dauka ma wata hidimar ya shiga ko kuma kila fita zaiyi ya siyo mata ya kawo mata, daga karshe ta yanke tunani akan lalai Ainah zai turawa tayi yaje ya dauko, dan Ummy tace zatasa a yi amma ita Ibtyn tace a barshi tanada ragowar, sam batasan daurawa su Ummy wahalar nan.
Baki ta saki tana ta kalleshi kafin ta kuma kallan danwaken ta janyo shi gabanta tasa yaji da mai tana cewa.
"Wai dagaske Habiby kaine kayi wannan?�?
Hanaye yasa a aljihunsa ya dan zauna tare da harde kafa yace “Ce miki akai shekarun danai a banza nai?�?
Ta sake kallanshi cike da mamaki, ya dan daure fuska yace “Wai ke kin dauka ban iya ba?" Ya fada cike da kuri.
"Wallahi nasha mamaki, Ummy fa tace bata taba ganin ka soya koda kwai ba bare abinci. A gida in an ganka a kitchen to tea ka shiga hadawa.�?
"Af yo komai nawa ta sani ne an gaya miki? Kinga yanzu sai kije ki basu labarina." Tana motsawa tace dashi “A’a fa karfa ka cika baki, ka bari baki ne zai bada amsar girkin naka ba wai ido ba." Ta diba shi kuma yai saurin rintsa ido yana shafa tafukan hannayensa kamar mai shafa mai. In baiyi dadi ba ai ya bada kanshi.
Shiru beji tace komai ba hakan yasa shi kuma rintsa ido yasan an samu matsala.
Ya juyo cikin dakewa da rashin san nuna abinda ke ransa yace “ya akai?" Ya tambayeta yana bin yanayinta da kallo.
"Me aka yi?" Ta fada tana kallanshi tana kokarin boye abinda ke ranta.
Ido ya kankance yana mata wani kallo, fork tasa ta sake dauka takai bakinta, bata ko sake magana ba sai dataci spoon hudu, ai Aminu besan lokacin da ya saki murmushi dan ya tabbata komai yayi.
"Habiby gaskiya ka iya wallahi, ni daga yanzu ma kai zaka dinga yi min yafi na Ummy dadi."
"Ke! Me zan dinga yi?" Kallanshi tayi cike da marairaicewa tace masa.
"Danwaken." Aminu yace.
"Tab kin maidani kuku? Hajiya sorry bazanyi karya ba dan bazan iya ba.�?
"Allah ba ruwana babyn ciki na ne yake so bani ba.�?
Suna ‘yar hirar nan ta cinye tas da yake waya yake yi sai ta tashi ta kai kayan kitchen anan ta tarar da aika-aikar da yayi. 
Goshinta ta dafe lalai taci dan wake da wahala, dan dacin wannan dan waken da wannan uban aiki ai gwara ba’aci ba.
Nan ta shiga tattare kwanukan da aka bata sannan ta shiga wanke-wanke, sai datai wajen rabi ya shigo tare da kamo kugunta ya rike sannan ya kwantar da kansa a kafadarta yace “yaushe aka tara kwanuka haka? Dazu fa da na shigo babu kwanukan wanke-wanke.�?
Kallansa tadanyi kadan tace “Dan wake ne ya tara mana.�?
Baki yadan bude yace “Wai nine na tara kenan?�?
Murmushi tadanyi “Madam karfa kice in tayaki dan bansan ya akeyi ba. Nadaiyi sanda ina karatu a kasar waje amma ban iya ba.�?
Ta dan yi kasa dakai tanayi yana tsaye a jikinta suna ‘yar hira kafin daga baya ya tayata kifa kwanuka har suka gama.
Yadan kalleta yace “Sai muje yanzu kuma a bani ladan aiki." Ya fada yana jan hannunta ya nufi dakinsa.
Suna shiga daki tana dariya tace “Nawa ne kudin?" Ta cigaba da dariya kasa kasa.
"A'a wallahi ni bana kudi nai miki ba, zuwa zaki nan gurin." Ya nuna kusa dashi yana gyara mata filo.
"Yaya an kusa kiran sallah fa." Ya shafa kai saboda gabaki daya yasawa ransa dan haka ya mike ya kamota.
"Dama ance ‘yan yaran nan sufi jan aji gurin shimfida.�?
Suka saki dariya tare da hawa kan gadon. Daga nan fa suka ci gaba da farantawa juna rai, Aminu gaba daya yayi laushi a hannun ‘yar yarinyar da ya raina, a koda yaushe in suna tare har mamakin kansa yake, sai dai fa in suna cikin mutane yana nuna fa shi namiji ne babba, ita abin ma dariya yake bata wani sa’in in gaga ya daure a gaban mutane.
Lalai ba shakka su Hajiya ne suka juyar mai da hankali dan mutum ne mai saukin kai da yiwa mutum uzuri in yai laifi.

***********

Dawowarta daga makaranta kenan ta baje a parlor ta kwanta tana ta bacci, gajiyar da tayi ne ya hanata ko cire hijabi bare ta nemi abinci. Cikin bacci taji tamkar ana sallama amman ta kasa tashi, duk a tunaninta ma mafarki takeyi.
"Ibtisam." Umma ta kira sunanta tana bugun kafarta. Ibty ta bude ido a hankali, wa zata gaji? Ai da sauri ta tashi ta rungume ta tana murna.
"Umma sannunku da zuwa, Yaya Bilkisu hardake?"
Umma ta girgiza kai tace “Yanzu in barawo ne ya shigo har sata sai yai kina baje kina bacci.�?
Sai a lokacin ta dan rufe baki tace “A bude nabar kofar?�?
Umma ta harareta hakan yasata yin murmushi tace “Zan kula nan gaba.�?
Bilkisu tadan tura kekenta bayan ta gama dan zagaya falan dan yau ne zuwanta na farko tace “Ke kuma a haka kike sha'awar kwanciya? Kinga a yadda muka tarar dake kuwa? Ke a takure babynmu a takure?�?
Kai ta sunkuyar dakai cike da jin kunyar Umma yanzu fa kowa yasan mai tai ta samu cikin ko?
Bata san ana magana akan cikin nan saboda kunya takeji sosai.
"Nima dawowata daga makaranta kenan na kwanta sai bacci, bari na kawo muku ruwa."
"A'a bashi wucewa zamuyi Iman ta dage sai mun biyo." Cewar Umma tana sake kallan Ibty.
"Ina kukaje?"
"Asibiti." Iman ta fada tana shiga kitchen.
"Waye ba lafiya Umma?"
"Ba kowa Bilkisu muka kai, tunda tai barin nan take ciwon mara shine mukaje saboda ina tunanin ko be gama ficewa ba, munje kuma an duba lafiya lau."
Suka hada ido da Bilkisu, nan da nan na mata sannu. Hira suka taba dan Umma yanzu tana ta kokarin jan Ibty ta fahimci lalai duk abinda ya faru itace sila da ta ja Ibty a jiki to fa da duk hakan bata faru ba.
(Iyaye dan Allah mu dinga kokarin jan ‘ya’yan mu a jiki especially ‘ya’ya mata dan sai kin jasu a jiki ne zasu iya sanar dake matsalarsu.)
Bayan sun gama hira ne su Umma suka tashi suka tafi ita kuma taje tai wanka. 
Assignment din da aka basu tai shiga yin browsing tana yi Aminu ya dawo, ya zauna sukayi a tare ta rakashi dakinsa yai wanka sannan suka fito falo.

******

A bangaren Umar kuwa gaba daya yanzu Allah ya jarabceshi da mugun san Bilkisu, ga sha’awa dake damunsa kullum in zai kwanta, tabbas yaji dadin saduwa da ita gashi yanzu duk yanda ya bata hakuri ta kafe ta tsare tace a’a.
Gaba daya ba wanda yake kulashi yanzu a gidan, abinci wannan a bakin kofarsa Iman ke ajiyemai tai tafiyarta, Abba kuma ya hanashi shiga cikin gidanshi.
Rayuwa tamai zafi yanzu har letters yake rubutawa Bilkisu ya sa a kwanon dayaci abinci, text kuwa kullum sai ya mata amma ba reply, gaba daya ya canza ya maida hankalinsa gun aiki dan ba wanda ke kulashi, kauye ma Kawunsa yace in har zaizo ta ya tabbatar ya daidaita da matarsa, ita kanta Goggo taki kulashi.
Wannan abu yamai yawa abokan ma yanzu duk sun daina harka dashi.
 Watansu biyar da rabuwa zazzabi ya kamashi cikin dare, ya turawa Bilkisu sako tare da sanar da ita halin da yake ciki, kamar bazata kula ba sai kuma ta tuna halin data shiga a dalilin kin zuwan dayai, haka ta fito va tare da sanin kowa ba ta nufi bangarensa, a hankali tadan kwankwasa yana daga ciki ya mike dakyar dan ko abincin rana baici ba, yana tafe yana fatan Allah yasa itace, a hankali ya bude kofar nan suka hada ido, fuska ta daure tana neman juyawa, da sauri ya rikota yana cewa “Billy ki yafemin dan Allah!�?
Tsayawa tai bata juyo ba, hannu yasa a kan kafadarta zafin dataji ne yasa tadan juyo, bakinta nadan rawa tace “Da gaske bazaka rubuta takarda ta ba?�?
Ya girgiza kai yace “Ko a haka zamu dawamma bazan sakeki ba, ki tuna ni mijinki ne, nayi iya kokarina na ganin kin hakura, to amma tunda kinki zan umarceki a matsayina na mijinki ki koma dakinki gobe.�?
Yanayinta ne ya canza ya dawo gabanta ya tsuguna yace “Billy please…�?.�?
Tausayinshi ne ya kamata ta rike hannunsa tace “Kasha magani?�?
Ai bai jira komai ba ya rungumeta yace “Dan Allah ki dawo, wallahi Allah bazan sake wulakantaki ba in har na kuma duk hukuncin da kika ga dama ki dauka.�?
Nan suka daidaita washegari ta sanar dasu Abba ta koma dakinta.
Umar gaba daya ya canza, tunsanda yaji Ibtisam nada ciki ya tsani kanshi, ita gata can tana rainon cikinta amma shi dan asara ya jawo sanadiyar zubar da cikin datai.
Wannan dalili yasa ya kara kaimi gun kula da Bilkisu yana kuma fatan Allah ya basu wani cikin.
Hankalinsu Abba yanzu ya kwanta dan Umar ya canza banda zafin rai wanda haryanzu yake dashi.

*****
Cikin Ibty yakai wata tara haihuwa yau ko gobe, ta ajiye karatunta a level 3 akan in ta haihu zata dawo ta cigaba, Aminu da kanshi ya nemi alfarma aka kawo Iman gidan ta zauna da ita, sannan duk weekend Ainah na zuwa tai kwana biyu sai ta koma.
Kulawa ta ko ina tana samu sau da dama takan yi kuka sai taga anya ta cancanci wannan farincikin kuwa? Ilham da Islam na tsananin santa ita ma tana iya kacin kokarin ta, har addu’a take a ranta akan kada Allah ya bata damar nuna banbanci tsakaninsu da ‘ya’yan da zata haifa nan gaba.
Alhamdulila nakuda ta zo mata suka nufi asibiti inda ta haifi danta namiji, Aminu ya sa mashi sunan Abdullahi dan yana san sunan.

Aminu bai kara waiwayar zuri’ar Hajiya ba bai kuma sake bi takansu ba, mahaifin Na’imah dai suna gaisawa wani sa’in har gida yake zuwa ya gaisheshi banda shi bai bi takan kowa a cikinsu, a koda yaushe yana godewa Allah daya kawo Ibtisam cikin rayuwarsa, yanzu hankalinsa a kwance yake sai dan abinda ba’a rasa ba wanda dukanmu dani da ke mai karantawa munsan rayuwar aure dole in ana tare wani sa’in za’a samu matsala�?..

Allah ya kara daidaita rayuwar auranmu da mazajenmu, wanda basuyi ba kuma Allah ya kawo miji na gari.
